# DNA Chain Ledger Functions

The version-3 (cometbft) chain's tagged state-root hierarchy and the
witness-side functions that read/write the committed ledger state it
covers. `shared/dnac/ledger_roots_v2.c` is compiled into BOTH `libdna.so`
(messenger build) and `libnodus` (nodus build); the `nodus_witness_*`
entries live in `nodus/src/witness/` and are nodus-only.

Contract of every tag and preimage: `shared/dnac/ledger_roots_v2.h`
(TAG TABLE + "Composition preimages"). Entries marked **W-A** were
added or changed by the final pre-testnet wipe, package W-A (keyless
treasury pools — decision
`docs/plans/decisions/2026-09-28-treasury-pools-and-exact-self-stake.md`).

---

## 1. Shared root hashing (`dnac/ledger_roots_v2.h`)

| Function | Description |
|----------|-------------|
| `int dna_v2_empty_root(dna_v2_empty_kind_t kind, uint8_t out[64])` | SHA3-512 of the kind's 16-byte tag alone. **W-A:** new kind `DNA_V2_EMPTY_TREASURY` (`"NDS.E.TREAS.v1"`), appended after `DNA_V2_EMPTY_ACCRUAL`. |
| `int dna_v2_treasury_leaf_hash(uint32_t pool_id, uint64_t balance, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (W-A).** `SHA3-512("NDS.TRLEAF.v1" 16 B ‖ pool_id u32 BE ‖ balance u64 BE)`. 0 / -1. |
| `int dna_v2_treasury_root(const uint32_t *pool_ids, const uint64_t *balances, size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (W-A).** Tagged Merkle over treasury leaves, pool_id STRICTLY ascending (duplicate / descending → -1), inner `"NDS.TRNODE.v1"`, odd node promoted, n==1 → the leaf, n==0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_TREASURY`. |
| `int dna_v2_system_root(const uint8_t validator_root[64], const uint8_t delegation_root[64], const uint8_t chain_config_root[64], const uint8_t validator_set_root[64], const uint8_t domain_registry_root[64], const uint8_t manifest_root[64], const uint8_t attendance_root[64], const uint8_t treasury_root[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **CHANGED (W-A):** 8 legs under `"NDS.SYS.v4"` — `treasury_root` appended as the LAST parameter/leg (was 7 legs, `"NDS.SYS.v3"`). |
| `int dna_v2_system_payload_root(const uint8_t validator_root[64], const uint8_t delegation_root[64], const uint8_t chain_config_root[64], const uint8_t validator_set_root[64], const uint8_t treasury_root[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **CHANGED (W-A):** 5 legs under `"NDS.SYSPAYL.v3"` — `treasury_root` appended LAST (was 4 legs, `"NDS.SYSPAYL.v2"`). |
| `int dna_v2_accrual_leaf_hash(const uint8_t owner_fp[64], uint64_t amount, uint8_t out[64])` | Reward-accrual leaf (`"NDS.ACLEAF.v1"`), unchanged. |
| `int dna_v2_accrual_root(const uint8_t (*owner_fps)[64], const uint64_t *amounts, size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | Reward-accrual root (`"NDS.ACNODE.v1"`), unchanged. |
| `int dna_v2_core_root(...)` | 7-leg CORE composition (`"NDS.CORE.v2"`), unchanged. |

### 1.1 Storage reward v1 — the storage leg (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-04-storage-reward-approved.md`; bytes `docs/plans/2026-10-04-storage-reward-bytes.md` items 1-6)

Pure functions, no database. Wired by packages B1 / B2a (§24; the archive-reward rows below are marked B2a — decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-05-archive-reward-bytes-approved.md`, bytes `docs/plans/2026-10-05-archive-reward-bytes.md` items 1-5 and §6), inert until the storage vote. Every tag is 16 bytes, zero-padded ASCII; integers big-endian. KATs come from independent oracles (`storage_reward_kat.json`, `archive_reward_kat.json`).

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_REG`, `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_SETS`, `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_REPORTS` (`dna_v2_empty_kind_t`) | **NEW kinds**, appended after `DNA_V2_EMPTY_TREASURY`: `dna_v2_empty_root` hashes `"NDS.E.STREG.v1"` / `"NDS.E.STSET.v1"` / `"NDS.E.STREP.v1"`. |
| `DNA_V2_STORAGE_SET_MAX` (256u), `DNA_V2_STORAGE_BITMAP_MAX` (32u) | **NEW.** Storage set cap (design rev 2.2 F3) and the longest report bitmap, ceil(256/8). |
| `DNA_V2_STORAGE_ACTIVE` (1), `DNA_V2_STORAGE_EXITING` (2), `DNA_V2_STORAGE_RELEASED` (3) | **NEW.** Registry status byte; 0 is invalid. |
| `DNA_V2_STORAGE_EXIT_KIND` (0x11), `DNA_V2_STORAGE_EXIT_OUT_IDX` (201) | **NEW.** The exit release UTXO's synthetic kind byte and output index (no other writer uses either). |
| `dna_v2_storage_node_row_t { node_fp[64]; payee_fp[64]; bond; status; registered_height; exit_height; fail_streak; grace_until; }` | **NEW.** One registry row; canonical order node_fp ASC. `uint32_t fail_streak` added by the archive reward (B2a); `uint64_t grace_until` added by K9 (§24.9). |
| `int dna_v2_storage_node_leaf_hash(const dna_v2_storage_node_row_t *row, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (leaf v2 since B2a).** `SHA3-512("NDS.STLEAF.v2" ‖ node_fp[64] ‖ payee_fp[64] ‖ bond u64 ‖ status u8 ‖ registered_height u64 ‖ exit_height u64 ‖ fail_streak u32 ‖ grace_until u64)` (181-byte preimage since K9 — grace_until appended last, the tag unchanged because the leaf was never activated; archive bytes item 4 — the v1 leaf was replaced before any activation). Any u64 grace_until is encoded as given. 0 / -1 (NULL, status outside 1..3). |
| `int dna_v2_storage_registry_root(const dna_v2_storage_node_row_t *rows, size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** Tagged Merkle over registry leaves, node_fp STRICTLY ascending (duplicate / descending → -1), inner `"NDS.STRNODE.v1"`, odd node promoted, n==1 → the leaf, n==0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_REG`. A row with an invalid status → -1. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_set_hash(uint64_t epoch_start, const uint8_t (*node_fps)[64], size_t count, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** S(H) = `SHA3-512("NDS.STSET.v1" ‖ epoch_start u64 ‖ count u32 ‖ node_fp[0..count−1])`, a plain hash. node_fps STRICTLY ascending; count 0 legal (node_fps may be NULL); count > 256 → -1. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_sets_leaf_hash(uint64_t epoch_start, const uint8_t set_hash[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.STSLEAF.v1" ‖ epoch_start u64 ‖ S(H)[64])`. 0 / -1. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_sets_root(const uint64_t *epoch_starts, const uint8_t (*set_hashes)[64], size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** Tagged Merkle over sets leaves, epoch_start STRICTLY ascending, inner `"NDS.STSNODE.v1"`, n==0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_SETS`. |
| `dna_v2_storage_report_t { epoch_start; seat; set_hash[64]; bitmap_len; bitmap[32]; }` | **NEW.** One committed report; canonical order (epoch_start, seat) ASC. Bytes past bitmap_len are not hashed. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_report_leaf_hash(const dna_v2_storage_report_t *rep, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.STREP.v1" ‖ epoch_start u64 ‖ seat u32 ‖ S(H)[64] ‖ bitmap_len u16 ‖ bitmap[bitmap_len])`. Takes the bytes as given — bitmap_len == ceil(count/8) and zero unused bits are the caller's checks. 0 / -1 (NULL, bitmap_len > 32). |
| `int dna_v2_storage_reports_root(const dna_v2_storage_report_t *reps, size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** Tagged Merkle over report leaves, (epoch_start, seat) STRICTLY ascending lexicographic (equal pair / descending → -1), inner `"NDS.STRPNODE.v1"`, n==0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_REPORTS`. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_root(const uint8_t registry_root[64], const uint8_t sets_root[64], const uint8_t reports_root[64], const uint8_t segments_root[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (4 legs since B2a — signature CHANGED).** `SHA3-512("NDS.STOR.v2" ‖ registry_root ‖ sets_root ‖ reports_root ‖ segments_root)` (archive bytes item 5; the 3-leg `"NDS.STOR.v1"` form was replaced before any activation). 0 / -1 (NULL leg). |
| `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_SEGS` (`dna_v2_empty_kind_t`) | **NEW (B2a)**, appended: `dna_v2_empty_root` hashes `"NDS.E.STSEG.v1"`. |
| `DNA_V2_SEGMENT_BLOCKS` (`24u * DNAC_EPOCH_LENGTH` = 17280 at the shipped E = 720), `DNA_V2_STORAGE_HOLDERS` (3u), `DNA_V2_STORAGE_FAIL_LIMIT` (3u), `DNA_V2_STORAGE_SAMPLES` (3u), `DNA_V2_STORAGE_NONCE_LEN` (32u) | **NEW (B2a).** Blocks per segment (a preimage constant: one payday period of 24 epochs; `ledger_roots_v2.h` now includes `dnac/dnac.h` for the epoch length, and `ledger_roots_v2.c` asserts 17280 at E = 720 — only the Genesis Protocol short-epoch build, E = 15, runs P = 360), R, the skip threshold, probe samples per target, the probe nonce length. |
| `int dna_v2_segment_root(uint64_t k, const uint8_t (*hashes)[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (B2a).** Root(k) = `SHA3-512("NDS.STSEG.v1" ‖ k u64 ‖ 17280 u32 ‖ hashes[0..17279])` — a plain hash over EXACTLY 17280 header hashes (heights (k−1)·17280+1 .. k·17280). -1 on NULL, k == 0, k·17280 > UINT64_MAX, allocation. |
| `int dna_v2_segment_leaf_hash(uint64_t k, const uint8_t root[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (B2a).** `SHA3-512("NDS.STSGLEAF.v1" ‖ k u64 ‖ root[64])`. |
| `int dna_v2_segments_root(const uint64_t *ks, const uint8_t (*roots)[64], size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (B2a).** Tagged Merkle over segment leaves, k STRICTLY ascending, inner `"NDS.STSGNODE.v1"`, n==0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_SEGS`. |
| `int dna_v2_segment_assign_key(const uint8_t root[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (B2a).** A(k) = `SHA3-512("NDS.STASGN.v1" ‖ root[64])`. |
| `int dna_v2_segment_holders(const uint8_t a_key[64], const uint8_t (*node_fps)[64], const uint32_t *fail_streaks, size_t n, size_t out_idx[3], size_t *n_out)` | **NEW (B2a).** holders(k): the ≤ 3 members with fail_streak < 3 of smallest `A XOR node_fp` (64-byte big-endian = memcmp order); `out_idx` in ascending distance; input order irrelevant; any duplicate fp → -1; n ≤ 256. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_sample_x(const uint8_t nonce[32], const uint8_t target_fp[64], uint32_t i, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (B2a, OFF-CHAIN).** x_i = `SHA3-512("NDS.STSAMP.v1" ‖ nonce ‖ target_fp ‖ i u32)` — the probe client's and the KAT's one implementation. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_sample_index(const uint8_t x[64], uint64_t eligible_blocks, uint32_t parts_total, uint64_t *block_index, uint32_t *part_index)` | **NEW (B2a, OFF-CHAIN).** block index = BE u64 x[0..8) mod B (a 0-based position in the target's eligible blocks, ascending height), part index = BE u32 x[8..12) mod parts_total; either out may be NULL; B == 0 or parts_total == 0 → -1. |
| `int dna_v2_system_root_v5(const uint8_t validator_root[64], const uint8_t delegation_root[64], const uint8_t chain_config_root[64], const uint8_t validator_set_root[64], const uint8_t domain_registry_root[64], const uint8_t manifest_root[64], const uint8_t attendance_root[64], const uint8_t treasury_root[64], const uint8_t storage_root[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** 9 legs under `"NDS.SYS.v5"`: the 8 `dna_v2_system_root` legs in the same order, `storage_root` last. `dna_v2_system_root` (`"NDS.SYS.v4"`) and `dna_v2_system_payload_root` (`"NDS.SYSPAYL.v3"`) are unchanged. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_exit_id(const uint8_t chain_id[32], uint64_t release_height, const uint8_t node_fp[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.STEXIT.v1" ‖ chain_id[32] ‖ DNA_DOMAIN_CORE u32 ‖ release_height u64 ‖ node_fp[64])`; node_fp = SHA3-512(node_pk) as given. 0 / -1. |
| `int dna_v2_storage_exit_nullifier(const uint8_t exit_id[64], uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** `SHA3-512(exit_id ‖ 0x11 ‖ u32be(201))` — the EPGRAD nullifier shape with its own kind and index. 0 / -1. |

### 1.2 HF-7 P1a — the §8.9 root functions (INERT, no caller; snapshot design `docs/plans/2026-09-11-nodus-ledger-snapshot-design.md` §8.9, APPROVED by the operator 2026-10-15; Kurultay #24 / #27)

Pure functions, no database, **no caller** — they become live only when the HF-7 wiring (package P1c) selects them by rule-set generation. Every existing v5 / v1 function above is unchanged, byte for byte. Every tag is 16 bytes, zero-padded ASCII; integers big-endian (§8.9 C1-C4). Return convention: 0 / -1 — -1 on NULL, any integer outside the item's STATED range (never truncated, §8.9 C3), a non-strictly-ascending key (equal or descending neighbour, never sorted silently, C5), allocation or digest failure. Merkle per C6 (odd node carried up unchanged, n == 1 → the leaf), empty set per C7. KAT: the independent oracle `nodus/tests/vectors/hf7_root_coverage_kat.json` (generator `hf7_root_coverage_oracle.py`; SELF-CONSISTENT), pinned by `nodus/tests/test_roots_hf7.c`.

| Item | §8.9 | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| `DNA_V2_EMPTY_BCOPY`, `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STMEM`, `DNA_V2_EMPTY_REPLAY` (`dna_v2_empty_kind_t`) | S1, S3, S7 | **NEW kinds**, appended after `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_SEGS`: `dna_v2_empty_root` hashes `"NDS.E.BCOPY.v1"` / `"NDS.E.STMEM.v1"` / `"NDS.E.REPLAY.v1"`. |
| `DNA_V2_HF7_U63_MAX` | C3 | **NEW.** 2^63 − 1, the upper bound of every "0..2^63−1" / "1..2^63−1" range. |
| `dna_v2_bcopy_row_t { uint64_t epoch_start; uint8_t validator_fp[64]; uint8_t owner_fp[64]; uint64_t kind; uint64_t amount; }` | S1 | **NEW.** One `v2_balance_copy` row. `kind` is taken as uint64_t (the SQLite INTEGER unnarrowed) and encoded as u8 after the range check. |
| `int dna_v2_bcopy_leaf_hash(const dna_v2_bcopy_row_t *row, uint8_t out[64])` | S1 | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.BCLEAF.v1" ‖ epoch_start u64 ‖ validator_fp[64] ‖ owner_fp[64] ‖ kind u8 ‖ amount u64)` (161-byte preimage). -1 if epoch_start > 2^63−1, kind ∉ {0, 1}, amount > 2^63−1. |
| `int dna_v2_bcopy_root(const dna_v2_bcopy_row_t *rows, size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | S1 | **NEW.** Tagged Merkle, rows STRICTLY ascending by (epoch_start, validator_fp, owner_fp, kind) — integers numerically, fingerprints memcmp, field by field; inner `"NDS.BCNODE.v1"`; n == 0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_BCOPY`. |
| `int dna_v2_vstat_hash(const uint64_t *active_count, uint8_t out[64])` | S2 | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.VSTAT.v1" ‖ active_count u64)`. The row is mandatory: NULL (absent) → -1; active_count > 2^63−1 → -1. No empty tag. |
| `dna_v2_stmem_row_t { uint64_t epoch_start; uint8_t node_fp[64]; uint64_t fail_streak; uint64_t grace_until; }` | S3 | **NEW.** One `v2_storage_set_members` row; `fail_streak` taken as uint64_t, encoded as u32 after the range check. |
| `int dna_v2_stmem_leaf_hash(const dna_v2_stmem_row_t *row, uint8_t out[64])` | S3 | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.SMLEAF.v1" ‖ epoch_start u64 ‖ node_fp[64] ‖ fail_streak u32 ‖ grace_until u64)` (100-byte preimage). -1 if epoch_start ∉ 1..2^63−1, fail_streak > 2^32−1, grace_until > 2^63−1. |
| `int dna_v2_stmem_root(const dna_v2_stmem_row_t *rows, size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | S3 | **NEW.** Tagged Merkle, rows STRICTLY ascending by (epoch_start, node_fp); inner `"NDS.SMNODE.v1"`; n == 0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STMEM`. |
| `int dna_v2_system_root_v6(const uint8_t validator_root[64], const uint8_t delegation_root[64], const uint8_t chain_config_root[64], const uint8_t validator_set_root[64], const uint8_t domain_registry_root[64], const uint8_t manifest_root[64], const uint8_t attendance_root[64], const uint8_t treasury_root[64], const uint8_t storage_root[64], const uint8_t bcopy_root[64], const uint8_t vstat[64], const uint8_t stmem_root[64], uint8_t out[64])` | S4 | **NEW.** 12 legs under `"NDS.SYS.v6"`: the nine `dna_v2_system_root_v5` legs in v5 order, then bcopy_root, vstat, stmem_root (16 + 12 × 64 bytes). Each leg opaque. -1 on a NULL leg. |
| `int dna_v2_segment_leaf_hash_v2(uint64_t k, const uint8_t root[64], uint64_t published_height, uint8_t out[64])` | S5 | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.STSGLEAF.v2" ‖ k u64 ‖ Root(k)[64] ‖ published_height u64)` (96-byte preimage). -1 if k or published_height ∉ 1..2^63−1. |
| `int dna_v2_segments_root_v2(const uint64_t *ks, const uint8_t (*roots)[64], const uint64_t *published_heights, size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | S5 | **NEW.** Tagged Merkle over v2 leaves, k STRICTLY ascending; inner `"NDS.STSGNODE.v1"` and n == 0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STORAGE_SEGS` UNCHANGED. |
| `int dna_v2_domain_head_hash_v2(const dna_v2_domain_head_t *head, const uint8_t last_upd_hash[64], uint8_t out[64])` | S6 | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.DOMHEAD.v2" ‖ dna_v2_domain_head_encode(head) [89 B] ‖ last_upd_hash[64])` (169-byte preimage). enc89 fields take their full type width. |
| `int dna_v2_domains_root_v2(const dna_v2_domain_head_t *heads, const uint8_t (*last_upd_hashes)[64], size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | S6 | **NEW.** domains_root over v2 head hashes, shape unchanged from `dna_v2_domains_root`: heads STRICTLY ascending by domain_id, `heads[0].domain_id == DNA_DOMAIN_SYSTEM`, n == 0 → -1 (no empty tag), inner `"NDS.DOMNODE.v1"`. `last_upd_hashes[i]` belongs to `heads[i]`. |
| `dna_v2_replay_row_t { uint8_t tx_id[64]; uint8_t intent_id[64]; uint64_t expiry_height; }` | S7 | **NEW.** One replay-relation row. |
| `int dna_v2_replay_leaf_hash(const dna_v2_replay_row_t *row, uint8_t out[64])` | S7 | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.RPLEAF.v1" ‖ tx_id[64] ‖ intent_id[64] ‖ expiry_height u64)` (152-byte preimage). -1 if expiry_height == 0 (range 1..2^64−1). |
| `int dna_v2_replay_root(const dna_v2_replay_row_t *rows, size_t n, uint8_t out[64])` | S7 | **NEW.** Tagged Merkle, rows STRICTLY ascending by tx_id (memcmp); a repeated intent_id anywhere in the set → -1 (checked over a sorted copy of the intent_ids, O(n log n); equal keys are byte-identical, so the sort's tie order cannot change the answer); a tx_id equal to another row's intent_id is not a conflict. Inner `"NDS.RPNODE.v1"`; n == 0 → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_REPLAY`. |
| `int dna_v2_global_root_v2(const uint8_t domains_root[64], const uint8_t replay_root[64], uint8_t out[64])` | S8 | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.GLOBAL.v2" ‖ domains_root ‖ replay_root)`. `dna_v2_global_root` (`"NDS.GLOBAL.v1"`) unchanged. |

### 1.3 HF-7 P1c-1 — the §8.9 roots wired into the witness, gated on generation ROOTS (snapshot design §8.9, §10.2 item 8, §10.3 matrix, §10.9, §10.11; Kurultay #24 item 7, Kurultay #27 summary items 7-8; branch `hf7-p1c1`, not versioned)

Every new behaviour is selected by the SYSTEM runtime's generation `>= NODUS_RT_GEN_ROOTS` (5). That generation is INERT until package P1d (no compiled table entry, `NODUS_RT_GEN_MAX` unchanged), so on every chain every root, byte and verdict is today's. **The gate:** in the apply engine `doms_roots_on(doms, n_dom)` (static, `nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) — the resolved runtime (`doms_load` → `nodus_witness_v2_runtime_for`) of the working set's SYSTEM domain, i.e. the same `rt->generation` phase 8's SYSTEM state-root hook reads; after the 6c re-scan it is the generation the phase-6b edge switched to, so block A−1's roots are already under ROOTS (matrix §10.3). A SYSTEM without a resolved runtime answers "not ROOTS" (the hook's own NULL rule). Outside the engine (`nodus_witness_global_root_v2`) the same answer is read through `nodus_witness_v2_runtime_for(w, DNA_DOMAIN_SYSTEM, 0, …)`.

| Function | Description |
|----------|-------------|
| `int nodus_witness_bcopy_root(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`) | **NEW.** §8.9 S1 over every `v2_balance_copy` row, SQL `ORDER BY epoch_start, validator_fp, owner_fp, kind`; `typeof()` on every column; epoch_start ≥ 0, 64-byte fingerprints, kind ∈ {0, 1}, amount ≥ 0 checked BEFORE `dna_v2_bcopy_root` — a negative / out-of-range value is a FAULT (-1), never a cast. Empty → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_BCOPY`. **Count-first (HF-7 P1c review F4, §10.12):** `SELECT COUNT(*)` on the same connection immediately before the scan, ONE allocation of exactly that many rows (no doubling); a scan that yields a row past the count, or ends with a different count, is a FAULT (-1); a negative count or one whose buffer overflows `size_t` is a FAULT. Root bytes unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_vstat_hash(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`) | **NEW.** §8.9 S2 over `validator_stats` key `'active_count'` (an INTEGER ≥ 0). The absent row is -1 (S2: mandatory). |
| `int nodus_witness_stmem_root(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`) | **NEW.** §8.9 S3 over every `v2_storage_set_members` row, `ORDER BY epoch_start, node_fp`; epoch_start ≥ 1, fail_streak 0..UINT32_MAX, grace_until ≥ 0. Empty → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_STMEM`. Count-first, one allocation, count ≠ scan → FAULT — the `nodus_witness_bcopy_root` rule above (F4). |
| `int nodus_witness_replay_root(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`) | **NEW.** §8.9 S7 over every `v2_replay` row (S19), `ORDER BY tx_id`; 64-byte ids, expiry_height an INTEGER ≥ 1 (a negative stored value refused, never cast into u64 — P1a audit finding B's second guard). Empty → `DNA_V2_EMPTY_REPLAY` (block A−1's value). Reads only; the relation's writes / prune are package P1c-2's. Count-first, one allocation, count ≠ scan → FAULT — the `nodus_witness_bcopy_root` rule above (F4: the doubling buffer held ≈ 570 MB plus a realloc peak at the 2.2 M-row worst case; now exactly n × 136 bytes). |
| `int nodus_witness_storage_segments_root_v2(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_v2_storage.h`) | **NEW.** §8.9 S5: `dna_v2_segments_root_v2` over the SAME scan as `nodus_witness_storage_segments_root` (k contiguous 1..n, k and published_height 1..INT64_MAX), published_height in the leaf. The v1 function is unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_storage_root_segv2(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`) | **NEW.** The `"NDS.STOR.v2"` composition with the v2 segments leg; registry / sets / reports legs shared with `nodus_witness_storage_root_v2` (unchanged — both are one static body, `storage_root_impl`). |
| `int nodus_witness_system_root_v6(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`) | **NEW.** §8.9 S4: the nine v5 legs read as `nodus_witness_system_root_v5` reads them (generation-4 validator / delegation leaves), storage leg via `nodus_witness_storage_root_segv2`, then bcopy / vstat / stmem → `dna_v2_system_root_v6`. |
| `int nodus_rt_system_state_root(const nodus_domain_runtime_t *rt, struct nodus_witness *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_v2_claims.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** `rt->generation >= NODUS_RT_GEN_ROOTS` → `nodus_witness_system_root_v6`; GEN_STORAGE..ROOTS−1 → v5, older → v4, NULL → v4, all as before. |
| `int nodus_witness_global_compose(nodus_witness_t *w, int roots_v2, const dna_v2_domain_head_t *heads, const uint8_t (*last_upd_hashes)[64], size_t n, uint8_t out_domains[64], uint8_t out_global[64])` (`nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`) | **NEW.** THE one GLOBAL composition every site calls (phase 13, the no-block-row recompute, the genesis, `nodus_witness_global_root_v2`) so the sites move together (Kurultay #27 item 8). `roots_v2 == 0`: `dna_v2_domains_root` + `dna_v2_global_root` — today's bytes, `last_upd_hashes` unread (may be NULL). `roots_v2 == 1`: `dna_v2_domains_root_v2(heads, last_upd_hashes)` + `dna_v2_global_root_v2(domains_root, nodus_witness_replay_root(w))` — the replay leg read at the call (phase 13: after phase 12 and the prune). |
| `int nodus_witness_global_root_v2(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out_global[64], uint8_t out_domains[64], uint8_t out_system[64], uint8_t out_core[64])` (`nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** its committed-heads branch, when SYSTEM resolves to ROOTS, reads every head with its `last_upd_hash` (NULL / not 64 bytes → -1) and composes through `nodus_witness_global_compose(…, 1, …)`. Otherwise (older generation, unresolvable runtime) the SELECT and the bytes are the old ones; the head-less pre-S5 fixture branch is unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_committed_global_root(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** the no-block-row recompute composes through `nodus_witness_global_compose` with `doms_roots_on`; under ROOTS every active head's column must be a 64-byte value or there is no root (-1). The tip-row read is unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_genesis_cmt(…)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** composes through `nodus_witness_global_compose` with `doms_roots_on` and activates each head with the sentinel column under ROOTS. The genesis registry is generation 1 today (`nodus_witness_domreg_init_genesis`), so its bytes are today's. |
| `NODUS_V2_LUH_OK` 0, `NODUS_V2_LUH_UNSEEDED` 1, `NODUS_V2_LUH_MIRROR` 2 (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **NEW.** Return codes of the head read below. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_head_last_upd(nodus_witness_t *w, uint32_t domain_id, uint8_t out[64])` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **NEW.** Phase 9's previous-update read under ROOTS (head-authoritative, §10.2 item 8 / G4): the head row's `last_upd_hash`, mirror-checked against `v2_root_history (domain_id, domain_height = the head's domain_height)` — the row bound to the head's own coordinates (phase 11 / `head_activate` write it), never "the latest row". Agree or ABSENT row → `NODUS_V2_LUH_OK` with `out` (JUDGMENT: no Kurultay text writes the absent case; G4 stops the node on a DISAGREEING row, and a restore that ships no history must execute); column NULL → `NODUS_V2_LUH_UNSEEDED`; disagreeing row → `NODUS_V2_LUH_MIRROR`; no head / malformed column or row / read fault → -1. Reads only. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_domain_heads_seed_last_upd(nodus_witness_t *w)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **NEW.** Production caller since HF-7 P1d (§29): `phase_6b_gen5_edge`, in phase 6b of block A−1, after the registry switch and before the 6c re-scan. For every `v2_domain_heads` row (domain_id range-checked 0..UINT32_MAX — P1a audit finding A) fills `last_upd_hash` with TODAY'S history query (latest `v2_domain_updates.upd_hash`, else the genesis sentinel `dna_dupd_prev_genesis`), cross-checked with the mirror row (disagree → -1, absent → accepted). Same value already present → left (idempotent); a different value → -1. Inside the caller's transaction only (autocommit → -1); an empty head table → -1. |
| static `head_store` / `head_load` / `head_activate` / `prev_update_hash`, phases 9 / 10 / 13 (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour, internal):** below ROOTS byte-identical (the column is neither named nor selected; `prev_update_hash` keeps today's query, now one shared string `PREV_UPDATE_SQL`). Under ROOTS: `head_load` selects the column (state LUH_NULL / LUH_OK / LUH_BAD, judged by the reader); `head_store` always names and binds it (an `INSERT OR REPLACE` that omitted it would null the seed); `head_activate` stores the genesis sentinel; phase 9 reads `nodus_witness_v2_head_last_upd` (UNSEEDED / MIRROR / fault → node FAULT); phase 10 gives a touched head this block's update hash, an untouched head keeps its value (no write); phase 13 FAULTs on an untouched head whose column is not a valid 64-byte value. |

Constants: `NODUS_TREASURY_POOL_MIN` 1, `NODUS_TREASURY_POOL_MAX` 9,
`NODUS_TREASURY_POOL_COUNT` 9. (`NODUS_TREASURY_POOL_FOUNDATION` 8 is
**REMOVED** by general multisig with the refund below — nothing names
pool 8 any more.)

|----------|-------------|

| `int nodus_witness_treasury_total(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t *out)` | **NEW (W-A).** Σ `v2_treasury.balance` through the same row checks, checked add — the supply equation's treasury term. |
| ~~`int nodus_witness_treasury_credit(nodus_witness_t *w, uint32_t pool_id, uint64_t amount)`~~ | **REMOVED (general multisig).** Added by W-A for the refund below, its only caller; deleted with it (no dead code). No block path writes a pool after genesis. |
| ~~`int nodus_witness_treasury_graduation_refund(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t active_since_block, uint64_t self_stake, int *out_to_treasury)`~~ | **REMOVED (general multisig, decision `2026-09-29-general-multisig.md`).** W-A credited a genesis seat's bond into pool 8 with no UTXO; now EVERY graduate releases its bond as a locked UTXO to its `unstake_destination_fp` — for a genesis seat the Foundation multisig address. |
| `int nodus_witness_system_root_v2(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` | **CHANGED (W-A, behaviour):** composes the treasury leg (8 legs, `"NDS.SYS.v4"`). Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_system_payload_root_v2(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t out[64])` | **CHANGED (W-A, behaviour):** composes the treasury leg (5 legs, `"NDS.SYSPAYL.v3"`). Signature unchanged. |

## 5b. HF-6 slice 1 — validator consent, validator_id, vset v2 (INERT; design `docs/plans/2026-10-03-hf5-validator-owner-stake-design.md` §6)

No live path calls anything in this section yet; the bytes are pinned by `shared/dnac/tests/valbind_oracle.py` (independent oracle) in `nodus/tests/test_valbind_wire.c` and `test_vset_wire.c`.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int dna_valbind_consent_msg(uint8_t out[2765], const uint8_t chain32[32], uint8_t op, const uint8_t subject[64], const uint8_t owner_fp[64], const uint8_t consensus_pk[2592], uint64_t expiry_height)` (`shared/dnac/valbind_wire.h`) | **NEW.** Builds the 2765-byte node-consent message: version u32 = 1 ‖ chain 32 ‖ op u8 ‖ subject 64 ‖ owner_fp 64 ‖ consensus_pk 2592 ‖ expiry u64 (BE). op 1 = STAKE (subject must be all-zero), 2 = ROTATE (subject must not be all-zero), 3 = STORAGE_REGISTER (`DNA_VALBIND_OP_STORAGE_REGISTER`, design rev 6 §6.1 / §11.3: subject must be all-zero, the 2592-byte key field carries the storage node_pk); anything else -1, `out` untouched. |
| `int dna_valbind_expiry_ok(uint64_t h, uint64_t expiry)` | **NEW.** 1 iff `expiry >= h && expiry - h <= 720` (`DNA_VALBIND_MAX_EXPIRY_BLOCKS`, a fixed number, not the epoch length); never forms `h + 720`. |
| `int dna_valbind_consent_sign(uint8_t sig[4627], size_t *siglen, const uint8_t msg[2765], const uint8_t sk[4896])` | **NEW.** Refuses a malformed message (version, op ∉ {1, 2, 3}, op/subject rule); signs with ML-DSA-87 ctx `"nodus.valbind.v1"` (16 B, no NUL). |
| `int dna_valbind_consent_verify(const uint8_t *sig, size_t siglen, const uint8_t msg[2765])` | **NEW.** Parses version/op (1, 2 or 3)/subject, then verifies with the consensus_pk EMBEDDED at offset 165 (no caller key — signer binding). Does NOT check chain, owner or expiry: the caller does (design §2 reconstruction, `dna_valbind_expiry_ok`). |
| `int dna_validator_id_new(uint8_t out[64], const uint8_t chain32[32], const uint8_t intent_id[64])` | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.VALID.v1"+4×0 ‖ chain32 ‖ intent_id)` (112-byte preimage). |
| `int dna_validator_id_migrated(uint8_t out[64], const uint8_t chain32[32], const uint8_t old_pubkey[2592])` | **NEW.** `SHA3-512("NDS.VALMIG.v1"+3×0 ‖ chain32 ‖ SHA3-512(old_pubkey))` (112-byte preimage). |
| `dna_vset_entry_t` (`shared/dnac/vset_wire.h`) | **CHANGED:** two fields appended, `validator_id[64]`, `owner_fp[64]`. Under ruleset 1 both must be zero (encode rejects nonzero — fail closed; decode leaves them zero). |
| `size_t dna_vset_encoded_len_ex(snap, uint32_t max_ruleset)`, `int dna_vset_encode_ex(snap, max_ruleset, dst, cap, written)`, `int dna_vset_decode_ex(src, len, max_ruleset, out)`, `int dna_vset_hash_ex(snap, max_ruleset, out)`, `int dna_vset_hash_bytes_ex(buf, len, max_ruleset, out)` | **NEW.** Accept rulesets 1..`max_ruleset` (1 or 2; else -1). Ruleset 2 (`DNA_VSET_RULESET_TOPN_V2`): entry 2770 B = voter_id 32 ‖ validator_id 64 ‖ owner_fp 64 ‖ consensus_pk 2592 ‖ total u64 ‖ self u64 ‖ commission u16; tag `"NDS.VSET.v2"`; seed all-zero; duplicate voter_id / validator_id / pk, all-zero validator_id / owner_fp, and voter_id ≠ SHA3-512(pk)[0..31] rejected in encode AND decode. `hash_bytes_ex` reads the ruleset at header offset 10 to pick the tag. Max v2 encoding 354638 (`DNA_VSET_MAX_ENC_LEN_V2`). |
| `dna_vset_encoded_len / encode / decode / hash` | **CHANGED (implementation only):** now `_ex(…, 1)` wrappers — behaviour byte-identical, still ruleset 1 only. |
| `int dna_vset_hash_bytes(const uint8_t *buf, size_t len, uint8_t out[64])` | **UNCHANGED** body: any 1..338254 bytes under the v1 tag, header not read (kept so no input's result changes). The live store/load path (`nodus_witness_vset.c`) must move to the `_ex` forms together when v2 is switched on. |

### 5b.1 HF-6 sub-slice 3a — schema S18 + v2 row access (INERT; design rev 6 §1.2, §6.3, §6.4, §7; plan `tasks/hf6-slice3-plan.md` §1/§2 "3a", decision D1 option (i))

Consumed since sub-slice 3d by the generation-4 boundary / root readers (5b.1c). Every existing validator / delegation function is unchanged. Return convention: 0 found / done, 1 absent, -1 fault — a half-migrated row (validators row without its `v2_validator_roles` row or the reverse), a malformed row and a missing table are -1, never 1. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_v2_schema.c` (`run_s18_rung`), `nodus/tests/test_v2_valrow.c`.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_V2_SCHEMA_VERSION_S18` (= 18u), `nodus_v2s18_mig_fail_t`, `int nodus_witness_db_migrate_v2s18(nodus_witness_t *w)`, `int nodus_witness_db_migrate_v2s18_ex(nodus_witness_t *w, nodus_v2s18_mig_fail_t fail_at)` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_v2_schema.h`) | **NEW.** Atomic 17 → 18 rung (versions below 17 cascade through S17 first): `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS v2_validator_roles (validator_id BLOB PRIMARY KEY, owner_fp BLOB NOT NULL, pending_consensus_pubkey BLOB NOT NULL, consensus_key_effective_epoch INTEGER NOT NULL)`, column-sequence shape check, `user_version = 18`. 0 migrated / already 18; -1 failure (full rollback) incl. version 19+. Five fault stages (AFTER_BEGIN, AFTER_REVALIDATE, AFTER_TABLES, AFTER_VERIFY, BEFORE_COMMIT). |
| `int nodus_witness_db_ensure_v2s17_indexes(nodus_witness_t *w)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** runs at S17 **or S18** (was S17 only) — the at-open rung now reaches S18 before this step. |
| `static int witness_open_migrate_s18(nodus_witness_t *witness, const char *db_path)` (`nodus_witness.c`) | **RENAMED + CHANGED** from `witness_open_migrate_s17`: on an established version-3 chain at S16 **or S17**, calls `nodus_witness_db_migrate_v2s18`. |
| `nodus_v2_validator_row_t` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_validator.h`) | **NEW** witness-local type: `dnac_validator_record_t rec` (`rec.pubkey` = consensus pubkey) ‖ `validator_id[64]` ‖ `owner_fp[64]` ‖ `pending_consensus_pubkey[2592]` ‖ `uint64_t consensus_key_effective_epoch`. Constants `NODUS_V2_VALIDATOR_ID_LEN`, `NODUS_V2_OWNER_FP_LEN` (64). |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_get(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t validator_id[64], nodus_v2_validator_row_t *out)` | **NEW.** `validators v LEFT JOIN v2_validator_roles r ON r.validator_id = v.pubkey_hash WHERE v.pubkey_hash = ?`. Validates blob lengths, non-zero validator_id / owner_fp, the single "no pending key" encoding (2592 × 0 ⇔ epoch 0), epoch ≥ 0. An orphan roles row is -1. |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_get_by_pubkey(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t *pubkey, nodus_v2_validator_row_t *out)` | **DELETED (§5b.20, red-team round 1 close-out).** Was: same join `WHERE v.pubkey = ?`, a second row with the pubkey -1. No production caller since slice 5c (§5b.4). |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_update(nodus_witness_t *w, const nodus_v2_validator_row_t *row)` | **NEW.** Inside the caller's transaction only (autocommit → -1, nothing written). `UPDATE validators` (the 13 mutable columns) `WHERE pubkey_hash = validator_id AND pubkey = rec.pubkey`, then `UPDATE v2_validator_roles SET pending_consensus_pubkey, consensus_key_effective_epoch WHERE validator_id AND owner_fp` — validator_id, consensus pubkey and owner_fp are immutable (no TRANSFER_OWNER). Refuses before writing: all-zero validator_id / owner_fp, a mixed pending encoding, epoch > INT64_MAX. 0 both updated; 1 neither table holds the key; -1 otherwise (the caller rolls back). |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_bonded_tenured(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t tenure_anchor, nodus_v2_validator_row_t *out, int cap, int *count_out)` | **NEW** — the gen-4 arm; `nodus_validator_bonded_tenured` stays byte-identical. Same WHERE (ACTIVE/ELIGIBLE, tenure or `active_since_block <= 1`) and `ORDER BY pubkey ASC`; each candidate's `pubkey_hash` is its validator_id. More rows than `cap` or a candidate without a roles row is -1. |
| `nodus_v2_delegation_row_t` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_delegation.h`) | **NEW**: `delegator_fp[64]` ‖ `validator_id[64]` ‖ `uint64_t amount` ‖ `uint64_t delegated_at_block`. `NODUS_V2_DELEGATION_KEY_LEN` (64). |
| `int nodus_delegation_v2_get(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t delegator_fp[64], const uint8_t validator_id[64], nodus_v2_delegation_row_t *out)` | **NEW.** `WHERE delegator_hash = ? AND validator_hash = ?`, keys bound as given (no hashing). Malformed row (key ≠ 64 B, amount ∉ [1, INT64_MAX], negative height) → -1. |
| `int nodus_delegation_v2_delete(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t delegator_fp[64], const uint8_t validator_id[64])` | **NEW.** 0 deleted / 1 absent / -1. |
| `int nodus_delegation_v2_count_by_validator(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t validator_id[64], int *count_out)` | **NEW.** `COUNT(*) WHERE validator_hash = ?`; -1 on error or a count outside [0, INT_MAX]. |
| `int nodus_delegation_v2_list_by_validator(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t validator_id[64], nodus_v2_delegation_row_t *out, int cap, int *count_out)` | **NEW.** Every row of the validator `ORDER BY delegator_hash ASC` (a total order — PK); more rows than `cap` → -1, never a truncation; a malformed row → -1. |

### 5b.1b HF-6 sub-slice 3b — the generation-4 edge + the validator migration (design rev 6 §7, §8; plan `tasks/hf6-slice3-plan.md` §2 "3b"; decisions Kurultay #14 K4 / K8, Kurultay #16 D2)

Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf6_migrate.c` (EVM builds), `nodus/tests/test_hf4_switch.c` `t_storage_switch`. Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 sub-slice 3b".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_witness_v2_roles_migrate(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h_next, const uint8_t chain32[32], const uint8_t foundation_fp[64])` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_v2_migrate.h`) | **NEW.** The one-shot generation-4 migration, inside the caller's transaction (autocommit → -2, nothing read). Read all → compute in memory → write. `validators.pubkey_hash` := `dna_validator_id_migrated(chain32, pk)`; one `v2_validator_roles` row (validator_id, owner, 2592 × 0x00, 0) per validator, owner = `foundation_fp` iff `active_since_block <= 1`, else SHA3-512(pk); bonded rows (ACTIVE/ELIGIBLE) must hold exactly `DNAC_SELF_STAKE_AMOUNT` → `DNAC_HF6_STAKE_MIN_DEFAULT` with total/external_delegated += the difference, which becomes the self-delegation (owner_fp, validator_id): top-up of an existing re-keyed row (height kept) or a new row at `h_next`; delegations re-keyed to (SHA3-512(dpk), validator_id); `v2_balance_copy` of every epoch re-keyed (validator_fp and kind-0 owner := validator_id, amounts kept); K4: Foundation rows' accruals merged into `foundation_fp` (checked add ≤ INT64_MAX), sources deleted. Returns 0, or -2 (node FAULT) on: `h_next` ∉ [1, INT64_MAX], all-zero `foundation_fp`, a non-empty roles table, a key not equal to the tagged hash of its pubkey, a bonded row ≠ 10M, a delegation / copy row naming no validator, an amount outside [0, INT64_MAX], a duplicate computed key, a staked-supply mismatch, any SQLite error or unexpected row count. |
| `static int phase_6b_gen4_edge(nodus_witness_t *w, nodus_v2_block_t *blk, dom_ctx_t *doms, size_t n_dom)` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **NEW** engine phase 6b‴ (after 6b′, BEFORE 6b″ — an EVM edge in the same block faults — and before 6c). Trigger (since slice 8c, §5b.19): param 18 `RULESET_GEN_ROLES` = `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROLES_D` (D4) effective at h+1 and absent at h (until 8c the development param-16 row); then `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED` (else FAULT), schema == S18 (else FAULT), `nodus_witness_domreg_generation_switch(3, 4)` ("registry is not generation 3" FAULT), `nodus_witness_v2_roles_migrate`, SYSTEM + CORE touched. 0 / -2. |
| `RULESET_EDGES` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **CHANGED:** the param-16 row (GEN_STORAGE_BASE → GEN_STORAGE) is removed; only HF-4's param-9 row remains. |
| `DNAC_HF6_FOUNDATION_OWNER`, `DNAC_HF6_FOUNDATION_OWNER_IS_DEFAULT`, `DNAC_HF6_STAKE_MIN_DEFAULT` (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`) | **NEW.** The K8 Foundation address as a 64-byte brace initializer (`#ifndef`, test-build override only; `_IS_DEFAULT` defined only for the compiled K8 value); the 2M × 10^8 raw STAKE_MIN default (static-asserted == 2·10^14 and < `DNAC_SELF_STAKE_AMOUNT`). |

| `RTN_SYS_STAKE_V2_CALL_LEN` (7301), `RTN_SYS_MAX_CALL_LEN` (`nodus_witness_rt_native.c`) | **NEW.** The generation-4 STAKE call `owner_fp[64] ‖ consensus_pk[2592] ‖ consent_sig[4627] ‖ consent_expiry u64 ‖ commission u16 ‖ bond u64`; the longest SYSTEM call, now the governing call of the capacity asserts (single leg 708,174; worst envelope 860,228 ≤ 2^20 — `DNA_ENV_MAX_TOTAL_LEN` unchanged). |

| `static uint64_t rtn_stake_min_at(const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx)` | **NEW.** THE ONE read of the bond floor: at slice 4 the compiled `DNAC_HF6_STAKE_MIN_DEFAULT` (2M NODUS). **Slice 8a:** now `static int rtn_stake_min_at(ctx, uint64_t *out)` returning the engine fact `ctx->stake_min` (param 19) — §5b.17. |
| `static int rtn_stake2_vid(const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW.** `dna_validator_id_new(ctx->chain_id, ctx->intent_id)` (§6.2). 0 / -2. |
| `static int rtn_stake2_read_plan(const dna_env_view_t *env, uint16_t leg_index, const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx, nodus_rt_read_req_t *reqs_out, uint16_t max_reqs, uint16_t *n_out)` | **NEW.** `[op 7 active_count] [op 13 validator_id] [op 17 SHA3-512(consensus_pk)]`. |
| `static int rtn_stake2_exec(...)` | **NEW.** Owner rule against the call's owner_fp (kind 1 / kind 3); commission ≤ max; `STAKE_MIN ≤ bond ≤ INT64_MAX`; `dna_valbind_expiry_ok(h, expiry)`; consent message rebuilt (version 1, `ctx->chain_id`, op 1, 64 × 0 subject, owner_fp, consensus_pk, expiry) and `dna_valbind_consent_verify`; reads: validator_id absent, key not reserved, active_count present, Rule M. Effects: CREATE op 13 (the §6.3 row), CREATE op 17 (vid ‖ role 1), SET op 7 (+1). 0 / -1 / -2. |
| `static int rtn_sys_call_flow(...)`, `static int rtn_sysfund_shape(...)`, `static uint32_t rtn_sys_call_gen_of(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** the 3c generation-4 STAKE refusals are removed — lock = bond; the sibling pairs like ops 2-5; 7301 B → `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE`. |
| `RTN_SYS_OP_KEYRES` (17); `RTN_SYS_OPS[15]`; `RTN_SYS_OP_VAL2` (13) kinds | **NEW / CHANGED.** Op 17: CREATE / ABSENT, key 64 (SHA3-512(consensus_pk), untagged), value 65 (validator_id ‖ role). Op 13 gains CREATE / ABSENT. `NODUS_RT_SYSTEM_ADAPTER.n_ops` 14 → 15. |
| `static nodus_adapter_status_t rtn_sys_val2_create(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t *key, const uint8_t *v)`, `static int rtn_sys_keyres_fetch(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t *key, uint16_t key_len, uint8_t rec[65])` | **NEW.** VAL2 CREATE: strict INSERT into `validators` (pubkey = consensus key; `unstake_destination_fp` = hex(owner_fp), `unstake_destination_pubkey` = 2592 × 0 — full-length for the v1 loader, never read under gen 4) and `v2_validator_roles`; each exactly one row. KEYRES fetch via `nodus_validator_v2_key_get`. `rtn_sys_probe` / `_read` / `_mutate` gain the op-17 arms and the op-13 CREATE arm. |
| `nodus_rt_native_describe_leg` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** an applied 7301-B STAKE is described by ADDRESS — `rec_v2` 0, `rec_validator_fp` = `rec_dest_fp` = owner_fp, `rec_amount` = bond (the describer has no chain id, so it cannot derive the validator_id). |
| `NODUS_V2_KEY_HASH_LEN`, `NODUS_V2_KEY_ROLE_ACTIVE` (1), `NODUS_V2_KEY_ROLE_PENDING` (2); `int nodus_validator_v2_key_get(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t key_hash[64], uint8_t validator_id_out[64], uint8_t *role_out)` (`nodus_witness_validator.h`) | **NEW.** One `v2_consensus_keys` row: 0 found / 1 absent / -1 fault (validator_id not 64 B or all-zero, role ∉ {1, 2}). |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_key_insert(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t key_hash[64], const uint8_t validator_id[64], uint8_t role)` | **NEW.** Strict INSERT inside the caller's transaction (autocommit → -1); refuses role ∉ {1, 2} and all-zero key / id. 0 / -1. |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_key_release(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t validator_id[64], const uint8_t *consensus_pubkey)` | **NEW.** Inside the caller's transaction: deletes the role-1 row (SHA3-512(pubkey), validator_id) — exactly one, else -1 — then every other row of the validator (since slice 6e: every role-2 row only, §5b.10). 0 / -1. Caller: `v2ep_graduate` (generation 4). |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_roles_migrate(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_migrate.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** also requires `v2_consensus_keys` empty and writes one role-1 reservation per migrated row whose status is not UNSTAKED. |
| `static int v2ep_graduate(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** generation 4 releases the graduate's reservation (`nodus_validator_v2_key_release`) after the row write-back. |
| `nodus_witness_db_migrate_v2s18_ex(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_schema.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** the S18 rung also creates and shape-verifies `v2_consensus_keys (consensus_pk_hash PK, validator_id, role)` — same unreleased rung, no S19. |

### 5b.2 HF-6 slice 5a — water-fill power cap (INERT; design rev 6 §5; Kurultay #14 K1, K2, K5)

`shared/dnac/power_cap.c` is compiled into BOTH `libnodus` (nodus build) and `libdna.so` (messenger build, shared and static lists, next to `vset_wire.c`). No live path calls it yet; every consumer listed in design §5 switches to it in a later slice. Vectors are pinned by the independent oracle `shared/dnac/tests/power_cap_oracle.py` (output `power_cap_oracle_out.txt`) in `nodus/tests/test_power_cap.c`.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int dna_power_cap_counted(const uint64_t *p, const uint8_t (*validator_id)[64], uint32_t n, uint32_t ratio_bps, uint64_t *counted_out)` (`shared/dnac/power_cap.h`) | **NEW.** Counted power per validator. `p_i` = whole coins (`total_stake_i / DNAC_DECIMAL_UNIT`, the caller divides), each ≥ 1. Walk order p DESC, ties `validator_id` ASC (memcmp) on an internal index array — the caller's arrays are never reordered; `counted_out` is in the caller's order. `r′ = max(ratio_bps, ⌈10000/n⌉)`; for k = 0, 1, … over the sorted `q[0..n-1]`: `S_k = q[k] + … + q[n-1]` (§5's 1-based `Σ_{i>k} p_i`, the uncapped tail including the entry under test), `c_k = ⌊r′·S_k / (10000 − r′·k)⌋`, stop at the first k with `q[k] ≤ c_k`; `counted_i = min(p_i, c_k)`. §5 examples at ratio 3000: `[88,2×6]` → `[5,2×6]`, `[45,45,10]` → `[10,10,10]`, `[10]×7` unchanged. Guarantees (proved in the header): stops at some k < n with a positive denominator, every counted ≥ 1, no counted share exceeds r′ (for n ≤ 3 r′ is 100% / 50% / 33.34%). Integer only, every sum/product checked (`dna_ck_*_u64`). Returns 0, or -1 with `counted_out` untouched on: a NULL argument, n == 0, n > `DNA_MAX_ACTIVE_VALIDATORS` (128), ratio outside [`DNA_POWER_CAP_BPS_MIN` 500, `DNA_POWER_CAP_BPS_MAX` 3000], any `p_i == 0`, u64 overflow, a denominator ≤ 0. |

### 5b.3 HF-6 slice 5b — the ONE voting-power helper and every consumer (design rev 6 §5, §8, §11.7; Kurultay #14 K1, K2, K5; plan `tasks/hf6-slice5-plan.md` §2 "5b"; ORCHESTRATOR decisions D2, D3, D5, D6)

Every consumer of a snapshot's voting power now calls `nodus_witness_vset_powers`. Byte-identical for every ruleset-1 snapshot (the only ruleset the 5b store/read path carries — no ruleset-2 snapshot can reach a consumer before 5c/5d). Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf6_power.c` (EVM builds), `nodus/tests/test_committee_cache.c` (5)/(6). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 5b".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `DNAC_HF6_POWER_CAP_BPS_DEFAULT` (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`) | **NEW** = `3000u` (design §5 / §8 param 20 default). `_Static_assert` in `nodus_witness_vset.c`: within [`DNA_POWER_CAP_BPS_MIN`, `DNA_POWER_CAP_BPS_MAX`]. Slice 8a made it the param-20 no-row default (§5b.17); D4 consts[2] is slice 8b's. |
| `int nodus_witness_power_cap_bps_for_epoch(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t epoch_start, uint32_t *ratio_out)` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_vset.h`) | **NEW.** THE ratio read, keyed on the snapshot's epoch START. At slice 5b it wrote `DNAC_HF6_POWER_CAP_BPS_DEFAULT`; **slice 8a** replaced only the body with the fail-closed param-20 chain-config read (the `committee_target_for_epoch` pattern) — §5b.17. 0 / -1. |
| `int nodus_witness_vset_powers(nodus_witness_t *w, const dna_vset_snapshot_t *snap, uint64_t out[])` (`nodus_witness_vset.h`) | **NEW.** Voting power per entry, snapshot order, by the snapshot's own ruleset: ruleset 1 → `total_stake / DNAC_DECIMAL_UNIT` (identity; zero allowed, as before); ruleset 2 → `p_i = total_stake / DNAC_DECIMAL_UNIT`, any `p_i == 0` → -1, ratio from the seam at `snap->epoch`, `dna_power_cap_counted(p, validator_id, n, ratio, out)` (its -1 → -1); any other ruleset → -1. `out` holds `active_count` values (written only on 0); `active_count > DNA_MAX_ACTIVE_VALIDATORS` → -1. The snapshot is never modified (`total_stake` stays raw). **Every caller treats -1 as a node fault.** |
| `nodus_committee_member_t.power` (`nodus_witness_committee.h`) | **NEW field** (`uint64_t`). Set only by `nodus_committee_get_for_block` (hit and miss alike); left 0 by `nodus_committee_compute_for_epoch` / `_bootstrap_for_epoch` (`emit_member`) — the snapshot builder never reads it. Not on any wire or hash. |
| `nodus_witness_t.cached_committee_powers[DNAC_MAX_ACTIVE_VALIDATORS]` (`nodus_witness.h`) | **NEW** parallel cache array (+1 KB). Filled on the miss, copied out on the hit. |
| `int nodus_committee_get_for_block(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** fills `power`. Snapshot-served epoch: `nodus_witness_vset_powers` over the served row (-1 → -1, cache left invalid). Row-less epoch: below key generation `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE` recomputed as before with `power = total_stake / DNAC_DECIMAL_UNIT`; at generation ≥ 4, or an unreadable generation, -1 with the cache left invalid (decision D5). |
| `static int v2ep_rn_weight_verdict(nodus_witness_t *w, const dna_vset_snapshot_t *s, uint64_t *p_out, uint64_t *m_out, int *allowed_out)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED signature** (+ `w`): powers from the helper (Rule N's floor over counted power, design §5); helper -1 → -1 (the boundary faults). Sole caller `v2ep_rule_n`. |
| `nodus_cmt_app_init_chain` / `nodus_cmt_app_finalize_block` (`nodus_witness_cmt_app.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signatures unchanged:** InitChain compares the request's powers with the helper's output over snapshot(0); the boundary ValidatorUpdates diff computes both snapshots' powers with the helper once before its loops (each by its own ruleset; matching by pubkey; power 0 on the new side stays a fault); helper -1 → `CMT_FAULT`. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_settlement_apply(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_econ.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** member power (share numerator, Σ denominator — K2) from the helper, computed once before pass 1; inner split (`self_bond / total_stake`, commission, `a_d / Σa_d`) unchanged on raw amounts; helper -1 → -2. `v2ec_member_load` no longer sets `power`. Bridge and the ruleset-2 refusal unchanged (5c). |
| `static int st_settle(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_storage.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** seat power from the helper (decision D3); helper -1 → fault; F2 / payee unchanged (6b). |
| `static int committee_snapshot_for_height(...)` HF-2 powers (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`), `nodus-cli` local approval selection (`nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** read `nodus_committee_member_t.power`. The CLI committee display (RPC `"stake"`) is unchanged (decision D2 → 5e). |

### 5b.4 HF-6 slice 5c — both snapshot versions stored and read, the v2-entry readers, the id-keyed econ bridge (design rev 6 §1.6, §6.5, §7.5; plan `tasks/hf6-slice5-plan.md` §2 "5c"; ORCHESTRATOR decision D6)

Inert on a real chain: the builder still writes ruleset 1 (slice 5d switches it by key generation), and accepting ruleset 2 on read is a strict superset for every well-formed ruleset-1 row. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_vset_persist.c` §4–§5, `test_hf6_readers.c` case E, `test_storage_b2.c` case E, `test_cmt_app.c` `val_updates_v2_old_side`. Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 5c".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_witness_vset_insert(...)` / `int nodus_witness_vset_get(...)` / `int nodus_witness_vset_build_for_epoch(...)` (`nodus_witness_vset.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signatures unchanged:** every codec call is the `_ex` family at `max_ruleset = DNA_VSET_RULESET_TOPN_V2` (`dna_vset_hash_bytes_ex`, `dna_vset_decode_ex`, `dna_vset_encoded_len_ex`, `dna_vset_encode_ex`); the outer length cap is `DNA_VSET_MAX_ENC_LEN_V2` (354638), each ruleset's own ceiling enforced by the hash. The blob's header picks the tag, so a stored ruleset-1 row re-hashes to exactly its stored hash (`NDS.VSET.v1`), a ruleset-2 row hashes under `NDS.VSET.v2`. `_insert` now also refuses (-1) a blob shorter than a header or naming ruleset 0 / ≥ 3 — rows the old decode already refused on every read. No ruleset-1-only codec entry point remains in `nodus_witness_vset.c`. |
| `int nodus_witness_vset_entry_from_member(const nodus_committee_member_t *m, uint32_t ruleset, dna_vset_entry_t *e)` (`nodus_witness_vset.h`) | **NEW.** The builder's per-entry fill (`vset_build_snapshot` calls it for every member): `voter_id` = `nodus_chain_config_derive_witness_id(pubkey)`, pubkey, `total_stake`, `self_bond` = member `self_stake`, `commission_bps`; ruleset 2 copies the member's `validator_id` / `owner_fp` (a zero id is not repaired — the v2 encoder refuses it); ruleset 1 writes both all zero whatever the member carries. 0 / -1 (NULL, a ruleset other than 1 / 2, a voter_id derivation failure). |
| `nodus_committee_member_t.validator_id[64]`, `.owner_fp[64]` (`nodus_witness_committee.h`) | **NEW fields** (builder-only, decision D6). Set by `nodus_committee_compute_for_epoch` (`emit_member`, from the candidate's v2 row) at key generation ≥ `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE`; all zero below it and on the bootstrap path. NOT cached: `nodus_committee_get_for_block` returns them all zero on a hit and on a miss (the snapshot-served miss included, even when the snapshot's entries carry ids). Member size 2752 B (~344 KB × 128). `emit_member` now writes `power = 0` explicitly. |
| `int nodus_committee_compute_for_epoch(...)` / `int nodus_committee_get_for_block(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signatures unchanged:** the two id fields above; nothing else (the tiebreak and the generation plumbing are slice 5d's). |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_settlement_apply(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_econ.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** the ruleset-2 refusal is GONE. Member resolution by the snapshot's ruleset (static `v2ec_member_load` gains a `ruleset` parameter): ruleset 2 → copy key `entry.validator_id`, row `nodus_validator_v2_get(entry.validator_id)` (-1 → -2; absent → unpaid, its share stays in the pool), payee `entry.owner_fp`; a found row whose `owner_fp` or consensus pubkey differs from the entry's → -2 (the pubkey check is slice 6's to replace); ruleset 1 at generation ≥ 4 (§7.5 bridge) → copy key `dna_validator_id_migrated(chain id, pubkey)`, row `nodus_validator_v2_get(that id)` — the old "found row's validator_id == migrated id" check is structural now; payee the row's `owner_fp`. A ruleset-2 governing snapshot below generation 4 → -2. Below generation 4 a ruleset-1 snapshot is paid byte-identically. Attendance stays keyed by the entry pubkey. |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_get_by_pubkey(...)` | **No production caller after 5c** (its only one was the econ bridge above). Not removed in 5c (file outside the slice's whitelist) — removed in the red-team round 1 close-out (§5b.20). |

### 5b.5 HF-6 slice 5d — the v1 → v2 snapshot switch, the validator_id tiebreak, the generation plumbing (design rev 6 §7.7, §7.8 item 8 — the khan's tiebreak decision 2026-10-12 "A ile devam edebiliriz" —, §8; plan `tasks/hf6-slice5-plan.md` §1, §2 "5d"; ORCHESTRATOR decision D4 (i))

The chain's behaviour changes here: the first boundary reading key generation ≥ `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE` stores the first ruleset-2 snapshot. These rows SUPERSEDE the "signature unchanged" wording of the §5b.2 / §5b.4 rows for the functions below. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_committee_election.c` (7), `test_hf6_readers.c` cases E (re-based), F, G; harness `test_hf6_edge.sh` 2b, 8a-8c. Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 5d".

|------|-------------|

The bootstrap path (`e_start < E + 1`) does not read `key_gen`. 0 / -1 as before. Callers: `vset_build_snapshot` (the boundary's / genesis's value), `nodus_committee_get_for_block`'s miss path (its own one read — always < 4 there, D5). (`nodus_witness_genesis_seed_validators`, which had no caller, was deleted as dead code.) |
| `int nodus_witness_vset_build_for_epoch(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t epoch_start, int max_active, uint32_t key_gen, dna_vset_snapshot_t **snapshot_out, uint8_t **blob_out, size_t *blob_len_out, uint8_t hash_out64[64])` (`nodus_witness_vset.h`) | **CHANGED (signature):** new `key_gen` (after `max_active`), handed to the committee core unchanged; the snapshot's `selection_ruleset` = `DNA_VSET_RULESET_TOPN_V2` when `key_gen ≥ NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE`, else `TOPN_V1` (static `vset_build_snapshot`, also new `key_gen`). |

A chain whose edge flips the registry in H−1 stores the first ruleset-2 row at B₀ = the first multiple of E above H, for epoch B₀ + E; every row ≤ B₀ stays ruleset 1. |
| `int nodus_witness_vset_preview_next(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t boundary_height, uint32_t key_gen, dna_vset_snapshot_t **snapshot_out)` | **CHANGED (signature):** `key_gen` before the out-parameter; Rule N (static `v2ep_rule_n`, new `key_gen`) passes the boundary's read — the same value `commit_next` receives, so the preview Rule N judges and the stored snapshot cannot differ in ruleset, candidates or tiebreak. |

| `static int rtn_sysfund_shape(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** a SYSFUND leg pairs with op 10 only under generation 4. |
| `static uint32_t rtn_sys_call_gen_of(uint32_t op, uint32_t len)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** op 10 → `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE`. |
| `static uint64_t rtn_rotate2_first_boundary(uint64_t h)` | **NEW (internal).** F = the first epoch boundary ≥ h (h at a boundary, else ceil(h / E) · E). |
| `static int rtn_rotate2_read_plan(const dna_env_view_t *env, uint16_t leg_index, nodus_rt_read_req_t *reqs_out, uint16_t max_reqs, uint16_t *n_out)` | **NEW (internal).** `[VAL2(13, validator_id)] [KEYRES(17, SHA3-512(new_pk))]`; 0 / -1 / -2. |
| `static int rtn_rotate2_exec(const dna_env_view_t *env, uint16_t leg_index, const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx, const nodus_rt_read_res_t *reads, uint16_t n_reads, uint8_t *res_out, size_t res_cap, size_t *res_len_out)` | **NEW (internal).** Owner of the committed row (kind 1 / 3), status ACTIVE / ELIGIBLE, no pending key, new_pk ≠ current, reservation absent (any role), expiry window, the op-2 consent rebuilt and verified under new_pk; effects CREATE op 17 (role 2) then SET op 13 (pending = new_pk, epoch = F). 0 / -1 / -2. |
| `int nodus_rt_system_read_plan(...)` / `int nodus_rt_system_exec(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** dispatch op 10 under generation 4 to the two functions above; below generation 4 op 10 stays the -1 default. Signatures unchanged. |

### 5b.8 HF-6 slice 6c — by-validator_id membership, the swap writer, the key promotion (design rev 6 §3 + khan A2 / A5 / A1, §4; plan `tasks/hf6-slice6-plan.md` §1.4, §1.5, §1.11, §2 "6c", B3 / B4 / B5 / B8)

Inert on a chain with no pending row: every by-id arm runs only at key generation ≥ `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE` (or on a ruleset-2 snapshot) and agrees with the pubkey match on every row that never rotated; `nodus_validator_v2_rotate_apply`'s caller is slice 6d's boundary swap step (§5b.9). Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf6_rotate.c` case C (EVM builds). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 6c".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_validator_v2_rotate_apply(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t validator_id[64], const uint8_t *old_pk, const uint8_t *new_pk)` (`nodus_witness_validator.h`) | **NEW.** The row switch (design §3 "Satır değişimi"). Inside the caller's transaction (autocommit → -1, nothing written); refuses before writing an all-zero validator_id or new_pk and new_pk == old_pk. Three UPDATEs, each exactly one row else -1: `validators SET pubkey = new WHERE pubkey_hash = id AND pubkey = old`; `v2_validator_roles SET pending = 2592 × 0x00, epoch = 0 WHERE validator_id = id AND pending = new`; `v2_consensus_keys SET role = 1 WHERE consensus_pk_hash = SHA3-512(new) AND validator_id = id AND role = 2`. The old key's role-1 row is kept (released by the 6d sweep at U+E — khan A2). The ONE writer of `validators.pubkey`. A wrong old key writes nothing; a later-step failure leaves earlier steps for the caller's rollback. 0 / -1. |
| `static int v2ep_member_in_snapshot(const dna_vset_snapshot_t *snap, const uint8_t *validator_id, const uint8_t pubkey[2592], const uint8_t *chain32, size_t *idx_out)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **NEW (static), replaces `v2ep_pubkey_in_snapshot`.** With `chain32` (caller at gen ≥ 4): ruleset 2 → by `entry.validator_id`; ruleset 1 → `dna_validator_id_migrated(chain32, entry.pubkey) == validator_id` (the econ v1-snapshot bridge). Without `chain32` (gens 1-3) → the old pubkey memcmp for any ruleset (byte-identical). 1 entry (*idx_out) / 0 not an entry / -1 fault (gen 4 without an id, derivation failure, a ruleset other than 1 / 2). |
| `static int v2ep_graduate(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** the R5-3 deferral uses `v2ep_member_in_snapshot` (gen 4: by validator_id; -1 → -2); the generation-4 write-back zeroes `pending_consensus_pubkey` and `consensus_key_effective_epoch`. `nodus_validator_v2_key_release` is unchanged — its second DELETE already takes the role-2 row and, before the sweep, the previous key's kept role-1 row (changed by slice 6e, §5b.10: role-2 rows only; the sweep releases the previous key). |
| `static int v2ep_rule_n(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h, uint32_t key_gen, const uint8_t chain_id[32])` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED signature** (+ `chain_id`, the boundary's derived V2 chain id). The bonded scan also reads `pubkey_hash`; the duty test uses `v2ep_member_in_snapshot` (gen 4: by validator_id; -1 → the boundary faults); `nodus_witness_v2_attendance_meets_bar` reads the DUTY ENTRY's pubkey (byte-identical below gen 4). The counter UPDATE stays keyed by the row's pubkey. Sole caller `nodus_witness_v2_epoch_boundary_apply`. |
| `int nodus_witness_vset_apply_boundary_flips(...)` (`nodus_witness_vset.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** pass 2 on a ruleset-2 snapshot is `WHERE pubkey_hash = entry.validator_id AND status = ELIGIBLE`; a ruleset-1 snapshot keeps `WHERE pubkey = entry.pubkey`. |
| `static int v2ec_member_load(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_econ.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** the ruleset-2 key check is `entry.pubkey ∈ {row.pubkey, row.pending}` (the pending arm only for a non-zero pending key); any other key → -2. Owner check unchanged. |
| `int nodus_rt_native_describe_leg(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** an applied ROTATE leg is described with `NODUS_RT_DESC_REC_NONE` (it was -1, which would fail the address index's block). Signature unchanged. |

### 5b.9 HF-6 slice 6d — the freeze's rotation budget, the swap at U, the sweep at U+E (design rev 6 §3 + khan A1 / A2 / A3; plan `tasks/hf6-slice6-plan.md` §1.2, §1.4, §1.8, §2 "6d")

Generation ≥ 4 only; each step reads its candidates first and returns before any other read when there are none (byte- and fault-identical to 6c on a chain with no pending row). No public builder signature changes. No fault-injection stage in 6d (the engine's mapper fails closed on an unnamed stage) — added by slice 6e (§5b.10); the sweep's candidates and `nodus_validator_v2_key_sweep_previous`'s signature also moved in 6e. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf6_rotate.c` cases C and D, `nodus/tests/test_cmt_app.c` `t_val_updates_rotation`. Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 6d".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `static int vset_rotation_pass(nodus_witness_t *w, nodus_committee_member_t *members, dna_vset_snapshot_t *snap)` (`nodus_witness_vset.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Called by `vset_build_snapshot` after the entry fill at key_gen ≥ 4 (so by `commit_next`, `preview_next`, `build_for_epoch`). Candidates: `v2_validator_roles` rows with a pending key and effective epoch ≤ F = snap->epoch − E, validator_id ASC; none ⇒ return 0. Else: counted powers of the provisional S(U) and of the stored S(F) (authority resolver; absent ⇒ -1; P = 0 / helper refusal ⇒ -1); walk accepts c iff `P − (R + p) > P * 2 / 3` in both sets (absent from S(U) ⇒ waits; absent from S(F) ⇒ p_F = 0; refusal ⇒ skip and continue); an accepted member takes its pending key and its entry is re-filled by `nodus_witness_vset_entry_from_member`. Writes nothing. 0 / -1. Helpers (static): `vset_rot_candidates`, `vset_rot_total`, `vset_rot_fits`. |
| `static int vset_build_snapshot(...)` / `int nodus_witness_vset_build_for_epoch(...)` / `int nodus_witness_vset_commit_next(...)` / `int nodus_witness_vset_preview_next(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signatures unchanged:** at key_gen ≥ 4 the rotation pass runs inside the one core; -1 now also covers its faults (pending row present only). |
| `static int v2ep_rotate_swap(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Boundary step 1d (after 1b/1b′/1c, before graduation). Pending rows validator_id ASC; none ⇒ 0 without reading S(h). S(h) ruleset 1 ⇒ 0. For each row whose S(h) entry (by validator_id) carries its pending key: `nodus_validator_v2_rotate_apply(w, id, row.pubkey, pending)`. 0 / -2. |
| `static int v2ep_rotate_sweep(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h, const uint8_t chain_id[32])` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Boundary step 3d (after the attendance digest and reset, before the flips). Candidates from `nodus_validator_v2_key_rotated_ids`; none ⇒ 0 without reading a snapshot. Swapped at h − E (S(h−E) seats it under the row's current key, `v2ep_member_in_snapshot`) ⇒ `nodus_validator_v2_key_sweep_previous` + `DELETE FROM v2_attendance WHERE voter_id = prev_hash[0..31]` (0 or 1 row); else S(h) must seat it under the current key (swapped at h, kept); any other shape or an UNSTAKED / unreadable row ⇒ -2. |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_key_rotated_ids(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t (**ids_out)[64], size_t *n_out)` (`nodus_witness_validator.h`) | **NEW.** Read-only: the validator_ids with more than one role-1 `v2_consensus_keys` row (`GROUP BY validator_id HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 ORDER BY validator_id ASC`); heap array (free()), NULL when none. 0 / -1 (SQLite error, malformed id, allocation). |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_key_sweep_previous(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t validator_id[64], const uint8_t *consensus_pubkey, uint8_t prev_hash_out[64])` (`nodus_witness_validator.h`) | **NEW.** Inside the caller's transaction (autocommit → -1, nothing written). The validator's role-1 rows must be exactly two with exactly one keyed by SHA3-512(`consensus_pubkey`); the other is deleted (one row, else -1) and its hash written to `prev_hash_out`. Role-2 rows untouched. 0 / -1. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_epoch_boundary_apply(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** steps 1d (swap) and 3d (sweep) at key generation ≥ 4. |

### 5b.10 HF-6 slice 6e — the p2p pending set; the rotation stages; the graduate's previous key (design rev 6 §3 + khan A2; plan `tasks/hf6-slice6-plan.md` §0.6 #14, §2 "6e", §5 B2)

Tests: `nodus/tests/test_witness_p2p.c` (2a''), `nodus/tests/test_hf6_readers.c` D (pin), `nodus/tests/test_hf6_rotate.c` cases D (F61 / F62) and E (EVM builds). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 6e".

A prepare / bind / step failure is logged (WARN) and adds nothing more. Empty below generation 4. |

Not listed in `tests/v2_genesis_fixture.h` `v2x_cmt_fail_class`. |

| `int nodus_validator_v2_key_release(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t validator_id[64], const uint8_t *consensus_pubkey)` (`nodus_witness_validator.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** the second DELETE is `WHERE validator_id = ? AND role = 2` — a previous key kept at role 1 (swapped at U, graduating at U+E) is left for the sweep of the same boundary. |
| `int nodus_validator_v2_key_rotated_ids(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t (**ids_out)[64], size_t *n_out)` (`nodus_witness_validator.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** `… GROUP BY validator_id HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 UNION SELECT k.validator_id FROM v2_consensus_keys k JOIN validators v ON v.pubkey_hash = k.validator_id WHERE k.role = 1 AND v.status = UNSTAKED ORDER BY 1 ASC` — also the graduates still holding a previous key. |

The previous key's row deleted (one row, else -1), its hash to `prev_hash_out`. Sole caller `v2ep_rotate_sweep`. 0 / -1. |
| `static int v2ep_rotate_sweep(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h, const uint8_t chain_id[32])` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** an UNSTAKED candidate is legal iff S(h−E) seats it under its current key (swapped at h−E, graduated at h) — released through `key_sweep_previous(…, graduated = 1, …)` with the same attendance DELETE; an UNSTAKED candidate not swapped at h−E ⇒ -2. The one release site of a previous key and its attendance row. |
| `static int v2ep_graduate(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour) through `key_release`:** a graduate's previous key (if any) survives step 2 at role 1 until step 3d of the same boundary. |

### 5b.11 HF-6 6f-1 client — `validator consent`, `v2-envelope stake2 | rotate` (design rev 6 §2, §6.1, §6.2, §9; decision `2026-10-03-role-stake-amounts.md` 1B; plan `tasks/hf6-slice6-plan.md` §2 "6f", §5 B6)

Tests: `nodus/tests/test_v2_stake_client.c` (EVM builds). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 6f-1 client". `nodus_v2_stake.c` calls no `dna_valbind_*` / `dna_validator_id_*` function (the web wallet's WASM module and native vector compile it without `valbind_wire.c`); it includes `dnac/valbind_wire.h` for the widths only.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `nodus_v2_stake_req_t` (`nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_stake.h`) | **CHANGED (appended fields):** `uint32_t generation` (0 in every older caller = the legacy layouts, byte-identical), `const uint8_t *consensus_pk` (gen-4 STAKE: the node key; ROTATE: the new key), `const uint8_t *consent_sig` (`DNA_VALBIND_SIG_LEN`), `uint64_t consent_expiry`, `const uint8_t *validator_id` (ROTATE). |
| `nodus_v2_stake_decoded_t` (`nodus_v2_stake.h`) | **CHANGED (appended fields):** `int gen4`, `owner_fp[64]`, `consensus_pk[2592]`, `consent_sig[DNA_VALBIND_SIG_LEN]`, `uint64_t consent_expiry`, `validator_id[64]` — all zero for every other layout. For a generation-4 layout `identity_pk` is the one signer of both legs (the owner). |
| `NODUS_V2_STAKE_OP_ROTATE` (= 10 = `DNA_SYSRULE_ROTATE_CONSENSUS_KEY`), `NODUS_V2_STAKE2_CALL_LEN` (7301u), `NODUS_V2_ROTATE_CALL_LEN` (7291u), `NODUS_V2_STAKE_ERR_GEN` (−46) | **NEW.** `ERR_GEN`: ROTATE below `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE`; DELEGATE / UNDELEGATE / UNSTAKE at or above it (their generation-4 layouts are not built) — until slice 7b, which builds them (§5b.16). |
| `int nodus_v2_stake_build(const nodus_v2_stake_req_t *req, nodus_v2_stake_built_t *out, nodus_v2_stake_err_t *err)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** at `generation >= NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE` op 1 lays out `owner_fp (= SHA3-512(pk)) ‖ consensus_pk ‖ consent_sig ‖ consent_expiry ‖ commission ‖ bond` (lock = bond; refuses commission > `DNAC_COMMISSION_BPS_MAX` → `ERR_COMMISSION`, bond outside 1..INT64_MAX → `ERR_BOND`, a missing `consensus_pk` / `consent_sig` → `ERR_ARG`) and op 10 lays out `validator_id ‖ new_pk ‖ consent_sig ‖ consent_expiry` (lock 0, fee-only; a missing input or an all-zero validator_id → `ERR_ARG`). Neither the consent signature nor its expiry window is checked here (the caller's). The read-back additionally compares every generation-4 field. Storage ops unchanged at every generation. |
| `int nodus_v2_stake_decode(const uint8_t *env, size_t env_len, nodus_v2_stake_decoded_t *out)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** also accepts op 1 with a 7301-byte call and op 10 (7291 bytes); identity = the leg-0 signer, both legs must carry it; a STAKE whose owner_fp ≠ SHA3-512(signer) → `ERR_DECODE`. |
| `NODUS_V2_CONSENT_MAGIC` (`"nodus-valbind-consent v1"`), `nodus_v2_consent_t` | **NEW.** The node-consent container: `op` (1 / 2), `chain32[32]`, `subject[64]`, `owner_fp[64]`, `consensus_pk[2592]`, `expiry`, `sig[DNA_VALBIND_SIG_LEN]`. A local text file, not a wire format, not signed. |
| `int nodus_v2_consent_text_encode(const nodus_v2_consent_t *c, char **text_out, size_t *len_out)` | **NEW.** Lines `nodus-valbind-consent v1`, `op <d>`, `chain_id <64 hex>`, `subject <128 hex>`, `owner <128 hex>`, `consensus_pk <5184 hex>`, `expiry <decimal>`, `sig <9254 hex>`, each `\n`-terminated, lowercase. Heap, NUL-terminated. Refuses op ∉ {1, 2} or a subject breaking the op rule (STAKE zero, ROTATE non-zero) → `ERR_ARG`; `ERR_ALLOC`. |
| `int nodus_v2_consent_text_parse(const char *text, size_t len, nodus_v2_consent_t *c)` | **NEW.** Exactly that format (fixed order, exact hex widths, lowercase only, canonical decimal without a leading zero or overflow, nothing after the last line) and the op / subject rule; else `ERR_DECODE` with `c` zeroed; NULL → `ERR_ARG`. Does not verify the signature. |
| `static int cmd_validator_consent(const char *server_ip, uint16_t server_port, int argc, char **argv, int cmd_start)` (`nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal); 6b-3 added a `storage` argument (§5b.14).** `nodus-cli -i <node dir> validator consent --op stake\|rotate --owner <hex128> [--subject <hex128>] --expiry <h> [--out <file>]`: chain id (`nodus_client_dnac_chain_id32`) and tip (`nodus_client_dnac_ruleset_info`) on one session as the node identity; refused unless `dna_valbind_expiry_ok(tip + 1, expiry)`; `dna_valbind_consent_msg` + `dna_valbind_consent_sign` with the `-i` key, verified; the container to `--out` or stdout. |
| `static int cli_consent_load(const char *path, nodus_v2_consent_t *c)`, `static int cli_consent_check(const nodus_v2_consent_t *c, uint8_t want_op, const uint8_t chain32[32], const uint8_t owner_fp[64], const uint8_t *validator_id, uint64_t tip)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Load + parse (≤ 16 384 bytes); re-check op, chain id, owner, ROTATE subject, the signature over the message rebuilt by `dna_valbind_consent_msg` (`dna_valbind_consent_verify`), and `dna_valbind_expiry_ok(tip + 1, expiry)`. |
| `static int cmd_v2_stake(...)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** verbs `stake2` (`--keys <owner> --consent <file> --bond <raw> [--commission <bps>]`) and `rotate` (`--keys <owner> --validator <validator_id hex128> --consent <file>`), both `(--dry-run \| --submit ip:port)`, refused below generation 4; `stake` / `delegate` / `undelegate` / `unstake` refused at generation ≥ 4 (since slice 7b only `stake` — §5b.16); `sreq.generation` = the selected generation; `stake2` prints `validator_id=` = `dna_validator_id_new(chain id, intent_id)`. |
