| `RTN_SYS_STREG2_CALL_LEN` (7299), `RTN_SYS_STEXIT2_CALL_LEN` (64), `RTN_STREG2_*_OFF`, `rtn_streg2_call_t`, `static int rtn_streg2_parse(const uint8_t *p, uint32_t len, rtn_streg2_call_t *c)`, `static int rtn_stexit2_parse(const uint8_t *p, uint32_t len, const uint8_t **node_fp_out)` (`nodus_witness_rt_native.c`) | **NEW.** REGISTER = owner_fp[64] ‖ node_pk[2592] ‖ consent_sig[4627] ‖ consent_expiry u64 ‖ bond u64; EXIT = node_fp[64]. Exact length only; both lengths join the "dominated by the generation-4 STAKE" capacity assert. |
| `RTN_SYS_STREG_CALL_LEN`, `RTN_SYS_STEXIT_CALL_LEN`, `rtn_streg_call_t`, `rtn_streg_parse` | **REMOVED** (the 2664 / 2592-byte node-key layouts; never accepted on a chain). |
| `RTN_STOR_PAYEE_OFF` → `RTN_STOR_OWNER_OFF` | **RENAMED**, same offset 2592: the `payee_fp` column (SQL name kept — the DDL is shape-checked on open) now holds the OWNER. The adapter's three uses follow; no byte change. |
| `static uint64_t rtn_storage_bond_at(const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx)` | **NEW.** THE ONE read of STORAGE_BOND: at 6b-1 `DNAC_HF6_STORAGE_BOND_DEFAULT`. **Slice 8a:** now `static int rtn_storage_bond_at(ctx, uint64_t *out)` returning the engine fact `ctx->storage_bond` (param 21), fail-closed — §5b.17. |
| `rtn_sys_call_identity` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** the storage ops have no pubkey identity any more (default −1 in every generation). |
| `rtn_sys_call_flow` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** ops 7 / 8 decode their 7299 / 64-byte layouts inside the generation-4 switch (REGISTER lock = bond, EXIT fee-only); the legacy arms are gone (unreachable — the ops exist only from generation 4). |
| `rtn_storage_read_plan` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** REGISTER `[STOR(SHA3-512(call.node_pk))] [STORCNT(1)]`, EXIT `[STOR(call bytes)]` — parsed directly, no `rtn_sys_call_identity`. |
| `rtn_storage_register_exec` | **CHANGED (rules):** shape → 7299 parse → `rtn_sys_owner_auth(call.owner_fp)` (kind 1 / 3) → bond == `rtn_storage_bond_at`, bond ≠ 0, bond ≤ INT64_MAX → `dna_valbind_expiry_ok` → consent rebuilt (op 3, zero subject, the call's owner_fp and node_pk, `ctx->chain_id`) and verified under node_pk → reads → RELEASED revive only when `x > 0 ∧ h > x ∧ ⌊(h−1)/E⌋ − ⌊(x−1)/E⌋ ≥ 2` (any owner — A2) → cap → sibling → record with owner_fp. |
| `rtn_storage_exit_exec` | **CHANGED (rules):** shape → 64 parse → read → row present, key == SHA3-512(row.node_pk) (else −2), `rtn_stor_rec_ok` → `rtn_sys_owner_auth(row owner)` (kind 1 / 3) → ACTIVE → sibling → SET EXITING. No node consent. |
| `rtn_desc_system` | **CHANGED:** ops 7 / 8 described inside the generation-4 switch through the v2 parsers (`REC_NONE`, unchanged); the legacy arms are gone. |
| `NODUS_V2_STREG_CALL_LEN` (7299u), `NODUS_V2_STEXIT_CALL_LEN` (64u) (`nodus_v2_stake.h`) | **CHANGED values** (were 2664u / 2592u). |
| `nodus_v2_stake_req_t.node_fp` | **NEW appended field** (`const uint8_t *`, STORAGE_EXIT's 64-byte node fingerprint). `dest_fp` is the legacy STAKE's only. |
| `nodus_v2_stake_decoded_t.node_fp[64]` | **NEW appended field.** REGISTER: SHA3-512(node_pk); EXIT: the call. |
| `NODUS_V2_STAKE_ERR_STORAGE_BOND` (−44) | **CHANGED meaning:** REGISTER bond outside [1, INT64_MAX] (the exact value is the chain's). |
| `NODUS_V2_STAKE_ERR_PAYEE` (−45) | **REMOVED** (value not reused). |
| `nodus_v2_stake_build` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** ops 7 / 8 only at `generation >= NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE` (else `ERR_GEN`); REGISTER needs `consensus_pk` (the node key) + `consent_sig` (`ERR_ARG`), owner_fp = SHA3-512(pk), lock = bond; EXIT needs `node_fp` (`ERR_ARG`), fee-only. The read-back compares the generation-4 fields. |
| `nodus_v2_stake_decode` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** op 7 (7299 B) and op 8 (64 B) decode as generation-4 layouts (identity = the leg-0 signer); a REGISTER whose owner_fp ≠ SHA3-512(signer) → `ERR_DECODE`. The v1 storage arms are gone. |
| `nodus_v2_consent_text_encode`, `nodus_v2_consent_text_parse` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** op 3 (STORAGE_REGISTER) accepted with a zero subject; op ∉ {1, 2, 3} or a broken op/subject rule refused as before. |
| `static int cmd_storage_tx_refused(const char *verb)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal), replaces `cmd_storage_tx`; REMOVED by 6b-3 (§5b.14).** `storage register | exit` printed why they were not built (owner-signed since HF-6) and returned 1 without contacting a node. |

### 5b.13 HF-6 slice 6b-2 — the storage settlement: pay the owner, F2 owner-OR-key, the three-state verdict, the v1-snapshot owner bridge (design rev 6 §11.6-§11.7, G12, khan A1 / A3 2026-10-12; Kurultay #16 item 8 + khan decision 2 "kural (a)"; plan `tasks/hf6-slice6b-plan.md` §1.5-§1.6, §2 "6b-2")

Tests: `nodus/tests/test_storage_b2.c` (+ `f2_verdict_pure`, `engine_owner_f2`; `engine_v2_power` + the A3 `seat_of` probe) — EVM builds. Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 6b-2". No byte, pin, leaf, KAT or wire change; no reporter code change (khan A3).

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `nodus_storage_verdict_t` { `NODUS_STORAGE_NO_EVIDENCE` = 0, `NODUS_STORAGE_OK` = 1, `NODUS_STORAGE_NOT_OK` = 2 } (`nodus_witness_v2_storage.h`) | **NEW.** The settlement's three states (design §11.6) — one enum so the floor is read one way. |
| `int nodus_storage_member_verdict(uint32_t n_seat, const uint64_t *power, const uint8_t (*seat_owner)[64], const uint8_t (*seat_key)[64], const uint8_t *reported, const uint8_t *bit, uint64_t p_total, const uint8_t member_owner[64], const uint8_t member_fp[64], nodus_storage_verdict_t *out, uint64_t *i_out, uint64_t *y_out)` | **NEW (pure).** Seat s excluded iff `seat_owner[s] == member_owner` OR `seat_key[s] == member_fp`; I = Σ power of reporting non-excluded seats, Y = the part with the member's bit; `2·I ≤ p_total` → NO_EVIDENCE, else `3·Y > 2·I` → OK, else NOT_OK (checked u64; the caller has checked F1). `i_out` / `y_out` optional. 0 / -1 (NULL, overflow). |
| `int nodus_witness_storage_seat_owners(nodus_witness_t *w, const dna_vset_snapshot_t *snap, const uint8_t chain_id[32], uint8_t (*owner_out)[64])` | **NEW.** owner(s) per seat: ruleset 2 → the entry's owner_fp (`w`, `chain_id` unread); ruleset 1 → the §7.5 bridge (`nodus_validator_v2_get(dna_validator_id_migrated(chain_id, pubkey))` → owner_fp); any other ruleset, an id that does not derive, a row absent or unreadable → -1 (khan A1: the settlement FAULTS), logged with the seat index. |
| `static int st_settle(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t B, const uint8_t chain_id[32], nodus_storage_boundary_t *out)` (`nodus_witness_v2_storage.c`) | **CHANGED signature** (+ `chain_id`, the boundary's — `nodus_witness_storage_boundary_apply` already received it; no caller outside this file). **CHANGED (rules):** F1 unchanged and still first; then the registry rows (moved up from after the verdicts), a duplicate seat key FAULT (was: only when the duplicated key matched a member), owner(s) once (skipped for an empty set), the verdict per member through `nodus_storage_member_verdict`; pay only OK members, to the row's `payee_fp` (the owner); W keeps every member's weight; fail_streak skipped for NO_EVIDENCE exactly as for grace (also at ≥ 3). |
| `nodus_storage_fail_streak_next` | **Documentation only:** not called for a NO_EVIDENCE member; `ok` = (verdict == OK). |
| `int nodus_witness_storage_boundary_apply(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** step 1 settles by the owner-OR-key rule and the three-state verdict; a ruleset-1 snapshot(H) whose seat has no migrated row is a FAULT (-2). |

### 5b.14 HF-6 slice 6b-3 — the storage owner CLI (design rev 6 §11.3-§11.5, §11.10, khan A2 / A4 2026-10-12; Kurultay #16 khan 1 / 3; plan `tasks/hf6-slice6b-plan.md` §2 "6b-3", §5 B5)

Harness: `nodus/tests/integration/stagef/tests/test_storage_archive.sh` (rewritten — README scenario row). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 6b-3". No client-library function changes (`nodus_v2_stake.c` unchanged since 6b-1).

|------|-------------|

| `static int cmd_v2_stake(const char *server_ip, uint16_t server_port, int argc, char **argv, int cmd_start)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** also serves `storage register --keys <owner> --consent <file>` and `storage exit --keys <owner> --node-fp <hex128>`, both `(--dry-run \| --submit ip:port)` (`argv[cmd_start]` = "storage"). Register: node fp = SHA3-512(consent.consensus_pk), bond = `DNAC_HF6_STORAGE_BOND_DEFAULT`, consent re-checked with op 3. Exit: `req.node_fp`, fee only. Below generation 4 refused with `storage registration exists only from generation 4` / `storage exit …` BEFORE the consent re-check; then `cli_storage_row_check`. Prints `storage <verb>: node <fp>`, `owner <fp>`, the bond and consequences before the ids; dry-run label `SYSTEM STORAGE_REGISTER` / `STORAGE_EXIT`; `ERR_STORAGE_BOND` mapped. |
| `static int cli_storage_row_check(nodus_client_t *client, int is_exit, const uint8_t node_fp[64], const uint8_t owner_fp[64])` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal).** The `dnac_storage_status` row on the builder's session. Register: refuses ACTIVE / EXITING, and RELEASED with x = 0 or committed + 1 < (⌊(x−1)/E⌋ + 2)·E + 1 (the re-registration rule, design §11.4; the first accepted height printed). Exit: refuses no row, not ACTIVE, or a row owner (`payee`) ≠ `owner_fp`. A failed query → warning, 0 (the chain decides). 0 build / −1 refused. |

| `static const char *cli_consent_op_name(uint8_t op)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal).** "a STAKE" / "a ROTATE" / "a STORAGE_REGISTER" / "an unknown op" for `cli_consent_check`'s op-mismatch message (which named op 3 "a ROTATE" before). |
| `static int cmd_storage_tx_refused(const char *verb)` | **REMOVED** (the 6b-1 stub). |

Node-local READS; nothing in consensus reads these replies. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_client_validator_list.c` (`test_v2_keys`, `test_v2_refusals`), `nodus/tests/test_storage_by_owner.c` (NEW). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 7a".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|

A node that predates the keys leaves `has_v2` 0 and both arrays zero. `dnac_validator_list_entry_t` (`dnac.h`) unchanged. |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_validator_list(nodus_client_t *client, int filter_status, int offset, int limit, nodus_dnac_validator_list_result_t *result_out)` (`nodus_client.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** the reply decoder (`dnac_validator_list_parse`) decodes the generation-4 per-entry `vid` / `owner` (bstr 64 each — `handle_dnac_validator_list_query`); a value that is not a 64-byte bstr, a key repeated in one entry, or one key without the other → `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` (result freed). |
| `nodus_dnac_storage_node_t { char node_fp[129]; nodus_dnac_storage_status_t st; }`, `nodus_dnac_storage_by_owner_t { uint64_t committed_height; uint64_t total; size_t count; nodus_dnac_storage_node_t *nodes; }` (`nodus.h`) | **NEW.** One `dnac_storage_by_owner` answer; `nodes` heap (`count` entries, NULL when 0). |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_storage_by_owner(nodus_client_t *client, const char *owner_hex, nodus_dnac_storage_by_owner_t *out)` (`nodus_client.c`) | **NEW.** `owner_hex` = 128 lowercase hex (checked before sending). Request `{"owner": tstr128}`. 0 (caller frees with `nodus_dnac_storage_by_owner_free`) / the node's `NODUS_ERR_*` (older node: "unknown DNAC method") / `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` (reply refused, incl. an entry whose `payee` ≠ `owner_hex`) / `NODUS_ERR_TIMEOUT` / −1 invalid args or transport. |
| `int nodus_dnac_storage_by_owner_decode(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, const char *owner_hex, nodus_dnac_storage_by_owner_t *out)` | **NEW.** Exported for tests. `ch`, `total`, `nodes` each exactly once (v3d_* discipline); `nodes` ≤ `NODUS_DNAC_STORAGE_SET_MAX` checked before allocation; \|nodes\| = min(total, 256); each entry = the `dnac_storage_status` rules plus a required `fp` (128 lowercase hex), found = true, `ch` = the reply's, `es` / `set` / `sc` = the first entry's; fp strictly ascending; `owner_hex` non-NULL ⇒ every `payee` equals it. 0 / −1 (`out` freed and zeroed). |
| `void nodus_dnac_storage_by_owner_free(nodus_dnac_storage_by_owner_t *r)` | **NEW.** Frees `nodes`, zeroes `*r`; NULL-safe. |
| `int nodus_dnac_storage_status_decode(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, nodus_dnac_storage_status_t *out)` | **CHANGED (internal), behaviour and signature unchanged:** its map walk moved into the file-static `st_status_map(cbor_decoder_t *d, size_t mc, nodus_dnac_storage_status_t *out, char *fp_out)` — `fp_out` NULL = the single reply (an `fp` key is skipped as unknown), non-NULL = a `dnac_storage_by_owner` entry (`fp` required). |
| `static void handle_dnac_storage_by_owner(nodus_witness_t *w, struct nodus_tcp_conn *conn, const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, uint32_t txn_id)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Method `dnac_storage_by_owner` (the generic `dnac_` route). ONE statement `SELECT node_fp … WHERE payee_fp = ?1 ORDER BY node_fp ASC` on `v2_storage_nodes`, stepped to its end: every row counted into `total`, the first 256 node_fps kept (count and list from the same read; fixed 256 × 64 B); storage_set(H) read once; each kept row through `st_status_fill` + `st_status_encode` (an entry = `fp` + the status keys); buffer 256 + n × 1280 B. Bad `owner` → `PROTOCOL_ERROR`; no version-3 chain → `NOT_FOUND`; store fault / a listed row unreadable / overflow → `INTERNAL_ERROR`. An owner without rows → total 0, empty list. |

| `static int cmd_storage_status_owner(const char *server_ip, uint16_t server_port, const char *owner_arg)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `nodus-cli storage status --owner <hex128>`: the generation line, `owner … N storage node(s) registered (every status)`, then `cli_storage_status_print` per node; a NOTE when total > listed. `cmd_storage_status` takes `--fp` or `--owner`, not both. |
| `test_storage_by_owner` (`nodus/tests/test_storage_by_owner.c`) | **NEW test.** The real dispatcher over a socketpair against planted rows, and the decoder's refusals over crafted replies (file header: what it proves / requires / leaves / how it can lie). |

Tests: `nodus/tests/test_v2_stake_client.c` (+ W1-W5, G1 extended) — EVM builds. Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 7b". No chain code, pin, wire-format or decision change: the chain side (`rtn_deleg_parse_v2` / `rtn_unstake_parse_v2` / `rtn_vupd_parse_v2`, `rtn_sys_call_flow`, the `rtn_*2_exec` arms) is sub-slice 3c's. A kind-3 (multisig) owner or delegator is not built.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_V2_STAKE_OP_VALIDATOR_UPDATE` (= 5 = `DNA_SYSRULE_VALIDATOR_UPDATE`) (`nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_stake.h`) | **NEW.** Built only at `generation >= NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE`; below it `ERR_GEN` (the legacy 2594-byte op-5 layout was never built by this module). `NODUS_V2_STAKE_ERR_OP` now means "not op 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 / 5 / 7 / 8 / 10". |
| `NODUS_V2_DELEG2_CALL_LEN` (136u), `NODUS_V2_UNSTAKE2_CALL_LEN` (64u), `NODUS_V2_VUPD2_CALL_LEN` (66u) (`nodus_v2_stake.h`) | **NEW.** `_Static_assert`ed to `64 + 64 + 8`, `64`, `64 + 2` and disjoint from the legacy 5192 / 2592. |
| `NODUS_V2_STAKE_ERR_GEN` (−46) | **CHANGED meaning:** ROTATE, VALIDATOR_UPDATE and the storage ops below generation 4. DELEGATE / UNDELEGATE / UNSTAKE are no longer refused at generation 4. |
| `nodus_v2_stake_req_t` | **No new field, no size change.** `validator_id` (appended in 6f-1 for ROTATE) also names the validator of a generation-4 DELEGATE / UNDELEGATE / UNSTAKE / VALIDATOR_UPDATE; `commission_bps` also carries the VALIDATOR_UPDATE commission; `validator_pk` is the legacy DELEGATE / UNDELEGATE target only. |
| `nodus_v2_stake_decoded_t` | **No new field, no size change.** For the generation-4 ops 2-5: `gen4` = 1, `identity_pk` = the one signer, `owner_fp` = the call's delegator_fp (DELEGATE / UNDELEGATE) or SHA3-512(signer) (UNSTAKE / VALIDATOR_UPDATE), `validator_id`, `amount` (0 for UNSTAKE / VALIDATOR_UPDATE), `commission_bps` (VALIDATOR_UPDATE); `validator_pk` zero. |
| `int nodus_v2_stake_build(const nodus_v2_stake_req_t *req, nodus_v2_stake_built_t *out, nodus_v2_stake_err_t *err)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** at `generation >= NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE` op 2 / 4 lay out `SHA3-512(pk) ‖ validator_id ‖ amount u64` (136 B; DELEGATE lock = amount, UNDELEGATE fee-only), op 3 `validator_id` (64 B, fee-only), op 5 `validator_id ‖ commission u16` (66 B, fee-only). Refusals: `validator_id` NULL or all zero → `ERR_ARG`; DELEGATE / UNDELEGATE amount ∉ [1, total supply] → `ERR_AMOUNT`; VALIDATOR_UPDATE commission > `DNAC_COMMISSION_BPS_MAX` → `ERR_COMMISSION`. A generation-4 DELEGATE funds from up to 15 inputs (the CORE leg is never name-gated at generation 4); the 14-input HF-8 cap stays on the legacy non-self DELEGATE. Generations 0-3: ops 2-4 unchanged byte for byte. |
| `int nodus_v2_stake_decode(const uint8_t *env, size_t env_len, nodus_v2_stake_decoded_t *out)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** op 2 / 4 with a 136-byte call, op 3 with a 64-byte call and op 5 (66 bytes only) decode as generation-4 layouts (identity = the leg-0 signer, both legs must carry it). `ERR_DECODE` for: a delegator_fp ≠ SHA3-512(signer), an all-zero validator_id, a VALIDATOR_UPDATE commission above `DNAC_COMMISSION_BPS_MAX`, an op-5 call of any other length. |
| `static int cli_validator_row(nodus_client_t *client, const uint8_t *pk, const uint8_t *vid, nodus_dnac_validator_list_entry_t *row)` (`nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED signature** (+ `vid`). `vid` non-NULL: match an entry with `has_v2` and that `validator_id` (at generation 4 `pubkey` is the consensus key); NULL: by `pk` as before. 1 / 0 / −1 unchanged. |
| `static int cmd_v2_stake(...)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** NEW verb `v2-envelope validator-update --keys <owner> --validator <validator_id hex128> --commission <bps>` (generation 4 only). `delegate` / `undelegate` take `--validator <validator_id hex128>` at generation 4 and `<hex5184 pubkey>` below; `unstake` takes `--validator <validator_id hex128>` at generation 4 (required) and none below; the wrong form for the node's generation is refused naming the generation. At generation 4 `unstake` / `validator-update` read the row by validator_id and refuse a status the chain refuses or an owner ≠ the `--keys` identity. The legacy `stake` stays refused at generation 4. `sreq.validator_id` is set for the generation-4 ops 2-5, `sreq.validator_pk` only below. |

### 5b.17 HF-6 slice 8a — chain_config params 19 STAKE_MIN / 20 POWER_CAP_BPS / 21 STORAGE_BOND; the three compiled seams become chain reads; `dnac_fee_info` reports them (design rev 6 §1.5, §5, §8, §11.3; Kurultay #14 K5 / K9, Kurultay #16 khan 3 and "4 ok"; plan `tasks/hf6-slice8-plan.md` §2 "8a", B4 / B5 / B6 / B10)

Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 8a". No descriptor, pin, pins header, D-literal or wire-format change; param 18 stays unassigned (8c). With no 19 / 20 / 21 row every reader answers the compiled default, so a chain without the votes is byte-identical.

|------|-------------|
| `DNAC_CFG_STAKE_MIN` (19), `DNAC_CFG_POWER_CAP_BPS` (20), `DNAC_CFG_STORAGE_BOND` (21); `DNAC_CFG_PARAM_MAX_ID` = 21 (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`) | **NEW.** Id 18 is NOT assigned (HF-6 slice 8c, the generation-4 vote). `chain_config_cache` / `_count` grow to 22 slots through the enum (`nodus_witness.h`). |
| `static inline bool dnac_cfg_param_read_by_consensus(uint8_t param_id)` | **CHANGED (list):** + 19, 20, 21 (18 stays off — refused like any unknown id by the witness scalar rule and the `verify.c` mirror). |
| `DNAC_HF6_STAKE_MIN_MIN` (10^13 raw = 100k NODUS) | **NEW.** Param 19's lower end (K9); the upper end is `INT64_MAX`. `_Static_assert`s: == 10^13, 0 < MIN ≤ `DNAC_HF6_STAKE_MIN_DEFAULT` ≤ INT64_MAX (moved here from `rt_native.c`'s inline `100000ULL * 100000000ULL`). |
| `DNAC_CFG_MIN_POWER_CAP_BPS` (500u), `DNAC_CFG_MAX_POWER_CAP_BPS` (3000u) | **NEW.** Param 20's range for the client mirror (which does not include `power_cap.h`); `_Static_assert`ed equal to `DNA_POWER_CAP_BPS_MIN` / `_MAX` in `nodus_witness_vset.c` and `nodus_witness_chain_config.c`. |
| `DNAC_HF6_STAKE_MIN_DEFAULT`, `DNAC_HF6_STORAGE_BOND_DEFAULT` / `_MIN` / `_MAX`, `DNAC_HF6_POWER_CAP_BPS_DEFAULT` | **CHANGED (role):** now the no-row values (and, for STORAGE_BOND, the scalar range) of params 19 / 21 / 20. Values unchanged. |
| `int nodus_chain_config_scalar_rules(uint8_t param_id, uint64_t new_value, uint64_t signed_at_block, uint64_t valid_before_block, uint64_t effective_block_height, uint64_t proposal_nonce)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** 19 ∈ [10^13, INT64_MAX] — the case bounds `new_value ≤ INT64_MAX` ITSELF (the int64 tail bounds the nonce and the three heights only; a 2^63 value would be stored negative and fault every node's reader, decision `2026-09-30-chain-config-int64-bounds.md`); 20 ∈ [500, 3000]; 21 ∈ [10^13, 10^14]. `CC_PARAM_MAX_ID` 17 → 21. |
| `uint64_t nodus_chain_config_grace_for_param(uint8_t param_id)` | **CHANGED:** 19, 20, 21 ERGONOMIC, each its own case. |
| `int nodus_chain_config_stateful_rules_ex(uint8_t param_id, uint64_t effective_block_height, const nodus_cc_state_facts_t *facts)` (and the 5-argument form) | **CHANGED:** 19-21 refused unless `judging_generation >= NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE` (4); no single-use rule; no other fact read. |
| `nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t.stake_min`, `.storage_bond` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_runtime.h`) | **NEW fields.** `uint64_t`: params 19 / 21 at `global_height` (row or compiled default), filled by the engine on EVERY ctx it builds (`exec_one_env`, `env_authorize_legs`), UNMETERED. 0 is never engine-filled: a hand-built ctx must set both. |
| `static int env_stake_min(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t height, uint64_t *out, char *reason, size_t reason_size)`, `static int env_storage_bond(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **NEW (internal).** The `env_name_prices` shape: one `nodus_chain_config_get_u64` at `height` with the compiled default; unreadable → -2 FAULT; a stored value outside [10^13, INT64_MAX] (19) / [10^13, 10^14] (21) → -2 FAULT ("… reads N, a value no committed row can hold"). Read once per item at both ctx sites. |
| `static int rtn_stake_min_at(const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx, uint64_t *out)`, `static int rtn_storage_bond_at(const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx, uint64_t *out)` (`nodus_witness_rt_native.c`) | **CHANGED signature** (were `static uint64_t …(ctx)` returning the compiled default). Return the engine fact; a ctx carrying 0 → -2 (node fault, never "no floor"). `rtn_stake2_exec` / `rtn_storage_register_exec` return -2 on it, -1 on `bond < floor` / `bond != STORAGE_BOND` as before. |
| `int nodus_witness_power_cap_bps_for_epoch(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t epoch_start, uint32_t *ratio_out)` (`nodus_witness_vset.h`) | **CHANGED (body):** `nodus_chain_config_get_u64(w, 20, epoch_start, 3000)`; a read fault → -1; a stored value outside [500, 3000] (checked on the u64 before narrowing) → -1; `*ratio_out` written only on 0. A row effective at `epoch_start + 1` does not count for that snapshot. `_Static_assert(DNAC_CHAIN_CONFIG_GRACE_ERGONOMIC_BLOCKS >= DNAC_EPOCH_LENGTH)` beside it (the frozen-ratio premise). |
| `static void handle_dnac_fee_info(...)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** 10 keys — + `stake_min`, `storage_bond`, `power_cap_bps`: params 19 / 21 / 20 active at tip + 1, compiled defaults with no row; an unreadable or out-of-range value → `INTERNAL_ERROR`, never a value. |
| `nodus_dnac_fee_info_t.stake_min`, `.storage_bond`, `.power_cap_bps` (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus_types.h`) | **NEW fields, appended at the END.** 0 = an older server that does not send the key. |

One addition: a reply the CBOR decoder cannot walk → -1 (was: 0 with partially zeroed fields). |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_fee_info(nodus_client_t *client, nodus_dnac_fee_info_t *result_out)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** decodes the three HF-6 keys; a malformed reply → `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR`. |
| `static int cmd_v2_stake(...)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** `storage register` builds the bond from the node's `fee_info.storage_bond` (the compiled default when the server predates the key); the `stake2` below-floor warning compares with `fee_info.stake_min` (same fallback). `chain-config propose` names `STAKE_MIN` / `POWER_CAP_BPS` / `STORAGE_BOND` (upper and lower case) with their ranges in the usage text. |

### 5b.18 HF-6 slice 8b — the generation-4 vote value D4: constructor, constants, literal, selfcheck row (design rev 6 §8.1, crypto gate APPROVED 2026-10-09, 296 bytes khan 2026-10-13; decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-09-kurultay-16-hf6-storage-summary.md`; plan `tasks/hf6-slice8-plan.md` §2 "8b")

Inert on chain: no consumer reads the literal outside the selfcheck until slice 8c (param 18 `RULESET_GEN_ROLES`, the edge and the storage gate on 18 / D4). The value comes from the independent oracle `shared/dnac/tests/hf6_vote_oracle.py` (`hf6_vote_oracle_out.txt`), never from this encoder. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf4_table.c` section 6'' (KAT, refusals, sensitivity), `nodus/tests/test_v2_native.c` K7 (the K8 literal). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 slice 8b".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int dna_rolegen_vote_digest(uint32_t generation, uint32_t base_generation, const uint8_t sys_ruleset_hash[DNA_DOM_HASH_LEN], const uint8_t core_ruleset_hash[DNA_DOM_HASH_LEN], uint32_t spec_version, const uint8_t foundation_owner[DNA_ROLEGEN_OWNER_LEN], const uint64_t *consts, uint32_t n_consts, uint64_t *out)` (`shared/dnac/domain_wire.h`) | **NEW.** `*out` = first 8 bytes BE, top bit cleared, of SHA3-512(tag ‖ generation u32 BE ‖ base u32 BE ‖ sys[64] ‖ core[64] ‖ spec u32 BE ‖ owner[64] ‖ n_consts u32 BE ‖ n × u64 BE) — offsets 0 / 16 / 20 / 24 / 88 / 152 / 156 / 220 / 224; 296 bytes for n = 9. Refuses (-1, `*out` untouched): NULL hash / owner / out, `n_consts > 64`, NULL consts with n > 0, a preimage of any length other than `DNA_ROLEGEN_PREIMAGE_LEN(n)`, hash backend failure. The `dna_evm_activation_digest` body with the EVM ruleset / manifest slots replaced by the owner. Compiled into libnodus and libdna (`domain_wire.c`); libdna has no caller. |
| `DNA_ROLEGEN_TAG` ("NDS.ROLEGEN.v1", zero-padded to 16), `DNA_ROLEGEN_OWNER_LEN` (64), `DNA_ROLEGEN_MAX_CONSTS` (64u), `DNA_ROLEGEN_PREIMAGE_LEN(n)` (224 + 8·n) | **NEW** (`shared/dnac/domain_wire.h`). Tag collision scan "NDS.ROLEGEN" → none (design §8.1). `_Static_assert(DNA_ROLEGEN_PREIMAGE_LEN(9) == 296)` in the constructor. |
| `DNAC_HF6_SWITCH_SPEC_VERSION` (1u) (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`) | **NEW.** The 3 → 4 phase code's version (registry switch + §7 migration); hashed into D4; bumps if that phase changes. The global `DNAC_RULESET_SWITCH_SPEC_VERSION` (D2's) is unchanged. |
| `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROLES_D` (= `0x4dc61072d5982acdULL` = 5604184871704996557, owner K8 — **superseded by §5b.25: `0x5922111a3501f58bULL`, then by §5b.26: `0x642af9b4bf735409ULL`**) (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`) | **NEW.** D4 — the only legal value of the generation-4 vote (param 18 in 8c). `#ifndef`-guarded: a TEST build that overrides `DNAC_HF6_FOUNDATION_OWNER` must define it too (`stagef_hf6_owner.sh` prints both; the harness owner `2cca88c4…ee196b93` gives `0x4fc24934a9155c64ULL`); one without the other refuses to start (selfcheck). `_Static_assert(≤ INT64_MAX)`. Production defines neither. |
| `static const uint64_t GEN4_VOTE_CONSTS[9]` (`nodus_witness_runtime.c`, `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED`) | **NEW (internal).** §8.1 order: `DNAC_HF6_STAKE_MIN_DEFAULT`, `DNAC_HF6_STAKE_MIN_MIN`, `DNAC_HF6_POWER_CAP_BPS_DEFAULT`, `DNA_POWER_CAP_BPS_MIN`, `DNA_POWER_CAP_BPS_MAX`, `DNAC_HF6_STORAGE_BOND_DEFAULT`, `DNAC_HF6_STORAGE_BOND_MIN`, `DNAC_HF6_STORAGE_BOND_MAX`, `DNA_VALBIND_MAX_EXPIRY_BLOCKS`; `_Static_assert` count == 9. |
| `int nodus_runtime_gen4_vote_digest(uint64_t *d_out)` (`nodus_witness_runtime.h`, `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED`) | **NEW.** `dna_rolegen_vote_digest(NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE (4), NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE_BASE (3), generation-4 SYSTEM / CORE `ruleset_hash` from the compiled table, DNAC_HF6_SWITCH_SPEC_VERSION, the compiled DNAC_HF6_FOUNDATION_OWNER, GEN4_VOTE_CONSTS, 9)`. 0 / -1 (NULL, a generation-4 entry missing, digest failure). Only caller: the selfcheck. |
| `int nodus_witness_runtime_selfcheck(void)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** EVM-enabled builds also re-derive D4 through `nodus_runtime_gen4_vote_digest` and refuse (-1, "the compiled RULESET_GEN_ROLES literal … does not re-derive") when it differs from `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROLES_D`. The storage RSGEN row (`DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_STORAGE_D`) stands until 8c (deleted there, §5b.19). |

### 5b.19 HF-6 slice 8c — the flip: param 18 `RULESET_GEN_ROLES` = D4 triggers generation 4; param 16 retired; the storage gate reads 18 (design rev 6 §8, §8.1, khan 2026-10-13; decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-09-kurultay-16-hf6-storage-summary.md` item 1; plan `tasks/hf6-slice8-plan.md` §2 "8c")

Branch only — not versioned, not voted. Byte-identical for generations 1-3 and for a chain with no param-16 / param-18 row. Mixed-fleet behaviour (an old binary refuses a param-18 proposal and diverges at the block that commits it; it accepts a param-16 proposal a new binary refuses; a committed param-16 row blocks every param-18 vote) is closed by procedure, `nodus/docs/DEPLOY_RUNBOOK.md` §2.2 HF-6 row. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf4_params.c` (8c sections), `dnac/tests/test_chain_config_verify.c` (5a'''), `test_v2_gas_price.c`, the nine engine fixtures that now seed param 18 = D4, `explorer/tests/test_explorer.c`.

### 5b.21 HF-6 — the key-reservation self-check, log-only (design rev 6 §3 khan A2 / A3, §4 G7; branch `hf6`, not versioned)

`v2_consensus_keys` is in no root. Every generation-4 epoch boundary now re-derives it from rooted state and compares row for row; a difference is LOGGED, never returned, never written, never a block decision. No consensus byte changes. Tests: `test_hf6_rotate` section K (every gen-4 boundary of C / D / E / F through `apply_both`; rolled-back corruption probes D4k, C6k, F2k). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 — the key-reservation self-check".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_witness_v2_keyres_diff(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h, const uint8_t chain_id[32], size_t *n_diff_out)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.h`) | **NEW, exported for tests.** Read-only, no transaction of its own. Derived set: role 1 = SHA3-512(pubkey) of every v2 row (`nodus_validator_v2_scan`) not UNSTAKED; role 2 = SHA3-512 of every non-zero `pending_consensus_pubkey`; role 1 = for every entry of a ruleset-2 S(h) whose key differs from the same validator_id's entry in S(h − E) (ruleset-1 S(h − E): `dna_validator_id_migrated` bridge), SHA3-512 of the S(h − E) key. No tolerance since khan A8 (§5b.22): every table row not derived is a difference (until A8 a validator with no seat in S(h − E) could hold ONE "unverified" extra role-1 row). Differences: missing, extra, another validator_id, another role, a key derived twice, a malformed row. `h` = the last boundary applied (multiple of E, ≥ E). 0 compared (`*n_diff_out` set) / 1 generation < 4 / -1 an input unreadable or a bad argument (`*n_diff_out` 0). |
| `static int v2ep_keyres_compare(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h, const uint8_t chain_id[32], v2ep_kr_report_t *rep)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **NEW (internal).** The core both of the above and of the wrapper; fills the totals (derived, table rows, differences — the `unverified` total was removed by khan A8, §5b.22) and the first 4 differences (kind, 8-byte key-hash and validator_id prefixes, expected / found role). |
| `static void v2ep_keyres_selfcheck(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h, uint64_t at, const uint8_t chain_id[32])` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED signature (§5b.22): + `at`**, the boundary being applied (log text only: `boundary <at> (start|end)`, "derived as of <h>"). Two calls since the A8 follow-up: step 0 (`h` = H − E, `at` = H; generation ≥ 4 and H ≥ 2E) and step 7 (`h` = `at` = H). **NEW (internal).** Boundary step 7 — after the balance copy (step 6), generation ≥ 4 only. Differences → one `QGP_LOG_ERROR` per reported difference + a summary line; no difference → `QGP_LOG_DEBUG`; the core unable to read → `QGP_LOG_WARN`. Returns nothing; `nodus_witness_v2_epoch_boundary_apply`'s result and writes are unchanged. |

### 5b.22 HF-6 khan A8 — the previous key of a validator S(F) does not seat is released in the swap at U (design rev 6 §3 "(A8)", khan 2026-10-13; branch `hf6-a8`, not versioned)

A rotation khan A3 accepts for a validator with no seat in S(F) = S(U − E) no longer keeps the old key reserved until U + E: the swap at U deletes its role-1 row. A validator S(F) seats is unchanged (khan A2: kept to U + E). The previous voter's attendance row of an A8 release is deleted at step 3d of the SAME boundary (after the digest of (U − E, U]), 0 or 1 row. `v2_consensus_keys` is now fully derivable; the self-check lost its A3 tolerance (§5b.21). Consensus-visible: a released key can be STAKEd again from U + 1 (it was refused until U + E); `v2_consensus_keys` is in no root; the attendance digest at U + E no longer folds that voter's (zeroed) row. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf6_rotate.c` C6a / C6 / C6k / C6s / C7c / C8, D4s / D5s (EVM builds). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 khan A8".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_validator_v2_key_release_previous(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t validator_id[64], const uint8_t *previous_pubkey, uint8_t prev_hash_out[64])` (`nodus_witness_validator.h`) | **NEW.** Inside the caller's transaction (autocommit → -1, nothing written). `DELETE FROM v2_consensus_keys WHERE consensus_pk_hash = SHA3-512(previous_pubkey) AND validator_id = ? AND role = 1` — exactly one row, else -1. The hash to `prev_hash_out`. Role-2 rows and the current key's row untouched. Sole caller `v2ep_rotate_swap`. 0 / -1. |
| `static int v2_key_delete_role1(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t kh[64], const uint8_t validator_id[64], const char *what)` (`nodus_witness_validator.c`) | **NEW (internal).** The one exact-row role-1 DELETE (changes == 1 else -1, logged under `what`), shared by `nodus_validator_v2_key_release` step 1, `nodus_validator_v2_key_sweep_previous` step 2 and `nodus_validator_v2_key_release_previous`. Behaviour of the two existing callers unchanged (their error log text now names the caller through `what`). |
| `static int v2ep_rotate_swap(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h, const uint8_t chain_id[32], v2ep_a8_rel_t *rel)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED signature** (+ `chain_id`, `rel`). After each `nodus_validator_v2_rotate_apply`: S(h − E) read once (at the first swap only); seat by `v2ep_member_in_snapshot` (ruleset 2: validator_id; ruleset 1: the migrated-id bridge — the mapping of `vset_rotation_pass` / `vset_rot_carried`). Seated ⇒ nothing more (A2). Not seated ⇒ `nodus_validator_v2_key_release_previous(w, id, old key, prev)` and `prev[0..31]` appended to `rel`. S(h − E) unreadable (rc -1) OR ABSENT (rc 1 — fail closed, operator 2026-10-13; the first A8 commit treated it as "not seated"), a mapping fault, a failed release, `rel` full (> `DNA_MAX_ACTIVE_VALIDATORS`) ⇒ -2. |
| `v2ep_a8_rel_t` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `{ uint8_t voter[DNA_MAX_ACTIVE_VALIDATORS][32]; size_t n; }` — on `nodus_witness_v2_epoch_boundary_apply`'s stack (4 KiB, no heap), filled at 1d, read at 3d. |
| `static int v2ep_rotate_sweep(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t h, const uint8_t chain_id[32], const v2ep_a8_rel_t *rel)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **CHANGED signature** (+ `rel`). FIRST deletes the attendance row of every `rel` voter (0 or 1 row each, in `rel` order), then the A2 sweep. Since A8 its candidates are exactly the swapped validators S(h − 2E) seated. **A8 follow-up (operator 2026-10-13):** a candidate classified "swapped at U = h − E" must be seated in S(U − E) (read once, by id / ruleset-1 bridge) under a key other than its current key, and the row `key_sweep_previous` deletes must be SHA3-512 of that key; else -2 (also S(U − E) absent / unreadable, U < E) — a stray role-1 row in the two-row shape is never released as a "previous key". |
| `nodus_witness_v2_epoch_boundary_apply` step 0 | **NEW (behaviour, log-only):** at generation ≥ 4 and H ≥ 2E, before step 1, `v2ep_keyres_selfcheck(w, H − E, H, chain_id)` — the table as STAKE / ROTATE left it between boundaries against the derivation of H − E, before the swap and the sweep write. Outcome ignored. |
| `static int v2ep_attendance_drop(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t *voter)` (`nodus_witness_v2_epoch.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `DELETE FROM v2_attendance WHERE voter_id = ?` — 0 or 1 row, else -2. Used by both arms of the sweep (the former inline DELETE). |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_epoch_boundary_apply(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** step 1d may delete role-1 rows (A8); step 3d deletes the A8 voters' attendance rows. Fault stages unchanged (F61 after 1d, F62 after 3d). |
| `nodus_validator_v2_rotate_apply`, `nodus_validator_v2_key_release`, `nodus_validator_v2_key_sweep_previous`, `nodus_validator_v2_key_rotated_ids` | **Unchanged behaviour** (contract comments updated: the old key's row is the caller's — A8 at U or A2 at U + E). |

### 5b.23 HF-6 — a multisig (kind-3) OWNER for the owner operations: builder, v2 co-sign files, CLI (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-09-29-general-multisig.md`, addendum "çok bacaklı ortak imza dosyası v2", operator 2026-10-13; design rev 6 §1.3; branch `hf6-msig`, not versioned)

No consensus change: the chain already accepts a kind-3 owner at generation 4 (`rtn_sys_owner_auth`, the generation-4 SYSTEM allowlist {1,2,3}). The kind-1 builder path and the v1 multisig files / functions are unchanged. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf6_msig_owner.c` (NEW, EVM builds). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 multisig owner".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `nodus_v2_stake_req_t.msig_desc` (`const uint8_t *`), `.msig_desc_len` (`size_t`), `.msig_signers` (`uint32_t`) (`nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_stake.h`) | **NEW appended fields** (zero in every older caller = kind 1, unchanged). Non-NULL `msig_desc`: the owner / delegator / change owner is SHA3-512(descriptor); `pk` / `sk` unused (may be NULL); K = `msig_signers`. |
| `nodus_v2_stake_decoded_t.auth_kind` (`uint8_t`), `.msig_signers` (`uint32_t`), `.msig_m`, `.msig_n` (`uint8_t`), `.msig_addr[64]` | **NEW appended fields.** `auth_kind` 1 or 3 (both legs the same); kind 3: K, the descriptor header's M / N, its address (= `owner_fp`); `identity_pk` is then all zero. |
| `nodus_v2_stake_built_t.auth_kind` (`uint8_t`), `.leg_digest[2][64]` | **NEW appended fields.** Kind 3: the pass-1 leg auth digests of leg 0 (SYSTEM) and leg 1 (CORE SYSFUND); `wire_id` then all zero (the unsigned envelope's is not final). |
| `int nodus_v2_stake_build(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** with `msig_desc` only the generation-4 layouts (else `ERR_GEN`); the descriptor length must be `DNA_MSIG_DESC_LEN(N)`, 2 ≤ N ≤ 7, 1 ≤ M ≤ N from its header and M ≤ K ≤ N (else `ERR_ARG`; tag and key order are not checked here — the file links no `msig_wire.c`, the web wallet's native vector compiles it alone); both legs auth_kind 3 = K zero slots ‖ 1 ‖ dlen u16 BE ‖ descriptor; encodes + preflights once, signs NOTHING. |
| `int nodus_v2_stake_decode(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** accepts auth_kind 3 on both legs for the generation-4 layouts only — one descriptor per leg, the same K, descriptor bytes and slot keys on both legs (signatures may differ; all-zero slots accepted); identity = the address; a STAKE / STORAGE_REGISTER owner_fp or DELEGATE / UNDELEGATE delegator_fp ≠ the address → `ERR_DECODE`; a legacy layout under kind 3 → `ERR_DECODE`. |
| `NODUS_V2_MSIG_EXPORT2_MAGIC` ("nodus-msig-export v2"), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_SIG2_MAGIC` ("nodus-msig-sig v2"), `NODUS_V2_MSIG2_MIN_LEGS` (2), `NODUS_V2_MSIG2_MAX_LEGS` (`DNA_ENV_MAX_LEGS`), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_SIG2_TEXT_MAX`, `NODUS_V2_MSIG_EXPORT2_TEXT_MAX` (`nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_msig.h`) | **NEW.** The v2 text formats (decision addendum): export = magic / `chain_id` / `tip` / `signers` / `digests <k>` / k × `digest <leg_index> <128 hex>` (ascending) / `envelope <hex>`; signature = magic / `pubkey <5184 hex>` / k × (`digest <i> <hex>` + `sig <i> <9254 hex>`). Read strictly in line order, final newline, nothing else. |
| `nodus_v2_msig_export2_t`, `nodus_v2_msig_sig2_t`, `nodus_v2_msig_review2_t` | **NEW types.** The parsed v2 export (env heap), one key's signature over every digest (~300 KB — heap it), what a co-signer is shown (address, M/N/K, tip, expiry, fee, digests, intent_id, and `op` = the `nodus_v2_stake_decoded_t` of the owner operation). |
| `int nodus_v2_msig_export2_encode(const nodus_v2_msig_export2_t *x, char **text_out, size_t *len_out)` / `int nodus_v2_msig_export2_parse(const char *text, size_t len, nodus_v2_msig_export2_t *x)` / `void nodus_v2_msig_export2_free(nodus_v2_msig_export2_t *x)` | **NEW.** k ∈ [2, `DNA_ENV_MAX_LEGS`], indices strictly ascending, K ∈ [1, 15]. `OK` / `_ERR_ARG` / `_ERR_ALLOC` / `NODUS_V2_MSIG_ERR_FORMAT`. |
| `int nodus_v2_msig_sig2_encode(const nodus_v2_msig_sig2_t *s, char **text_out, size_t *len_out)` / `int nodus_v2_msig_sig2_parse(const char *text, size_t len, nodus_v2_msig_sig2_t *s)` | **NEW.** A pair's sig line must carry its digest line's leg index; fewer than 2 pairs → `ERR_FORMAT`. |

| `int nodus_v2_msig_sig2_check(const nodus_v2_msig_export2_t *x, const nodus_v2_msig_leg_t *leg, const nodus_v2_msig_sig2_t *s)` | **NEW.** All or nothing: exactly the export's indices and digests (`ERR_SIG_DIGEST`), member (`ERR_NOT_MEMBER`), every signature verifies (`ERR_SIG`). |

Does NOT run the chain's auth hook (nodus-cli does, per leg). |

[--signers <K>] --export <file> [--submit ip:port]` (no `--dry-run`): the owner is the address (consent owner, validator / storage row owner compared with it), coins = the `--in` list, `--keys` = the session only; writes the v2 export. `validator consent` / `storage consent` take `--msig <descriptor>` (exactly one of `--owner` / `--msig`). Messages name "the --keys identity, or the --msig address". |

### 5b.24 HF-6 D1 — key rotation REMOVED; `v2_consensus_keys` permanent; the pending fields always zero (khan decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-13-kurultay-18-summary.md` items 1 and 4; design §3 box "KALDIRILDI — han kararı 2026-10-13"; branch `hf6-d`, not versioned)

Supersedes the rotation parts of §5b.6-§5b.10, §5b.21 and §5b.22 (slices 6a-6e, the self-check's rotation derivation, khan A8). The client ROTATE builder and CLI verbs (§5b.11) were removed by §5b.25 (D2). No version bump and no pin change in this package — §5b.25 did both. Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf6_rotate.c` (rewritten), `test_hf6_stake.c` B5/B6, `test_hf6_migrate.c` A1, `test_v2_valrow.c`, `test_witness_p2p.c` (2a''), `test_hf6_msig_owner.c` O2, `test_v2_stake_client.c` R1, `test_hf4_table.c` 3b, `test_hf6_readers.c` (inventory). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-6 D1"; runbook §2.7.

| `nodus_v2_epoch_result_t.n_grad_utxos`, `nodus_witness_treasury_graduation_refund`, `nodus_witness_treasury_credit` | **REMOVED** — see §2 / §3. |
| genesis validator destination (`gen_plan_build`, `nodus_witness_v2_gen.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** `unstake_destination_fp` is checked by SHAPE only (the graduation predicate); the rule "fp == SHA3-512(unstake_destination_pubkey)" is REMOVED — a genesis seat pays the Foundation multisig address, which no single key derives. `unstake_destination_pubkey` MUST be all zero on a genesis row (ONAY 2 item 2 — refused otherwise). |
| `NODUS_V2_GEN_CONFIG_VERSION_V5` (5u), `NODUS_V2_GEN_MAX_GENOUTS` (64), `nodus_v2_gen_output_t { uint8_t owner[64]; uint64_t amount; }`, `nodus_v2_gen_config_t.n_genesis_outputs` / `.genesis_outputs[64]` (`nodus_witness_v2_gen.h`) | **NEW (ONAY 2).** The genesis-output section: `u32 count ‖ count × (owner[64] ‖ amount u64 BE)` after the W-C tail, document order. `NODUS_V2_GEN_CONFIG_VERSION_V4` is RETIRED (every path refuses 4); `_v3_defaults` writes 5. Rule P.2 counts Σ outputs; `total_claimable` excludes them. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_gen_output_nullifier(const uint8_t source_commit[64], uint32_t index, uint8_t out[64])` | **NEW (ONAY 2).** `SHA3-512("NDS.GENOUT.v1" 16 B ‖ source_commit ‖ index u32 BE)` (84-byte preimage). 0 / -1. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_gen_seed_outputs(nodus_witness_t *w, const nodus_v2_gen_config_t *cfg, const uint8_t source_commit[64])` | **NEW (ONAY 2).** The ONE genesis-output writer: refuses a non-empty `utxo_set`, inserts each output (nullifier = tx_hash = the identity above, owner hex, amount, index 0, height 0, unlock 0, domain CORE, native), then requires exactly `n_genesis_outputs` rows summing to Σ outputs. Called by `gen_seed_state` (derivation, before the root) and `nodus_witness_v2_bundle_apply` (joiner, from the carried document). 0 / -1. |
| `nodus_v2_gen_config_parse_file` (`nodus/tools/nodus_v2_gen_config.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour, ONAY 2):** `config_version = 5` only; new `[genesis_output]` block (`owner` 128 hex, `amount`), 0..64, file order. Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_bundle_apply(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour, ONAY 2):** re-derives the genesis outputs from the carried document (after storing it, before the snapshots / registry / genesis apply). Signature unchanged. |

## 8. Scan supply buckets — `dnac_supply` additive keys (`docs/plans/decisions/2026-09-30-scan-supply-buckets.md`)

Read-only; no consensus caller, no `state_root` input.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_WITNESS_SUPPLY_TREASURY_POOLS` (9u), `nodus_witness_supply_view_t { int has_supply; nodus_witness_supply_t supply; uint64_t tip; uint64_t treasury[9]; uint64_t unclaimed; }` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_db.h`) | **NEW.** `treasury[i]` = `v2_treasury.balance` of pool i+1 (no row = 0); `unclaimed` = `nodus_witness_v2_unclaimed_total(DNA_DOMAIN_CORE, native)`. |
| `int nodus_witness_supply_view_get(nodus_witness_t *w, nodus_witness_supply_view_t *out)` (`nodus_witness_db.c`) | **NEW.** Successor chains only. One call on the single witness connection: refuses an open ledger transaction (`sqlite3_get_autocommit` == 0), then `nodus_witness_supply_get` (absent row → `has_supply` 0), `nodus_witness_v2_tip_height`, the treasury scan (non-INTEGER column, pool_id outside 1..9, negative balance → fault) and the unclaimed total. 0 / -1 (never a value on a fault). |
| `static void handle_dnac_supply(...)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour).** Version-3 arm reads through `nodus_witness_supply_view_get`; with a supply row it adds `"reward_pool"`, `"treasury"` (9 uints) and `"unclaimed"` (map 7 → 10 keys, worst case 329 of 512 bytes). A read fault on that arm now answers `NODUS_ERR_INTERNAL_ERROR` "supply state unreadable" (previously zeros for a supply-row fault). Legacy arm unchanged. |
| `NODUS_DNAC_TREASURY_POOLS` (9), `nodus_dnac_supply_buckets_t { bool has; uint64_t current_supply; uint64_t reward_pool; uint64_t treasury[9]; uint64_t unclaimed; }` (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus.h`) | **NEW.** `current_supply` is the SAME reply's `"current"`; `has` false = an older node. |
| `int nodus_dnac_supply_buckets_decode(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, nodus_dnac_supply_buckets_t *out)` (`nodus_client.c`) | **NEW.** Strict: duplicate key, truncation, non-uint, a `"treasury"` that is not exactly 9 uints, `"current"` missing, or 1-2 of the 3 bucket keys → -1 (`out` zeroed); none of the 3 → 0 with `has` false. |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_supply_buckets(nodus_client_t *client, nodus_dnac_supply_buckets_t *out)` (`nodus_client.c`) | **NEW.** Same `dnac_supply` request as `nodus_client_dnac_supply` (thin accessor; `nodus_dnac_supply_result_t` not grown). 0 / `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` malformed / the node's error code / `NODUS_ERR_TIMEOUT` / -1. |
| `exp_chain_tip_t.buckets` (`explorer/src/exp_chain.h`) | **NEW field.** Filled by a fourth round trip in `tip_once`; an older node is not a failure. |
| `EXP_META_SUPPLY_BUCKETS`, `EXP_SUPPLY_BUCKETS_BLOB_LEN` (97), `void exp_supply_buckets_pack(const nodus_dnac_supply_buckets_t *b, uint8_t out[97])`, `int exp_supply_buckets_unpack(const uint8_t *buf, size_t len, nodus_dnac_supply_buckets_t *out)`, `int exp_supply_circulating(const nodus_dnac_supply_buckets_t *b, uint64_t *out)` (`explorer/src/exp_chain.{h,c}`) | **NEW (pure).** The meta blob (has byte + 12 LE u64: current, reward_pool, treasury 1..9, unclaimed) and circulating = current − reward_pool − Σ treasury − unclaimed; -1 on `has` false or any underflow (never wrapped). |

## 9. HF-2 — power-weighted governance approval + net-zero blocks (`docs/plans/2026-09-30-gov-weight-netzero-design.md` rev 2; decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-09-30-governance-stake-weight-and-power-cap.md`)

One height-activated switch, chain_config param 7. No row = both rules as before (the consensus-read envelope-count bound `NODUS_V2_ENV_BATCH_MAX` was frozen at 3 075 so it does not move with the verdict's size — `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-2", "Byte-identical while off").

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `DNAC_CFG_HF2_ACTIVE` (7), `DNAC_CFG_HF2_ACTIVE_ON` (1ULL), `DNAC_CFG_PARAM_MAX_ID` 6 → 7 (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`) | **NEW.** Value domain exactly 1 (a one-way switch). `dnac_cfg_param_read_by_consensus` now also answers true for 7. Witness mirrors `CC_PARAM_HF2_ACTIVE` / `CC_HF2_ACTIVE_ON` (`nodus_witness_chain_config.c`, `_Static_assert`-pinned). |
| `int nodus_chain_config_scalar_rules(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** id 7 accepted with value 1 only (0 and every other value -1). Signature unchanged. |
| `uint64_t nodus_chain_config_grace_for_param(uint8_t param_id)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** id 7 → `DNAC_CHAIN_CONFIG_GRACE_ERGONOMIC_BLOCKS`. Signature unchanged. |
| `static int verify_chain_config_rules(...)` (`dnac/src/transaction/verify.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** client mirror — id 7 accepted with value 1 only. |
| `nodus_rt_committee_t.powers` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_runtime.h`) | **NEW field.** `const uint64_t *`, `count` entries in seat order, each `total_stake / DNAC_DECIMAL_UNIT` (the block-commit derivation). |
| `nodus_rt_auth_verdict_t.approved_power`, `.committee_power` | **NEW fields (appended LAST).** Kind 2 only: checked u64 sums of the verified approving seats' powers and of every seat's; both 0 when the committee sum overflows ("unweighable"); 0 for kinds 1/3. `sizeof` 1 424 → 1 440 B, so `NODUS_V2_APPLY_ENV_COST_BYTES` 21 824 → 21 856; `NODUS_V2_ENV_BATCH_MAX` stays **3 075** (frozen as a literal) and `NODUS_V2_APPLY_MAX_OPS` stays 17 237 (`_Static_assert`), the scratch budget `NODUS_V2_APPLY_SCRATCH_BUDGET_BYTES` rising 64 → 65 MiB instead (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h` pins). |
| `nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t.hf2_active` | **NEW field.** `uint8_t`, 1 when param 7 is active at `global_height`; filled by the engine on every ctx (`env_authorize_legs`, `exec_one_env`). |
| `int nodus_rt_auth_dsa87_v1(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** kind 2 refuses (-1) a non-empty committee view with `powers == NULL` (after the unchanged `count == 0` refusal) and fills the two power sums. Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_rt_system_exec(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** CHAIN_CONFIG approval — `ctx->hf2_active` 0: `n_approvals >= dna_bft_quorum(committee_n)` (unchanged); 1: `committee_power > 0` and `approved_power > committee_power * 2 / 3` (checked doubling; overflow refuses). Signature unchanged. |
| `static int env_hf2_active(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t height, uint8_t *on, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `nodus_chain_config_get_u64(w, 7, height, 0, &v)`; `*on` = (v == 1); a read fault or a stored value outside {0, 1} → -2 (FAULT, reason written). Called by `env_authorize_legs`, `exec_one_env` and phase 9. |
| `static int committee_snapshot_for_height(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t height, nodus_rt_committee_t *view, uint8_t **out_pubkeys, uint8_t (**out_fps)[64], uint64_t **out_powers, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` | **CHANGED (internal signature):** + `out_powers` (heap, caller frees; filled before the member array is freed). |
| phase 9 of `nodus_witness_v2_apply_block` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** with param 7 active at the block's height a touched domain whose root is unchanged writes its DomainUpdate with `pre_root == post_root` instead of a block VERDICT. |
| `nodus-cli chain-config propose --param HF2_ACTIVE --value 1 --effective <H>`; `v2-envelope chain-config` (`nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED (CLI).** `HF2_ACTIVE` / `hf2_active` in the param name table. The offline builder reads param 7 at tip + 1 from its database and, when active, picks approver keys in the given order until their power exceeds 2/3 of the committee's. The online `propose` keeps the seat rule for its own early abort (no RPC reports param 7) and prints a note. `witness` prints the seat quorum AND the committee's total voting power with the `> total*2/3` threshold. |

## 10. HF-3 — Comet-only block bounds + proposal fee/replay check (`docs/plans/2026-10-01-hf3-comet-block-bounds-design.md` rev 3; decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-01-hf3-comet-only-block-bounds.md`)

One height-activated switch, chain_config param 8, read ONCE per block-start context at the height of the block that context judges. No row = every rule as before, byte for byte (`nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-3").

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `DNAC_CFG_HF3_ACTIVE` (8), `DNAC_CFG_HF3_ACTIVE_ON` (1ULL), `DNAC_CFG_PARAM_MAX_ID` 7 → 8 (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`) | **NEW.** Value domain exactly 1 (a one-way switch, no "off" vote). `dnac_cfg_param_read_by_consensus` now also answers true for 8 (read list {4, 5, 6, 7, 8}). Witness mirrors `CC_PARAM_HF3_ACTIVE` / `CC_HF3_ACTIVE_ON`, `CC_PARAM_MAX_ID` 7 → 8 (`nodus_witness_chain_config.c`, `_Static_assert`-pinned). |
| `int nodus_chain_config_scalar_rules(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** id 8 accepted with value 1 only (0 and every other value -1). Signature unchanged. |
| `uint64_t nodus_chain_config_grace_for_param(uint8_t param_id)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** id 8 → `DNAC_CHAIN_CONFIG_GRACE_ERGONOMIC_BLOCKS`, through its own explicit `case` (not `default:`). Signature unchanged. |
| `static int verify_chain_config_rules(...)` (`dnac/src/transaction/verify.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** client mirror — id 8 accepted with value `DNAC_CFG_HF3_ACTIVE_ON` only. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_block_ctx_build(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t height, nodus_witness_v2_block_ctx_t *ctx)` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_v2_env.h`) | **CHANGED (signature):** + `height` = the height of the block the context judges (the caller's candidate tip + 1 on every pre-commit path). Selects ONE input — param 8 at `height` — which sets `ctx->hf3_active` and, when 1, the budget's unbounded flags. Returns 0 / -1 (SYSTEM not ACTIVE) / -2 node fault, which now also covers an unreadable param-8 row or one holding a value other than 0/1. Callers: `produce_batch_check_impl` (`candidate`), `nodus_cmt_app_prepare_proposal` (its own tip + 1), `nodus_cmt_app_entry_identity` (tip + 1), `cc_appr_verdict` (`h` = tip + 1). |
| `static int block_ctx_from_doms(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t height, dom_ctx_t *doms, size_t n_dom, nodus_witness_v2_block_ctx_t *ctx)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **CHANGED (internal signature):** + `w`, `height`. Reads the switch FIRST (`env_hf3_active`; a fault → -2 with the ctx zeroed), sets `ctx->budget.global_unbounded = ctx->hf3_active` and each domain slot's `unbounded = (hf3_active && quota_verify_cost == 0)`. Callers: `nodus_witness_v2_block_ctx_build`, `env_item_setup` (dry run, `s->height`), `v2_apply_block_body` (`blk->global_height`). |
| `nodus_witness_v2_block_ctx_t.hf3_active` (`nodus_witness_v2_env.h`) | **NEW field.** `uint8_t`, 0 or 1: committed param 8 at the context's height, resolved once by the builder; consumers (the seam's byte step, PrepareProposal's byte bound, rule 5, the seam's fee/replay check) read it and never re-read the switch. |
| `static int env_hf3_active(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t height, uint8_t *on, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `nodus_chain_config_get_u64(w, 8, height, 0, &v)`; `*on` = (v == 1); a read fault or a stored value outside {0, 1} → -2 (FAULT, reason written). One caller: `block_ctx_from_doms`. |
| `nodus_v2_env_status_t nodus_witness_v2_env_preflight_reserve_batch(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t proposed_global_height, const dna_env_leg_ctx_t *rulesets, size_t n_rulesets, const dna_meter_policy_t *policy, dna_meter_budget_t *budget, int skip_byte_bound, const nodus_v2_envelope_t *envs, size_t n_envs, dna_env_preflight_t *out, dna_meter_t *meters_out, size_t *fail_index_out, dna_env_preflight_status_t *pf_status_out, dna_meter_status_t *meter_status_out)` (`nodus_witness_v2_env.h`) | **CHANGED (signature):** + `int skip_byte_bound` after `budget`. 0 = step 4b (the whole-batch envelope-byte sum against `policy->max_block_env_bytes`) runs unchanged; nonzero = step 4b is skipped (no sum, no allocation, never `NODUS_V2_ENV_ERR_BLOCK_BYTES`). The caller takes it from the block-start context's `hf3_active` (the one production caller, `produce_batch_check_impl`, passes `(int)bctx->hf3_active`). |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_gas_price_at(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t height, uint64_t *price_out, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **NEW (exported; the read half of the HF-1 rule).** The committed `GAS_PRICE_RAW_PER_UNIT` (param 5) active at `height`, 0 when no row is active. Returns 0 (`*price_out` set) / -2 the row is unreadable on this node — a FAULT, never a verdict (reason written). |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_gas_price_judge(const dna_env_view_t *v, uint64_t price, uint32_t *code, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` | **NEW (exported; the pure half).** price 0 → rule off (returns 0 before any other test); every leg SYSTEM → exempt; otherwise refuses when `fee_amount < max(res_max_total_units × price, the flat floor)` — a u64 overflow of the product is a refusal. Returns 0 pass / -1 refused with `*code = NODUS_V2_TX_ERR_FEE` (reason written). The engine's static `env_gas_price_check` is now exactly `_at` followed by `_judge` (signature unchanged). |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_units_ceiling_check(const dna_env_view_t *v, uint32_t *code, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` | **NEW (exported; HF-3 rule 5, decision answer 10).** PURE: `res_max_total_units > INT64_MAX` → -1 with `*code = NODUS_V2_TX_ERR_CAPACITY` (reason written), else 0. The CALLER decides HF-3 is on (its context's `hf3_active`) and calls it only then: CheckTx dry run and FinalizeBlock item loop (after the gas price, before the reservation — a refused item keeps `gas_wanted = 0`), and the seam. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_replay_guard(nodus_witness_t *w, const dna_env_preflight_t *p, uint32_t generation, size_t item, int *hit, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` | **HF-7 P1c-2: + `generation` (the table it asks is chosen by it — §28 below).** **NEW (exported; was the static `env_replay_guard`, which is now a one-line wrapper).** Is the envelope's `intent_id` in the committed intent index or its `wire_id` in the committed tx index? Returns 0 with `*hit` set (1 = already committed) / -2 a node-local read fault (reason written) — never a verdict. `item` is for the fault text only. Callers: dry run and item loop (order preflight → replay → admission → gas price), and from the HF-3 height the seam (rule 6b, decision answer 12). |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_produce_batch_check_capped(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_produce.h`; body `produce_batch_check_impl`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** the envelope subset's context is built at `candidate` (tip + 1); when its `hf3_active` is 1 the seam passes `skip_byte_bound` = 1, and after a reservation-clean run — price read ONCE at `candidate` — every envelope in batch order passes `nodus_witness_v2_replay_guard`, then `nodus_witness_v2_gas_price_judge`, then `nodus_witness_v2_units_ceiling_check`. A refusal → -1, `result_out->kind = NODUS_V2_BATCH_FAIL_ENTRY_INVALID`, `*fail_index_out` = that envelope's index in `items`, raw statuses left at their OK values; an unreadable price or replay index → -2, kind `NODUS_V2_BATCH_FAIL_FAULT`. Below the HF-3 height none of this runs. Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_env_dry_run(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** when the context (built at tip + 1) has `hf3_active`, rule 5 runs after the gas price and before the reservation. Signature unchanged. |
| FinalizeBlock item loop (`v2_apply_block_body`, `nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** context built at `blk->global_height`; when `hf3_active`, rule 5 after the gas price and before the reservation (refused → `cmt_item_failed`, code 5). Global / quota-0 budget refusals cannot occur from H (flags). |
| `int nodus_cmt_app_prepare_proposal(...)` (`nodus_witness_cmt_app.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** reads the committed tip itself and builds the context at tip + 1 (an unreadable tip = node fault → `CMT_FAULT`); `max_env_bytes` = `UINT64_MAX` when `hf3_active`, else `policy->max_block_env_bytes` (never 0). The pack's budget copy carries the unbounded flags. Signature unchanged. |
| `dna_meter_domain_budget_t.unbounded`, `dna_meter_budget_t.global_unbounded` (`shared/dnac/res_meter.h`) | **NEW fields.** `uint8_t`, 0 or 1, placed in former struct padding (neither struct changed size). A flagged remainder is not a bound: reserve, the charges, finalize and abort neither read nor write that slot's `remaining_units` / `global_remaining` and evaluate no arithmetic on it; every meter-local quantity (`g_reserved`, `g_consumed`, `dom_dyn`, `dom_consumed`, released amounts, CEILING / LIMIT gates) is computed as for an unflagged slot. Both 0 (zeroed memory) = the pre-HF-3 behaviour. |
| `int dna_meter_budget_check(const dna_meter_budget_t *b)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** also returns -1 when `global_unbounded > 1` or any `dom[i].unbounded > 1` (a malformed budget, not a second kind of "unbounded"). Signature unchanged. |
| `dna_meter_status_t dna_meter_reserve(...)`, `dna_meter_charge_effects(...)`, `dna_meter_charge_read(...)`, `dna_meter_charge_write(...)`, `dna_meter_finalize(dna_meter_t *m)`, `dna_meter_abort(dna_meter_t *m)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** honour the flags as above — reserve never returns `ERR_GLOBAL_BUDGET` / `ERR_DOMAIN_BUDGET` for a flagged remainder; a charge past the static share still grows `dom_dyn` on a flagged slot; finalize sets `g_released` / `dom_released` for every slot but releases nothing into a flagged remainder; abort restores nothing to one. Signatures unchanged. |
| `nodus-cli chain-config propose --param HF3_ACTIVE --value 1 --effective <H>` (`nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED (CLI).** `HF3_ACTIVE` / `hf3_active` in `cc_param_name_to_id`'s table; the per-param range listing and the "Unknown param name" / usage texts name it. |

## 11. Web wallet package (c2) — the shared CORE SPEND builder (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus_v2_spend.h`, `nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_spend.c`)

Design `docs/plans/2026-09-25-web-wallet-nodus-send-design.md` §0a.3 (c2) / §1.3; decision
`docs/plans/decisions/2026-09-25-web-wallet-nodus-send-transport.md` ("İşlem kurucu" + addendum
2026-09-29 "Yol 2"). Moved out of `nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c` (the `t6_*` helpers and the plan/build
loops of `cmd_v2_spend`); behaviour unchanged. Compiled into `libnodus` (so also into `libdna.so`
via the messenger build) and, standalone, for the browser module. No I/O, no clock, no global RNG:
output seeds come from the caller's `nodus_v2_rand_fn`. (`qgp_dsa87_sign` is hedged — two builds
differ in signature bytes and `wire_id`, never in `intent_id` or any byte outside the auth blob.)

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_V2_SPEND_MAX_IN` (15), `NODUS_V2_SPEND_OUT_LEN` (232), `NODUS_V2_SPEND_MAX_OUTS` (3) | **NEW (moved).** Were `T6_SPEND_MAX_IN` / `T6_SPEND_OUT_LEN` / `T6_SPEND_MAX_OUTS` in nodus-cli. |
| `nodus_v2_spend_rc_t` | **NEW.** 0 OK, 1 `NONE_ELIGIBLE` (plan, count_all), negative refusals `-1 ARG` … `-23 DUP_OUTPUT` (header). |
| `nodus_v2_spend_order_t` | **NEW.** Only `NODUS_V2_SPEND_ORDER_LARGEST_FIRST` (0) — largest amount first, ties by nullifier. Smallest-first is ÖNERİ (`2026-09-25-spend-inputs-64-smallest-first.md` item 1) and refused. |
| `nodus_v2_coin_t { nul[64]; amount; kind; used; }`, `nodus_v2_spend_plan_t { idx[15]; n_in; native_in, token_in, native_change, token_change; }` | **NEW (moved).** Were `t6_coin_t` / `t6_spend_plan_t`. `nul` stays the first member. |
| `nodus_v2_ruleset_id_t { core_ruleset_version; core_ruleset_hash[64]; const dna_meter_policy_t *meter_policy; }` | **NEW.** The CORE ruleset identity + the BLOCK (SYSTEM) metering policy a SPEND is built and priced against. |
| `typedef int (*nodus_v2_rand_fn)(void *ctx, uint8_t *buf, size_t len)` | **NEW.** Caller randomness (0 = OK). |
| `nodus_v2_spend_err_t { k; fee; units; required; pass; n_in, n_out; leg; draws; }` | **NEW.** The numbers a refusal carries, for the caller's message. |
| `int nodus_v2_nul_cmp(const void *a, const void *b)` | **NEW (moved, was `t6_nul_cmp`).** memcmp of the first 64 bytes. |
| `int nodus_v2_spend_sort_coins(nodus_v2_coin_t *coins, int n_coins, nodus_v2_spend_order_t order)` | **NEW (was `qsort(…, t6_coin_cmp)`).** 0 / `ERR_ARG` (unknown order). |
| `int nodus_v2_spend_pick(const nodus_v2_coin_t *coins, int n_coins, uint8_t kind, uint64_t need, nodus_v2_spend_plan_t *plan, uint64_t *sum_out)` | **NEW (moved, was `t6_spend_pick`).** 0 / -1 short / -2 > 15 inputs / -3 sum overflow. |
| `void nodus_v2_xfer_out_put(uint8_t *rec, const char *owner_hex128, uint64_t amount, const uint8_t *token64, const uint8_t seed32[32])` | **NEW (moved, was `t6_xfer_out_put`).** One 232-byte output record. |
| `void nodus_v2_spend_effect_decl(uint32_t n_in, uint32_t n_out, uint32_t *effects_out, uint32_t *bytes_out)` | **NEW (moved, was `t6_spend_effect_decl`).** effects = n_in + n_out + 1; bytes = 116 + 148·n_in + 432·n_out. |
| `int nodus_v2_spend_ceiling(dna_env_in_t *env_in, const dna_meter_policy_t *pol, uint32_t n_reads, uint64_t *ceiling_out)` | **NEW (moved, was `t6_spend_ceiling`).** static_units + n_reads × w_read. 0 / -1. |
| `int nodus_v2_spend_units_for_shape(uint32_t core_ruleset_version, const dna_meter_policy_t *pol, uint32_t alen, int n_in, int n_out, uint64_t *units_out)` | **NEW (moved, was `t6_spend_units_for_shape(core_rt, …)`).** First parameter is now the ruleset version, not the runtime; refuses n_in ∉ 1..15, n_out ∉ 1..3. |
| `int nodus_v2_env_sign_one_key(const dna_env_in_t *env_in, uint8_t *const *auths, const dna_env_leg_ctx_t *lctx, const uint8_t chain32[32], uint64_t tip, const uint8_t *pk, const uint8_t *sk, uint8_t **env_out, size_t *env_len_out, dna_env_preflight_t *pf, nodus_v2_spend_err_t *err)` | **NEW (moved, was `t6_env_sign_one_key(…, const nodus_identity_t *key, …)`).** Takes pk/sk bytes; prints nothing — returns `ERR_PREFLIGHT1` / `ERR_SIGN` (`err->leg`) / `ERR_PREFLIGHT2` / `ERR_ENCODE` / `ERR_ALLOC`. |
| `int nodus_v2_ruleset_from_pins(nodus_v2_ruleset_id_t *out, dna_meter_policy_t *policy_storage)` | **NEW.** Fills the CORE tuple from `nodus_ruleset_pins.h` and rebuilds the SYSTEM meter policy into `policy_storage`; `ERR_PINS` unless its `dna_meter_policy_digest` equals the pinned digest (the WASM start-up self-check). Since HF-4 this is the GENERATION-1 wrapper of `nodus_v2_ruleset_from_pins_gen` (§15.8). |
| `int nodus_v2_spend_plan(const nodus_v2_spend_plan_req_t *req, nodus_v2_coin_t *coins, int n_coins, nodus_v2_spend_plan_t **plans_out, long *count_out, uint64_t *fee_out, nodus_v2_spend_err_t *err)` | **NEW (the plan loop of `cmd_v2_spend`).** Sorts `coins` in place (largest first, ties by nullifier), plans `count` disjoint spends, bounded gas-price fixed point (≤ 8 passes, one fee per batch; `fee_fixed` never raised). `*plans_out` heap. **0.25.2 — dust rules, single spend only (`count == 1`, not `count_all`), both off with `req->no_dust_sweep = 1`:** (1) SWEEP — after the covering pick, unused native coins smallest-first (ties by nullifier) are added up to 15 inputs while each is worth more than one more input's marginal fee (`(units(n+1) − units(n)) × gas_price`; any coin > 0 at gas 0); (2) ABSORB — unless `fee_fixed` or `amount_all`, a native change ≤ that marginal fee (≤ the fee floor at gas 0) is not created and is added to the fee; `*fee_out` includes it. |
| `int nodus_v2_spend_build(const nodus_v2_spend_build_req_t *req, nodus_v2_spend_built_t *out, nodus_v2_spend_err_t *err)`, `void nodus_v2_spend_built_free(nodus_v2_spend_built_t *b)` | **NEW (the build loop of `cmd_v2_spend`).** One envelope from one plan: call, leg, units, gas check, two-pass signature, then read-back and refusal if it differs from the request. Refuses tip 0 and expiry ∉ (tip, tip + 100]. Optional output-id shard (`shard_m`, `shard_i`). |
| `int nodus_v2_spend_decode(const uint8_t *env, size_t env_len, nodus_v2_spend_decoded_t *out)` | **NEW.** Self-consistent decode of a one-leg CORE SPEND this module builds: inputs, outputs (owner, amount, token, id), fee, expiry, units, declaration. |
| `static int cli_sign_one_key(...)`, `static int cli_rand(void *ctx, uint8_t *buf, size_t len)`, `static void cli_ruleset_id(const nodus_domain_runtime_t *core_rt, const nodus_domain_runtime_t *sys_rt, nodus_v2_ruleset_id_t *out)` (`nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal).** The CLI's wrappers: prints the sign refusal as before; `nodus_random` as the seed source; the compiled-table ruleset. |

## 12. Generated ruleset pins (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus_ruleset_pins.h`, `nodus/tools/gen_ruleset_pins.c`)

Decision `2026-09-25-web-wallet-nodus-send-transport.md` addendum 2026-09-29 "Yol 2".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_PIN_CORE_*`, `NODUS_PIN_SYS_METER_*` macros | **NEW (GENERATED — never edit).** CORE domain/kind/abi/ruleset_version/ruleset_hash and the SYSTEM meter policy fields, op list, weights and digest, read from the compiled table — since HF-4 as rule-set GENERATION 1 through `nodus_runtime_for_generation(1, …)` (bytes unchanged); generation 2 is rendered as `NODUS_PIN_G2_*` (§15.8). Regenerate: `cmake --build <nodus build> --target regen_ruleset_pins`. |
| `int nodus_ruleset_pins_render(char **out, size_t *out_len)` (`nodus/tools/gen_ruleset_pins.c`) | **NEW (tool).** Renders the header text (heap, caller frees); -1 if the runtime selfcheck fails or the SYSTEM policy does not match its committed digest. `test_ruleset_pins` byte-compares it with the checked-in file. |
| `NODUS_PIN_SYS_DOMAIN_ID`, `NODUS_PIN_SYS_RUNTIME_KIND`, `NODUS_PIN_SYS_RUNTIME_ABI`, `NODUS_PIN_SYS_RULESET_VERSION`, `NODUS_PIN_SYS_RULESET_HASH_INIT` | **NEW (GENERATED, 2026-09-30).** The SYSTEM ruleset identity — the staking envelopes' SYSTEM leg signs over it (`shared/dnac/env_preflight.c:77`). Same generator, same byte-compare test (`test_ruleset_pins` R4). |

## 13. Web wallet staking — the shared STAKE / DELEGATE / UNSTAKE / UNDELEGATE builder (`nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_stake.h`, `nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_stake.c`)

I/O-free, like the (c2) SPEND builder: nodus-cli `v2-envelope stake|delegate|undelegate|unstake` and the web wallet's `send.wasm` build every staking envelope with it (the web wallet builds ops 1, 2 and 4 only — it refuses any other op before calling the builder, `web-wallet/crypto/nodus-send-wasm.c`). Since B2b-CLI it also builds the storage ops 7 (STORAGE_REGISTER) and 8 (STORAGE_EXIT) for `nodus-cli storage register|exit` (§24.7).

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_v2_stake_ruleset_from_pins(nodus_v2_stake_ruleset_t *out)` | **NEW.** SYSTEM + CORE (version, hash) from the generated pins header; `NODUS_V2_SPEND_OK` / `_ERR_ARG` (NULL `out`). It copies the tuples only — it does not rebuild or check the SYSTEM meter policy digest (that is `nodus_v2_ruleset_from_pins`). Since HF-4 it is `nodus_v2_stake_ruleset_from_pins_gen(1, out)`, which reads the tuples through `nodus_v2_pins_tuples` (§15.8). |
| `int nodus_v2_stake_build(const nodus_v2_stake_req_t *req, nodus_v2_stake_built_t *out, nodus_v2_stake_err_t *err)` | **NEW.** Two-leg envelope: SYSTEM record leg (runtime_op 1 STAKE / 2 DELEGATE / 3 UNSTAKE / 4 UNDELEGATE) + CORE SYSFUND funding leg; kind-1 single signer; fee = max(floor, 400000 × gas_price); native unlocked coins in ascending nullifier order until lock + fee (lock 0 for UNSTAKE and UNDELEGATE); one deterministic change output. Signs, then reads the envelope back and refuses on any difference. Returns `NODUS_V2_SPEND_OK` or a refusal (−40..−43 bond / commission / amount / op, others shared with the spend builder). |
| `int nodus_v2_stake_decode(const uint8_t *env, size_t env_len, nodus_v2_stake_decoded_t *out)` | **NEW.** Strict decode of a staking envelope built by this module (shape, ops, declarations, input order, one signer = record identity). |
| `void nodus_v2_stake_built_free(nodus_v2_stake_built_t *b)` | **NEW.** NULL-safe. |
| `NODUS_V2_STAKE_OP_UNSTAKE` (= 3 = `DNA_SYSRULE_UNSTAKE`), `NODUS_V2_UNSTAKE_CALL_LEN` (2592u) | **NEW (2026-10-03).** Signatures unchanged; `nodus_v2_stake_build` and `nodus_v2_stake_decode` accept op 3. UNSTAKE call = the signer's own 2592-byte key (`RTN_SYS_UNSTAKE_CALL_LEN`; `rtn_sys_stake_auth` requires signer = that key); funding leg fee-only (lock 0, release 0 — `rtn_sys_call_flow`); the request's `amount`, `commission_bps`, `dest_fp` and `validator_pk` are ignored (no amount check, `validator_pk` may be NULL); the decoded `amount` and `validator_pk` are zero and the read-back skips both. The chain moves the row to RETIRING (`rtn_unstake_exec`, ACTIVE or ELIGIBLE only); bond and delegations are released at graduation. `NODUS_V2_STAKE_ERR_OP` now means "not op 1 / 2 / 3 / 4". |
| `static const char *cli_validator_status_name(uint8_t st)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `dnac_validator_status_t` byte → `ACTIVE` / `RETIRING` / `UNSTAKED` / `AUTO_RETIRED` / `ELIGIBLE` / `UNKNOWN`. |
| `static int cli_validator_row(nodus_client_t *client, const uint8_t *pk, nodus_dnac_validator_list_entry_t *row)` (`nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Walks `nodus_client_dnac_validator_list` (all statuses, pages of `DNAC_MAX_VALIDATORS`, at most 64 pages) for `pk`'s row. 1 found / 0 the listing ended without it / -1 query failed or the walk did not end. Used by `v2-envelope unstake` (refuses a found row that is not ACTIVE / ELIGIBLE; otherwise informational). |

Out of every root, vote and validity path. Written only with the node flag `addr_history_index: true` (nodus.json; `nodus_server_config_t.addr_history_index`, default false). Rows per op: the module header.

IF NOT EXISTS` of `addr_history` (PK `(h, i, seq)`, index `(owner, h, i, seq)`) and `addr_history_mark`; called from `nodus_witness_db_migrate_v12` on every open.
