## 15. HF-4 — the rule-set generation switch at a height + on-chain names (`docs/plans/2026-10-02-onchain-names-design.md` rev 4; decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-02-onchain-names.md`)

One height-activated vote, chain_config param 9, whose value names the target rule set; at the end of block H−1 the engine moves the SYSTEM and CORE registry records from compiled generation 1 to generation 2 (CORE op 8 NAME_REGISTER). No param-9 row = generation 1, byte-identical to the pre-HF-4 binary (`nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-4"). nodus 0.23.10 / dnac 0.19.5.

### 15.1 Parameters, constants and shared helpers (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`)

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN2` (9), `DNAC_CFG_NAME_PRICE_3P` (10), `DNAC_CFG_NAME_PRICE_4P` (11), `DNAC_CFG_NAME_PRICE_5P` (12), `DNAC_CFG_NAME_PRICE_6P` (13), `DNAC_CFG_PARAM_MAX_ID` 8 → 13 | **NEW.** `dnac_cfg_param_read_by_consensus` answers true for 9-13 too (read list {4..13}). Witness mirrors `CC_PARAM_RULESET_GEN2`, `CC_PARAM_NAME_PRICE_3P..6P`, `CC_PARAM_MAX_ID` 13 (`nodus_witness_chain_config.c`, `_Static_assert`-pinned). |
| `DNAC_RULESET_SWITCH_SPEC_VERSION` (1u) | **NEW.** Version of the phase-6b′ switch procedure; hashed into D2. |
| `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN2_D2` (`0x44dfbe7ad3c75adfULL` = 4962894749133920991) | **NEW.** The ONLY legal param-9 value: first 8 bytes BE of SHA3-512("NDS.RSGEN.v1" 16 B ‖ u32 BE 2 ‖ gen-2 SYSTEM ruleset_hash[64] ‖ gen-2 CORE ruleset_hash[64] ‖ u32 BE switch spec version), top bit cleared (`_Static_assert` ≤ INT64_MAX). Literal from the independent oracle `shared/dnac/tests/hf4_oracle.py` (G4); re-derived by `nodus_witness_runtime_selfcheck`. |
| `DNAC_CFG_MIN_NAME_PRICE` (10^8), `DNAC_CFG_MAX_NAME_PRICE` (10^15) | **NEW.** Inclusive range of params 10-13, raw. Witness mirrors `CC_MIN_NAME_PRICE` / `CC_MAX_NAME_PRICE`. |
| `DNAC_NAME_PRICE_3P_DEFAULT` (10^11), `_4P_DEFAULT` (5·10^10), `_5P_DEFAULT` (10^10), `_6P_DEFAULT` (10^8) | **NEW.** Compiled no-row prices (1 000 / 500 / 100 / 1 NODUS). `_Static_assert`: inside the range, non-increasing, 6P == the floor. |
| `DNAC_NAME_MIN_LEN` (3u), `DNAC_NAME_MAX_LEN` (36u), `DNAC_NAME_HEXLIKE_MIN_LEN` (8u) | **NEW.** Name byte-rule bounds. |
| `static inline int dnac_name_bytes_ok(const uint8_t *name, size_t len)` | **NEW.** The one name-byte rule (the CORE op-8 parse, the queries, every client): 3..36 bytes, each `a-z` or `0-9` (uppercase refused); a name of only `0-9a-f` with len ≥ 8 refused. 1 legal / 0 refused (NULL → 0). |
| `static inline uint64_t dnac_name_price_for_len(const uint64_t p[4], size_t len)` | **NEW.** Monotonic fold of the four tiers `p[0..3]` = params 10..13: 3 = max(P3..P6), 4 = max(P4..P6), 5 = max(P5, P6), 6..36 = P6. 0 for a length outside 3..36 or NULL `p`. Shared by the engine, `dnac_fee_info` and the clients. |
| `static int verify_chain_config_rules(...)` (`dnac/src/transaction/verify.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** client mirror — id 9 accepted with value `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN2_D2` only; ids 10-13 within `[DNAC_CFG_MIN_NAME_PRICE, DNAC_CFG_MAX_NAME_PRICE]`. The witness's stateful rules are NOT mirrored (no chain state). |

### 15.2 Chain-config rule authorities (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus_chain_config.h`, `nodus_witness_chain_config.c`)

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_chain_config_scalar_rules(uint8_t param_id, uint64_t new_value, uint64_t signed_at_block, uint64_t valid_before_block, uint64_t effective_block_height, uint64_t proposal_nonce)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** id 9 accepted with value exactly D2; ids 10-13 within [10^8, 10^15]. Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_chain_config_stateful_rules(uint8_t param_id, uint64_t effective_block_height, uint8_t hf2_active, uint8_t ruleset_gen2_voted, uint32_t judging_generation)` | **NEW.** Pure. Id 9: -1 if `ruleset_gen2_voted` (any param-9 row committed — single use), if `!hf2_active`, if `effective_block_height` is 0 or `effective − 1` is a nonzero multiple of `DNAC_EPOCH_LENGTH`. Ids 10-13: -1 unless `judging_generation >= NODUS_RT_GEN_2`. Ids 1-8: 0. Any other id: -1. Callers: `nodus_rt_system_exec` (ctx facts + its runtime's generation) and `cc_appr_rules_chain_config` (facts derived at the candidate height). |
| `uint64_t nodus_chain_config_grace_for_param(uint8_t param_id)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** ids 9-13 → `DNAC_CHAIN_CONFIG_GRACE_ERGONOMIC_BLOCKS`, each through an explicit `case`. Signature unchanged. |
| `static int cc_appr_rules_chain_config(...)` | **CHANGED (internal behaviour):** after the scalar rules, derives HF-2 at h (param 7), "any param-9 row" (param 9 at `INT64_MAX`) and the generation of the SYSTEM runtime the registry resolves, and applies `nodus_chain_config_stateful_rules`; unreadable → -2, refusal → -1 "stateful rules rejected". |
| ~~`int nodus_chain_config_apply(nodus_witness_t *w, const uint8_t *tx_data, uint32_t tx_len, uint64_t block_height, uint64_t block_timestamp)`~~ | **REMOVED (HF-4 review L1 F1)** with its private parse / rule / digest helpers (incl. `verify_cc_local_rules`): the legacy tx-lane apply had no caller since R3 W4. The one apply path is the SYSTEM CHAIN_CONFIG runtime. |
| `chain_config_cache` (`nodus_witness.h`), `CC_PARAM_SLOTS` | **CHANGED (size):** derived from `DNAC_CFG_PARAM_MAX_ID`, now 14 rows (ids 9-13 get slots — without them every read of 9-13 would answer -1 and FAULT). |

### 15.3 Rule-set generations (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_runtime.{h,c}`, `shared/dnac/domain_wire.{h,c}`)

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `DNA_CORERULE_NAME_REGISTER` ((uint32_t)8) | **NEW.** Owned by the generation-2 CORE descriptor (CORE v5, rules {1..8}) only. |
| `NODUS_RT_GEN_1` (1), `NODUS_RT_GEN_2` (2), `NODUS_RT_GEN_MAX` (= `NODUS_RT_GEN_2`) | **NEW.** Generation ids. |
| `nodus_domain_runtime_t.generation` | **NEW field.** `uint32_t`, the generation an entry belongs to; not hashed, not an identity axis; 0 in synthetic test runtimes (read as "not generation 2"). |
| `const nodus_domain_runtime_t *nodus_runtime_builtin_table(size_t *n_out)` | **CHANGED (contract):** now defined as generation 1 ONLY — the same two entries it returned before HF-4 (SYSTEM v6 + CORE v4, `*n_out` = 2) — genesis seeding, the pre-registry supply walk and fixtures; never the lookup surface for a committed tuple. Signature unchanged. |
| `const nodus_domain_runtime_t *nodus_runtime_all_table(size_t *n_out)` | **NEW.** Every entry of every generation, generation-major. `nodus_witness_v2_runtime_for` (no test override) resolves committed tuples against it. |
| `uint32_t nodus_runtime_generation_count(void)` | **NEW.** Number of compiled generations (2). |
| `const nodus_domain_runtime_t *nodus_runtime_generation_table(uint32_t generation, size_t *n_out)` | **NEW.** One generation's slice (SYSTEM then CORE); NULL for an unknown generation. |
| `const nodus_domain_runtime_t *nodus_runtime_for_generation(uint32_t generation, uint32_t domain_id)` | **NEW.** The (domain, generation) entry — replaces every "first entry of this domain" lookup. NULL if absent. |
| `const nodus_domain_runtime_t *nodus_runtime_lookup(uint32_t domain_id, uint8_t runtime_kind, uint32_t runtime_abi, uint32_t ruleset_version, const uint8_t ruleset_hash[DNA_DOM_HASH_LEN])` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** exact-tuple lookup across EVERY generation. Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_runtime_selfcheck(void)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** also checks exact-tuple uniqueness, one SYSTEM + one CORE per generation with contiguous ids, strictly increasing `ruleset_version` and unchanged kind/ABI per domain, generation 1 = the genesis literals, every generation's SYSTEM policy pricing every rule id of its two descriptors, and that `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN2_D2` re-derives through `dna_ruleset_gen_digest`. The generation-list failures are logged (`W_RUNTIME`). Signature unchanged. |
| `static int sys_policy_build(dna_meter_policy_t *p, uint32_t max_op)` (`nodus_witness_runtime.c`) | **CHANGED (internal signature):** + `max_op` — generation 1 passes 7 (policy byte-identical, digest `8f1f9cb2…`), generation 2 passes 8 (digest `0c251ca2…`). |
| `nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t.ruleset_gen2_voted` | **NEW field.** `uint8_t`, 1 when ANY param-9 row exists (any effective height); filled by the engine per item (`env_ruleset_gen2_voted`); UNMETERED — not a mediated read. One consumer: the SYSTEM CHAIN_CONFIG exec. |
| `nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t.name_price[4]` | **NEW field.** `uint64_t`, params 10..13 at `global_height` (row or compiled default); filled by the engine (`env_name_prices`). One consumer: the CORE NAME_REGISTER exec. |
| `int dna_ruleset_gen_digest(uint32_t generation, const uint8_t sys_ruleset_hash[DNA_DOM_HASH_LEN], const uint8_t core_ruleset_hash[DNA_DOM_HASH_LEN], uint32_t switch_spec_version, uint64_t *out)` (`shared/dnac/domain_wire.h`) | **NEW.** SHA3-512 over the 152-byte preimage `"NDS.RSGEN.v1"` (16 B) ‖ generation u32 BE ‖ sys hash ‖ core hash ‖ switch_spec_version u32 BE; `*out` = first 8 digest bytes BE with the top bit cleared. 0 / -1 (NULL or hash failure; `*out` untouched). Consumer: selfcheck only. |

### 15.4 Engine — the vote facts and the switch (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`, `nodus_witness_domreg.{h,c}`, `nodus_witness_v2_claims.c`)

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `static int env_ruleset_gen2_voted(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t *voted, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` | **NEW (internal).** `nodus_chain_config_get_u64(w, 9, INT64_MAX, 0, &v)`; `*voted` = (a row is active). 0 / -2 FAULT (reason written). Called by `exec_one_env` (once per envelope) and `env_authorize_legs`. |
| `static int env_name_prices(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t height, uint64_t out[4], char *reason, size_t reason_size)` | **NEW (internal).** Params 10..13 at `height` with the compiled defaults; an unreadable row or a value outside [10^8, 10^15] → -2 FAULT. Same two callers. |
| `static int phase_6b_ruleset_switch(nodus_witness_t *w, nodus_v2_block_t *blk, dom_ctx_t *doms, size_t n_dom)` | **NEW (internal).** Phase 6b′ of `v2_apply_block_body` (after the item/claim loop, before 6c). Reads param 9 at h+1 and h; on the edge (present at h+1, absent at h) requires value == D2, calls `nodus_witness_domreg_generation_switch(w, 1, 2)` and marks SYSTEM and CORE touched. 0 (switched or nothing) / -2 FAULT (unreadable, wrong value, registry not generation 1, rewrite failure, h+1 > INT64_MAX). |
| `int nodus_witness_domreg_gen_manifest(const dna_domain_manifest_t *cur, const struct nodus_domain_runtime *from, const struct nodus_domain_runtime *to, dna_domain_manifest_t *out)` (`nodus_witness_domreg.h`) | **NEW.** Pure manifest transform: copies every field, replaces ruleset_version, ruleset_hash, tx_type_count, tx_types from `to`. 0 (validated) / 1 `cur` is not `from`'s exact tuple / -1 malformed (domain mismatch, kind/ABI change, invalid result). |
| `int nodus_witness_domreg_gen_record(const dna_domreg_record_t *cur, const dna_domain_manifest_t *new_man, dna_domreg_record_t *out)` | **NEW.** Pure record transform: every field copied except `current_manifest_hash` (recomputed over `new_man`); requires status ACTIVE and every pending / proposal / scheduling field empty. 0 / -1. |
| `int nodus_witness_domreg_generation_switch(nodus_witness_t *w, uint32_t from_gen, uint32_t to_gen)` | **NEW.** Rewrites the SYSTEM and CORE registry rows inside the caller's transaction; loads and transforms BOTH before writing EITHER; a pending manifest refuses; other domains untouched. 0 / 1 registry not at `from_gen` / -1 read, precondition or write fault. |
| `int nodus_witness_domreg_init_genesis(nodus_witness_t *w)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** refuses unless both `nodus_runtime_builtin_table` entries are generation 1 (genesis stays generation-1 seeding). Signature unchanged. |

| `int nodus_witness_v2_supply_check(nodus_witness_t *w)` | **CHANGED (comment only):** the pre-registry loop is generation 1 (`nodus_runtime_builtin_table`). |

### 15.5 CORE op 8 NAME_REGISTER (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_rt_native.{h,c}`)

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|

Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_rt_core_exec(const nodus_domain_runtime_t *rt, const dna_env_view_t *env, uint16_t leg_index, const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx, const nodus_rt_read_res_t *reads, uint16_t n_reads, uint8_t *res_out, size_t res_cap, size_t *res_len_out)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** op 8 → `rtn_name_exec` (generation ≥ 2; owner shape and parse decided BEFORE the generic verdict check so every owner problem is -1, never -2). Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_rt_system_exec(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** `rt` now read — CHAIN_CONFIG applies `nodus_chain_config_stateful_rules` after freshness and grace with `ctx->hf2_active`, `ctx->ruleset_gen2_voted` and `rt->generation` (NULL → 0). The CHAIN_CONFIG read plan stays empty. Signature unchanged. |
| `static int rtn_name_parse(const dna_env_view_t *env, uint16_t leg, rtn_name_call_t *n)` | **NEW (internal).** Call v1 `name_len u8 ‖ name ‖ price u64 BE ‖ transfer section (in 1..13, out 0..16)`, exact length, `dnac_name_bytes_ok`, every output native. 0 / -1. |
| `static int rtn_name_owner(const dna_env_view_t *env, uint16_t leg, const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx, const uint8_t **owner_out)` | **NEW (internal).** auth_kind 1, exactly one signer, no msig → `signer_fp[0]`; else -1. |
| `static int rtn_name_exec(const rtn_name_call_t *nc, const uint8_t *owner, const dna_env_view_t *env, const nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t *ctx, rtn_owners_t *own, const nodus_rt_read_res_t *reads, uint16_t n_reads, uint8_t *res_out, size_t res_cap, size_t *res_len_out)` | **NEW (internal).** One leg; NAME and OWNER absent; fee ≥ max(`DNAC_MIN_FEE_RAW`, `NODUS_W_BASE_TX_FEE`); declared price == `dnac_name_price_for_len(ctx->name_price, len)`; inputs present / unlocked (`unlock < height`) / owned / native; Σin == Σout + fee + price. Effects: change CREATEs, ONE NAME CREATE PRE_ABSENT (owner ‖ registered_height BE, 72 B), ONE pool SET (+fee+price), input DELETEs. 0 / -1 verdict / -2 engine breakage. |
| `static int rtn_core_name_fetch(...)`, `static int rtn_core_nameown_fetch(...)` | **NEW (internal).** `v2_names` by name / by owner, keys bound with `sqlite3_bind_blob`. 0 found / 1 absent / -1 fault. |
| `RTN_CORE_OPS` / `NODUS_RT_CORE_ADAPTER.n_ops` 4 → 6 | **CHANGED.** + op 5 NAME (CREATE, PRE_ABSENT, key 3..36, value 72) and op 6 NAMEOWN (read-only, key 64, value ≤ 36). `rtn_core_probe` / `rtn_core_read` / `rtn_core_mutate` serve them (mutate: strict INSERT, byte rule and height re-checked). |
| `int nodus_rt_core_name_owner_key(const dna_env_view_t *env, uint16_t leg_index, const nodus_rt_auth_verdict_t *verdict, uint32_t *op_id_out, uint8_t key_out[64])` (`nodus_witness_rt_native.h`) | **NEW.** The CheckTx-only synthetic OWNER conflict key of a NAME_REGISTER leg: (op 6, `signer_fp[0]`). 0 key filled / 1 not a CORE op-8 leg / -1 bad args or owner shape. |
| `nodus_rt_leg_desc_t.name_len`, `.name[36]`, `.name_price` | **NEW fields.** Filled by `nodus_rt_native_describe_leg` for op 8 (name not NUL-terminated); the price is never in `burned`. |

Mempool-only, outside every hash. |

### 15.7 Queries and client functions (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus.h`, `nodus/src/client/nodus_client.c`, `nodus_witness_handlers.c`)

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `static void handle_dnac_ruleset_info(...)`, `handle_dnac_name_lookup(...)`, `handle_dnac_name_of(...)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Methods `dnac_ruleset_info`, `dnac_name_lookup`, `dnac_name_of`. No version-3 chain → `NOT_FOUND`; bad argument → `PROTOCOL_ERROR`; store fault or a registry that does not resolve one generation → `INTERNAL_ERROR`. **CHANGED (update-required warning, design 2026-10-20 §1 A):** `dnac_ruleset_info` answers 11 keys — + `ng` (u32) / `nh` (u64), the pending generation switch (see the next row); an unreadable switch history → `INTERNAL_ERROR` "generation-switch history unreadable". |
| `static const RULESET_SWITCH_PARAMS[]`, `static int ruleset_next_switch(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t tip, uint32_t *ng, uint64_t *nh)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c`) | **NEW (internal).** The build's generation-switch params → target generation (9 → `NODUS_RT_GEN_2`, 14 → `NODUS_RT_GEN_EVM`, 18 → `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE`, 22 → `NODUS_RT_GEN_ROOTS`; the engine's edges in `nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`). `ruleset_next_switch`: the `chain_config_history` row of those params with `effective_block > tip`, `ORDER BY effective_block, param_id LIMIT 1` → (target generation, effective height); 0 with both 0 when none; -1 on a prepare/step fault, a non-integer column, a param the table does not map or `tip > INT64_MAX`. |
| `static void handle_dnac_fee_info(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** 7 keys — + `np` (array of 4: the computed prices at tip + 1 for 3 / 4 / 5 / 6+ characters) and `ns` (≤ 16 maps `{p, v, e}`: params 10-13 rows effective above tip + 1, ascending (effective, param)); an unreadable price or schedule → `INTERNAL_ERROR`. |
| `NODUS_DNAC_NAME_SCHED_MAX` (16u), `nodus_dnac_ruleset_info_t { tip; generation; sys_version; sys_hash[64]; core_version; core_hash[64]; policy_digest[64]; gen2_height; d2; next_generation; next_height; }` (`next_generation` uint32_t / `next_height` uint64_t: the optional `ng` / `nh`, 0 = no pending switch or an older node — added by the update-required warning, design 2026-10-20 §1 A), `nodus_dnac_name_result_t { found; owner[129]; name[37]; registered_height; committed_height; }`, `nodus_dnac_name_prices_t { price[4]; n_sched; sched[16] { param_id; value; effective; }; }` | **NEW.** |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_ruleset_info(nodus_client_t *client, nodus_dnac_ruleset_info_t *out)` | **NEW.** 0 / the node's `NODUS_ERR_*` (an older node: "unknown DNAC method" — fail closed) / `NODUS_ERR_TIMEOUT` / -1 invalid args or transport failure. |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_name_lookup(nodus_client_t *client, const char *name, nodus_dnac_name_result_t *out)` | **NEW.** `name` must already be lowercase. Same returns. |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_name_of(nodus_client_t *client, const char *owner_hex, nodus_dnac_name_result_t *out)` | **NEW.** `owner_hex` = 128 lowercase hex. Same returns. |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_name_prices(nodus_client_t *client, nodus_dnac_name_prices_t *out)` | **NEW.** `dnac_fee_info`'s `np` / `ns`; an older node (neither key) → `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` — a price is never guessed. |
| `int nodus_dnac_ruleset_info_decode(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, nodus_dnac_ruleset_info_t *out)`, `int nodus_dnac_name_result_decode(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, int is_name_of, nodus_dnac_name_result_t *out)`, `int nodus_dnac_name_prices_decode(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, nodus_dnac_name_prices_t *out)` | **NEW.** The raw-reply decoders of the four functions above, exported for tests. 0 / -1 malformed. **CHANGED:** `nodus_dnac_ruleset_info_decode` also reads `ng` (≤ UINT32_MAX) / `nh` as OPTIONAL keys — absent = 0, a duplicate or out-of-range value refused; the nine original keys stay required. |
| `int nodus_ruleset_compat(const nodus_dnac_ruleset_info_t *ri, uint32_t max_generation_supported, char *msg, size_t msg_len)` (`nodus.h`, `nodus_client.c`) | **NEW.** Pure (no network, no clock). 2 = UPDATE REQUIRED (`ri->generation > max`, checked first); 1 = UPDATE BEFORE (`ri->next_generation > max` and `ri->next_height > ri->tip`; the message names the generation, the block and `next_height − tip` blocks); 0 = OK; -1 = `ri == NULL`. Writes a one-line English message ending "Install the current release." to `msg` for 1 / 2, `""` for 0 (nothing when `msg == NULL` or `msg_len == 0`; truncated, NUL-terminated). A warning only — never selects what is built. |
| `nodus_dnac_v3_item_t.name[37]`, `.name_price` | **NEW fields.** `dnac_v3_block` optional keys `nm` / `pr` (applied NAME_REGISTER only, decoded as a pair); `""` = not a registration. Op string `"name_register"`. |
| `AHD_KINDS` (`nodus_client.c`) | **CHANGED.** + `"name"`. An older client refuses the kind and fails closed on the page. |

### 15.9 Shared name registration builder (`nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_name.h`, `nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_name.c`)

I/O-free, like the SPEND (§11) and staking (§13) builders: nodus-cli `name register` and the web wallet's `send.wasm` (`nsw_name_build`, `nsw_name_offline_build`; networked builds only) build every CORE op-8 NAME_REGISTER envelope with it. The caller asks the node for the generation (>= 2), "taken", "one name per ID" and the price; the builder never guesses a price.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_V2_NAME_MAX_IN` (13u), `NODUS_V2_NAME_MAX_OUTS` (1u), `NODUS_V2_NAME_CALL_MAX`, `NODUS_V2_NAME_MAX_PASSES` (8) | **NEW.** Input cap (16 reads − pool, NAME, OWNER), the change output, the largest call, the fee fixed-point pass limit. |
| `NODUS_V2_NAME_ERR_NAME` (−50), `NODUS_V2_NAME_ERR_PRICE` (−51), `NODUS_V2_NAME_ERR_FEE_FLOOR` (−52) | **NEW.** Refusals beyond the shared `nodus_v2_spend_rc_t` values. |
| `int nodus_v2_name_normalize(const char *in, char out[DNAC_NAME_MAX_LEN + 1])` | **NEW (was nodus-cli `t8_name_lower` + the byte-rule check).** ASCII-only A–Z → a–z, then `dnac_name_bytes_ok`. 0 / −1. |
| `int nodus_v2_name_price_for(const uint64_t price[4], size_t name_len, uint64_t *out)` | **NEW.** The tier (3, 4, 5, 6+ characters) from the node's `dnac_fee_info` prices. 0 / −1 (length outside 3..36). |
| `void nodus_v2_name_effect_decl(uint32_t n_in, uint32_t n_out, uint32_t name_len, uint32_t *effects_out, uint32_t *bytes_out)` | **NEW (was nodus-cli `t8_name_effect_decl`).** effects = in + out + 2, bytes = 23 + 84·effects + 348·out + (name_len + 72) + 9 + 64·in. |
| `int nodus_v2_name_build(const nodus_v2_name_req_t *req, nodus_v2_name_built_t *out, nodus_v2_name_err_t *err)` | **NEW (the body of `cmd_name_register`).** Coins filtered (zero / non-native / locked at tip + 1), largest first, ≤ 13 inputs; fee = floor (or a fixed fee), raised to units × gas price in ≤ 8 passes, units = `nodus_v2_spend_ceiling` with inputs + 3 reads; one change seed from `req->rand` per pass that writes a change; two-pass signature; read-back and refusal on any difference. `NODUS_V2_SPEND_OK` or a refusal. |
| `int nodus_v2_name_decode(const uint8_t *env, size_t env_len, nodus_v2_name_decoded_t *out)` | **NEW.** Strict decode of a registration built by this module (one CORE op-8 leg, kind-1 single signer, name rule, price > 0, ascending inputs ≤ 13, ≤ 1 native change, exact effect declaration). |
| `void nodus_v2_name_built_free(nodus_v2_name_built_t *b)` | **NEW.** NULL-safe. |

## 16. Validator list — the per-validator delegator count (`dnac_validator_list_query` "dlg")

A read-only display field: how many of a validator's `NODUS_MAX_DELEGATORS_PER_VALIDATOR` (2048, `nodus/include/nodus/nodus_types.h`) delegation slots are filled. No consensus path reads it.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `nodus_dnac_validator_list_entry_t` (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus_types.h`) | **NEW fields, appended:** `uint8_t has_delegator_count` (1 = the node answered a count), `uint32_t delegator_count`. An older node's reply leaves `has_delegator_count` 0 = unknown (not 0 delegators). `dnac_validator_list_entry_t` (`dnac.h`) is unchanged — `dnac_validator_list` copies field by field. |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_validator_list(nodus_client_t *client, int filter_status, int offset, int limit, nodus_dnac_validator_list_result_t *result_out)` (`nodus_client.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour).** Decodes the optional per-entry `dlg`; a `dlg` that is not a uint, exceeds `UINT32_MAX` or repeats in one entry → `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` (result freed). Signature unchanged. The reply decoder is now the file-static `dnac_validator_list_parse(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, nodus_dnac_validator_list_result_t *result_out)`. |
| `int nodus_client_test_parse_validator_list(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, nodus_dnac_validator_list_result_t *out)` (`nodus_client.c`, `NODUS_CLIENT_TEST_SEAM` only) | **NEW (test seam).** Zeroes `out`, then `dnac_validator_list_parse`. Never in libnodus. |
| `static void handle_dnac_validator_list_query(...)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour).** Each entry map gains `dlg` = `nodus_delegation_count_by_validator` (the same `COUNT(*) FROM delegations WHERE validator_hash = ?` as the chain's DELEGATE cap read, `rtn_sys_delegcnt_fetch`); all counts are read before encoding, and a failed count is an error reply, never 0. |
| `int dna_chain_cmd_validator_list(dnac_context_t *ctx, int filter_status)` (`messenger/cli/cli_dna_chain_impl.c`) | **CHANGED (output).** New `DELEGATORS` column: `N/2048` when the node answered, `?` otherwise. Signature unchanged. |

## 17. Shared vaults — the general-multisig client library (`nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_msig.h`, `nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_msig.c`; decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-09-29-general-multisig.md`)

I/O-free like §11 / §13 / §15.9: the bodies of nodus-cli `msig address`, `v2-envelope spend --msig`, `msig sign` and `msig combine`, moved out unchanged in behaviour (nodus-cli calls them); the web wallet's `send.wasm` compiles the same file (`web-wallet/crypto/nodus-send-wasm.c` "VAULTS"). No stdio, no clock, no global RNG (output seeds from the caller's `rand`). Test: `nodus/tests/test_v2_msig.c`. The v2 multi-leg functions (`*2`, the HF-6 multisig owner — §5b.23) live in the same file; since then it includes `client/nodus_v2_stake.h` (review2 decodes the owner operation with `nodus_v2_stake_decode`), so any build of `nodus_v2_msig.c` must also compile `nodus_v2_stake.c` (the wallet's `send.wasm` already does).

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_V2_MSIG_EXPORT_MAGIC` ("nodus-msig-export v1"), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_SIG_MAGIC` ("nodus-msig-sig v1"), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_PK_LEN` (2592), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_SIG_LEN` (4627), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_BUILD_OUTS` (2), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_MAX_OUTS` (16), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_SIG_TEXT_MAX` (32768) | **NEW.** The two text formats' first lines, key/signature widths, the outputs the builder writes / a read-back accepts (rt_native.c `RTN_SPEND_MAX_OUT`), the largest signature text. |
| `NODUS_V2_MSIG_ERR_DESC` (−60) … `NODUS_V2_MSIG_ERR_EXPIRY` (−74), `NODUS_V2_MSIG_EXPIRY_AHEAD` (90u) | **NEW.** Refusals beyond the shared `nodus_v2_spend_rc_t`: DESC, SIGNERS (K ∉ [M, N]), COIN (zero / duplicate), FEE_FLOOR, FORMAT (text), SHAPE (not one CORE SPEND leg, kind 3, K slots + ONE descriptor), DIGEST (exported ≠ re-derived), NOT_MEMBER, CALL (lengths), SIG_DIGEST, SIG, COUNT, DUP_SIGNER, RULESET, EXPIRY (expiry 0 or past the export's tip + 90 — the co-signer window, static-asserted = `NODUS_CMT_APP_MAX_EXPIRY_AHEAD` − 10). |

The caller's keys are unchanged. |
| `int nodus_v2_msig_unsigned_auth(uint32_t signers, const uint8_t *desc, size_t desc_len, uint8_t *out, size_t cap, size_t *len_out)` | **NEW.** count K ‖ K × 7219 zero ‖ 1 ‖ dlen u16 BE ‖ descriptor — the unsigned auth_kind-3 blob (the builder's, and what the wallet rebuilds from its own vault code). |

| `void nodus_v2_msig_built_free(nodus_v2_msig_built_t *b)` | **NEW.** NULL-safe. |

| `int nodus_v2_msig_leg_open(const nodus_v2_msig_export_t *x, dna_env_view_t *v, nodus_v2_msig_leg_t *leg)` | **NEW (was `msig_leg_open`).** One CORE SPEND leg, kind 3, K slots, ONE valid descriptor filling the blob; `leg->desc/keys` point into `x->env`. |
| `int nodus_v2_msig_core_version(const uint8_t *env, size_t env_len, uint32_t *version_out)` | **NEW.** The CORE leg's `ruleset_version` (the caller picks its tuple). |
| `int nodus_v2_msig_digest(const nodus_v2_msig_export_t *x, uint32_t core_version, const uint8_t core_hash[64], dna_env_preflight_t *pf)` | **NEW (was `msig_digest`, re-parametrised on the tuple).** `dna_env_preflight` at tip + 1. |

| `int nodus_v2_msig_sig_encode(...)` / `int nodus_v2_msig_sig_parse(...)` | **NEW (was the signature file writes / reads).** nodus-cli's signature text. |

| `int nodus_v2_msig_assemble(nodus_v2_msig_export_t *x, const dna_env_view_t *v, const uint8_t (*pks)[2592], const uint8_t (*sigs)[4627], int n_sig)` | **NEW (was the selection sort of `cmd_msig_combine`).** Exactly K, ascending by key, a key twice refused. |

Does NOT run the chain's auth hook (nodus-cli does after it; the browser cannot link it). |
| `static int msig_write_text(...)`, `static const nodus_domain_runtime_t *msig_core_rt(...)` (`nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c`) | **NEW (internal).** File write; the compiled CORE entry an export names (`cli_core_runtime_for_env`) with its message. |
| `static int msig_export_read(const char *path, nodus_v2_msig_export_t *x)`, `static int msig_leg_open(const nodus_v2_msig_export_t *x, dna_env_view_t *v, nodus_v2_msig_leg_t *leg)`, `static int msig_digest(const nodus_v2_msig_export_t *x, dna_env_preflight_t *pf)` (nodus-cli) | **CHANGED (internal signatures):** wrappers over the library that print the CLI's messages. **REMOVED:** `msig_hex_line`, `msig_kv`, `msig_kv_hex`, `msig_key_in`, `msig_pk_cmp`, the `msig_export_t` type — moved into the library. |
| `nsw_msig_*` (`web-wallet/crypto/nodus-send-wasm.c` "VAULTS") | **NEW (WASM exports).** Members (`_member_reset/_add_self/_add` — key from the signed profile), `_create`, `_load`, getters (`_addr`, `_desc_hex`, `_m`, `_n`, `_member`, `_is_member`), `_balance` (`dnac_balance`), coins (`_coins_reset/_coin_add`) and `_scan` (`dnac_v3_block`, 200 blocks per call; `_scan_next/_tip`, coin and event getters), `_build`, request parts (`_prop_in`, `_prop_*`, `_text`), `_review` + `_rv_*`, `_sign`, `_sig_reset/_sig_add/_sig_count/_sig_signer`, `_submit` (combine + `dnac_spend`), `_intent`, `_wire`. **Fix round 2026-10-03:** the review and `_sign` REFUSE a request spending any coin not in the vault's loaded coin set (F1); the review also refuses an expiry past the node's tip + 90 (F3); `int nsw_msig_sig_add(const char *text, const char *sender_hex)` — **CHANGED signature:** sender must equal SHA3-512(signer key), the approval is verified before it is kept, one verified per key; 0 kept / 1 already held from this key / −1 refused (F2); `_scan` reads at most 64 pages of a block and stops after the block that passes 4096 items, and never adds a coin id twice (F6). TEST builds only: `nsw_test_msig_member_add_pk`, `nsw_test_msig_build`, `nsw_test_msig_review`, `nsw_test_msig_consume`, `nsw_test_msig_seed_pk`, `nsw_test_msig_seed_sign`. |

## 18. Component split S1 — the witness host seam (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-01-nodus-component-split.md`; `nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_host.h`, `nodus/src/server/nodus_chain_backend.h`, `nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_network_file.h`)

The witness sees its process only through `nodus_witness_host_t`; the server reaches the witness only through `nodus_chain_backend_t` (`srv->chain`, replacing `srv->witness`). No behaviour change in the combined `nodus-server`. Narrative: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §10 "Component split".

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `typedef struct nodus_witness_host { const nodus_identity_t *identity; nodus_witness_host_config_t config; nodus_witness_find_session_conn_fn find_session_conn; void *ctx; } nodus_witness_host_t;` (`nodus_witness_host.h`) | **NEW.** `identity` non-owning, outlives the witness. `config` = `bind_ip[64]`, `external_ip[64]`, `witness_port`, `data_path[256]`, `seq_dir[256]` (dir of `nodus.addr_seq`; "" = `data_path`), `p2p`, `has_v2_genesis_pin`, `v2_genesis_pin[32]`, `addr_history_index`. |
| `typedef struct nodus_tcp_conn *(*nodus_witness_find_session_conn_fn)(void *ctx, const uint8_t pk[NODUS_PK_BYTES], const uint8_t token[NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN])` | **NEW.** The connection of the client session authenticated as `pk` with `token`, or NULL (gone / re-authenticated / never existed). Used by the delayed `dnac_cc_collect` reply (`cc_collect_session_conn`, `nodus_witness_chain_config.c`). |
| `NODUS_PARTIAL_WIPE_GENESIS_MARKER` (`".witness_db_seen"`) | **MOVED** from `nodus_server.h` to `nodus_witness_host.h`; value unchanged. |
| `const struct nodus_witness_host *host` (field of `nodus_witness_t`, `nodus_witness.h`) | **CHANGED:** replaces `struct nodus_server *server`. Every `w->server->identity.X` is now `w->host->identity->X`; config reads are `w->host->config.*`. |
| `int nodus_witness_init(nodus_witness_t *witness, const struct nodus_witness_host *host, const nodus_witness_config_t *config)` (`nodus_witness.h`) | **CHANGED SIGNATURE:** second parameter was `struct nodus_server *server`. -1 on NULL `witness` / `host` / `host->identity` / `config`. Stores `host` (not copied — must outlive the witness); `data_path` from `host->config.data_path`; the p2p host's `seq_dir` = `host->config.seq_dir` or, if empty, `data_path`. Called through the chain backend. |
| `void nodus_server_witness_host(nodus_server_t *srv, nodus_witness_host_t *out)` (`nodus_server.h`, `nodus_server.c`) | **NEW.** Fills `*out` (memset first): `identity = &srv->identity`; config copied from `srv->config` by `nodus_server_witness_host_config` (since S3, §17) with `seq_dir = identity_path` (the address-record sequence file stays in the identity directory); `find_session_conn = server_find_session_conn`, `ctx = srv`. A caller that edits `srv->config` afterwards must fill again. The `_Static_assert`s pinning the array sizes moved with the copy to `nodus_server.h` in S3. |
| `static struct nodus_tcp_conn *server_find_session_conn(void *ctx, const uint8_t pk[NODUS_PK_BYTES], const uint8_t token[NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN])` (`nodus_server.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Scans `srv->sessions[]` for `authenticated && conn && token match && client_pk match`; the former body of `cc_collect_session_conn`, moved unchanged. |
| `typedef struct { tick; read_pending; status; chain_open; listen_port; dispatch_dnac; cc_collect; session_closed; close; } nodus_chain_backend_ops_t;` + `struct nodus_chain_backend { const nodus_chain_backend_ops_t *ops; }` (`nodus_chain_backend.h:52-110`) | **NEW in S1, CHANGED in S3** (`read_pending` and `session_closed` added, `dispatch_dnac` gained `client_pk` / `token`). `void tick(b)` — one pass of the chain's work per server loop iteration; `bool read_pending(b)` — **S3:** true while the backend's own transport has connections on its pending-read list, so the server's polls wait 0 ms (in-process: always false); `void status(b, nodus_t2_status_info_t *info)` (untouched when no chain is open); `bool chain_open(b)` (arms the partial-wipe gate); `int listen_port(b, bool *opened)`; `void dispatch_dnac(b, struct nodus_tcp_conn *conn, const uint8_t client_pk[NODUS_PK_BYTES], const uint8_t token[NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN], const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, const char *method, uint32_t txn_id)` — **S3 signature:** the requesting session's key and token (in-process ignores them and reads `conn`; IPC sends them in the session preface); `void cc_collect(b, conn, const uint8_t client_pk[NODUS_PK_BYTES], const uint8_t token[NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN], const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, uint32_t txn_id)`; `void session_closed(b, struct nodus_tcp_conn *conn)` — **S3:** the client session in `conn`'s slot ended or the slot starts a new session; later `find_session_conn` must return NULL (in-process: nothing; IPC: closes the session socket); `void close(b)` (frees `b`). Implementations: `nodus_chain_backend_inproc.c`, `nodus_chain_backend_ipc.c` (§17). |
| `nodus_chain_backend_t *chain` (field of `nodus_server_t`, `nodus_server.h`) | **CHANGED:** replaces `nodus_witness_t *witness`. NULL when the witness failed to initialise: no `dnac_*` served (`NODUS_CHAIN_NO_WITNESS_MSG`, `"witness module not enabled"`, §17), no chain in `status`. |
| `static bool inproc_read_pending(nodus_chain_backend_t *b)`, `static void inproc_session_closed(nodus_chain_backend_t *b, struct nodus_tcp_conn *conn)` (`nodus_chain_backend_inproc.c`) | **NEW (internal, S3).** Always `false`; no-op (the in-process witness looks sessions up in the server's own table, which the server has just cleared). `inproc_status` now calls `nodus_witness_status_fill` (§17); `inproc_dispatch_dnac` takes and ignores `client_pk` / `token`. |
| `int nodus_chain_backend_inproc_open(const nodus_witness_host_t *host, const nodus_witness_config_t *config, nodus_chain_backend_t **out)` (`nodus_chain_backend.h`, `nodus_chain_backend_inproc.c`) | **NEW.** Allocates backend + witness, copies `*host` into the backend (the witness points at that copy), runs `nodus_witness_init`. 0 `*out` set; -1 witness refused (nothing allocated, `*out` NULL); -2 out of memory. |
| `int nodus_chain_backend_inproc_check_pin(const char *data_path, const uint8_t pin[32])` | **NEW.** `nodus_witness_check_chain_pin` for the server's pre-open start check. 0 no chain DB or match; -1 refuse to start. |
| `int nodus_witness_read_chain_id(const char *db_path, uint8_t out[32])` (`nodus_witness_network_file.h`) | **RENAMED + MOVED** from `nodus_server_read_chain_id` (`nodus_server.c`). Committed chain id of the version-3 DB at `db_path`, read-only handle via `nodus_witness_v2_gen_stored_chain_id`. 0 / -1. Used by `nodus-server --derive-v2-genesis`. |
| `int nodus_witness_check_chain_pin(const char *data_path, const uint8_t pin[32])` (`nodus_witness_network_file.h`) | **RENAMED + MOVED** from `nodus_server_check_chain_pin`. The DB the witness would open (lexicographically smallest canonical `witness_<32 lowercase hex>.db`) must be a readable version-3 chain whose id equals `pin`. 0 none present or match; -1 different / unreadable (logged). |
| `typedef struct { nodus_p2p_config_t *p2p; bool *has_v2_genesis_pin; uint8_t *v2_genesis_pin; bool *has_network_pin; uint8_t *network_pin; } nodus_network_file_target_t;` (`nodus_witness_network_file.h`, `NODUS_HAS_JSONC`) | **NEW.** The config fields a network file sets, by reference. |
| `int nodus_network_file_apply(const nodus_network_file_t *nf, const nodus_network_file_target_t *t)` | **CHANGED SIGNATURE + MOVED** (was `nodus_server_config_t *cfg`, in `nodus_server.c`). Merges the file's persistent peers into `*t->p2p` (no duplicates; list full → -1); a file pin arms `has_v2_genesis_pin` and `has_network_pin`; a pin differing from an already-given `--v2-genesis-pin` → -1. -1 also on any NULL pointer in `t`. |
| `static inline nodus_network_file_target_t nodus_server_network_file_target(nodus_server_config_t *cfg)` (`nodus_server.h`, `NODUS_HAS_JSONC`) | **NEW in S1; `static inline` in the header since S3** (no longer defined in `nodus_server.c`, so `tools/nodus_node_config.c` does not pull `nodus_server.c` into `nodus-witness`). The target view over `cfg->p2p`, `has_v2_genesis_pin`, `v2_genesis_pin`, `has_network_pin`, `network_pin`. |
| `int nodus_network_file_load(const char *path, nodus_network_file_t *out)`, `int nodus_network_file_write_pin(const char *path, const uint8_t chain32[32])`, `nodus_network_file_t` | **MOVED** to `nodus_witness_network_file.{h,c}` (from `nodus_server.{h,c}`); signatures and rules unchanged. |
| `int nodus_server_check_partial_wipe(const char *data_path)` | **UNCHANGED, stays in `nodus_server.c`** (decision item 9: the gate is core's). Reads the marker name from `nodus_witness_host.h`. |

## 19. Component split S3 — the `nodus-witness` process + the IPC chain backend (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-01-nodus-component-split.md` items 5, 7, 10, 11, 18, 19, 20, 21; `nodus/src/server/nodus_chain_backend.h`, `nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_ipc.h`, `nodus/tools/nodus_node_config.h`)

Opt-in (`witness_external`); the default combined `nodus-server` is unchanged. Core reaches the witness of the separate `nodus-witness` process over `<data_path>/witness.sock` (plaintext nodus wire frames, S2 Unix socket entry, same uid both ends). Not for production yet (no unit file / installer). Narrative, frame shapes and limits: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §10 "Component split", S3.

**Core side** (`nodus_chain_backend.h`, `nodus_chain_backend_ipc.c`, `nodus_server.{h,c}`):

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_CHAIN_NO_WITNESS_MSG` (`"witness module not enabled"`, `nodus_chain_backend.h`) | **NEW.** The error text (code `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR`) a `dnac_*` request gets when no witness can serve it (decision item 20): a server with no chain backend (`dispatch_t2`, which used the same literal before) and the IPC backend when `nodus-witness` cannot be reached, the session cap is reached, or the session socket is over its queue bound — the same bytes. |
| `NODUS_CHAIN_IPC_MAX_SESSION_CONNS` (`NODUS_TCP_MAX_CONNS - 64` = 960) | **NEW.** Most client sessions holding a socket to `nodus-witness` at once; the 64 kept slots hold the control connection and closed-but-not-yet-seen session sockets on the witness end. A session beyond it gets `NODUS_CHAIN_NO_WITNESS_MSG` (one WARN until the count drops below). |
| `int nodus_chain_backend_ipc_open(const char *data_path, nodus_chain_backend_t **out)` | **NEW.** The IPC backend for `<data_path>/witness.sock` (`"/tmp"` when `data_path` is empty). Opens no socket: the control connection is dialled on the first `tick`, re-dialled with backoff 250 ms doubling to 5000 ms while down, and queried with `ipc_status` every 1000 ms; a session socket is dialled on that session's first `dnac_*`, only while the control connection is up, and starts with the `ipc_hello` preface. `status` / `chain_open` / `listen_port` report the last `ipc_status` answer only while the control connection is up and the answer is younger than 5000 ms. A request whose session socket has ≥ `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_QUEUE_MAX` queued gets `NODUS_CHAIN_NO_WITNESS_MSG` and the socket stays open; a failed forward send closes the socket and gets the same error. Returns 0 (`*out` set); -1 bad argument or socket path too long (`*out` NULL; `nodus_server_init` then fails); -2 out of memory. |
| `static int ipc_status_decode(const uint8_t *p, size_t len, nodus_witness_ipc_status_t *out)`, `static const nodus_witness_ipc_status_t *ipc_snap(ipc_backend_t *)`, `static void ipc_ctl_send_q(ipc_backend_t *, const char *q)`, `static void ipc_ctl_tick(ipc_backend_t *)`, `static void ipc_session_close(ipc_backend_t *, ipc_session_t *)`, `static nodus_tcp_conn_t *ipc_session_conn(ipc_backend_t *, nodus_tcp_conn_t *client, const uint8_t pk[NODUS_PK_BYTES], const uint8_t token[NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN])`, `static void ipc_no_witness(nodus_tcp_conn_t *client, uint32_t txn_id)`, `static void ipc_forward(...)`, `static void ipc_on_frame(...)`, `static void ipc_on_disconnect(...)`, `static void ipcb_*(...)` (the nine ops) (`nodus_chain_backend_ipc.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Strict 7-key `ipc_status` decoder; the freshness gate; control-frame send; control dial/backoff/query; session-socket close; session-socket lookup/dial/preface (closes a socket of another connection or token in that slot; NULL when the control connection is down, the cap is reached or the dial fails); the item-20 error reply; the forward with the per-session bound; relay of a session frame to its client unchanged / snapshot update; slot bookkeeping on close. |
| `bool witness_external` (field of `nodus_server_config_t`, `nodus_server.h`) | **NEW.** JSON `"witness_external"` (boolean, any other type refuses the start) / `--witness-external`; default false = in-process witness. True: `nodus_server_init` opens the IPC backend, no 4004 port, no chain DB; logs `WITNESS: external — served by the nodus-witness process over <data_path>/witness.sock`; `nodus_server_run` logs `  Witness port: external (nodus-witness)`. `nodus-witness` refuses to start without it. |
| `bool genesis_marker_armed` (field of `nodus_server_t`, `nodus_server.h`) | **NEW.** External mode only: `nodus_server_run` writes the partial-wipe marker once, the first time the witness reports an open chain, and sets this. Never read in the in-process mode. |
| `static void session_chain_closed(nodus_server_t *srv, nodus_tcp_conn_t *conn)` (`nodus_server.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `srv->chain->ops->session_closed` when there is a backend; called in `on_tcp_accept` before a reused slot's `session_clear` and in `on_tcp_disconnect` before `session_clear`. |
| `static void server_write_genesis_marker(const char *data_path)` (`nodus_server.c`) | **NEW (internal).** The marker write formerly inline at the end of `nodus_server_init` (not fatal; the same `NODUS_SRV: WARNING …` lines); now also called from `nodus_server_run` in external mode. |
| `static inline void nodus_server_witness_host_config(const nodus_server_config_t *cfg, nodus_witness_host_config_t *out)` (`nodus_server.h`) | **NEW.** The ONE copy of the witness's config subset (memset, then `bind_ip`, `external_ip`, `witness_port`, `data_path`, `seq_dir = identity_path`, `p2p`, `has_v2_genesis_pin`, `v2_genesis_pin`, `addr_history_index`), used by `nodus_server_witness_host` and by `nodus-witness`, which then empties `seq_dir` (so `nodus.addr_seq` lives in `data_path`, items 10 / 21). `static inline` so `nodus-witness` does not link `nodus_server.c`. |
| `void nodus_server_witness_host(...)`, `nodus_server_network_file_target(...)` | **CHANGED** — see §18 (the former now delegates to `nodus_server_witness_host_config`; the latter is `static inline`). |

**Witness side** (`nodus_witness_ipc.h`, `nodus_witness_ipc.c`; includes no server header):

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_SOCK_NAME` (`"witness.sock"`) | **NEW.** The socket file under `data_path`. |
| `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_Q_HELLO` (`"ipc_hello"`), `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_Q_CTL` (`"ipc_ctl"`), `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_Q_STATUS` (`"ipc_status"`) | **NEW.** Frame methods (`"q"`). Session preface `{"q":"ipc_hello","pk":<bstr NODUS_PK_BYTES>,"tk":<bstr NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN>}`; control preface `{"q":"ipc_ctl"}`; query `{"q":"ipc_status"}`; answer `{"q","co","h","sr"(64),"ci"(32),"po","lp"}`. |
| `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_CTL_FRAME_MAX` (512) | **NEW.** Largest control frame either side builds. |
| `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_QUEUE_MAX` (`(size_t)4 * 1024 * 1024`) | **NEW.** Core side, per session socket: at or above this many queued bytes toward the witness a request is answered `NODUS_CHAIN_NO_WITNESS_MSG` and not queued. Checked before queueing (one max frame still fits an idle socket). |
| `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_REPLY_QUEUE_MAX` (`(size_t)16 * 1024 * 1024`) | **NEW.** Witness side, per connection: a frame read while this many reply bytes are queued toward core closes the connection instead of being dispatched. |
| `static inline int nodus_witness_ipc_sock_path(const char *data_path, char *out, size_t cap)` | **NEW.** `"<data_path or /tmp>/witness.sock"`; 0, or -1 when it does not fit `cap`. |
| `typedef struct { bool chain_open; uint64_t height; uint8_t state_root[64]; uint8_t chain_id[32]; bool p2p_opened; uint16_t listen_port; } nodus_witness_ipc_status_t;` | **NEW.** What the control connection answers. |
| `typedef struct { void (*dispatch_dnac)(void *ctx, struct nodus_tcp_conn *conn, const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, const char *method, uint32_t txn_id); void (*cc_collect)(void *ctx, struct nodus_tcp_conn *conn, const uint8_t client_pk[NODUS_PK_BYTES], const uint8_t token[NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN], const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, uint32_t txn_id); void (*status)(void *ctx, nodus_witness_ipc_status_t *out); void *ctx; } nodus_witness_ipc_handlers_t;` | **NEW.** Where the listener hands its work; production binds them to a witness, tests bind their own. |
| `typedef struct nodus_witness_ipc nodus_witness_ipc_t;` | **NEW.** Opaque listener (own `nodus_tcp_t` + per-slot kind/pk/token). |
| `void nodus_witness_status_fill(struct nodus_witness *w, nodus_t2_status_info_t *info)` | **NEW** (moved from `inproc_status`). `block_height`, `state_root` (the committed global root of the `v2_blocks` tip on a successor chain, zero without a committed row) and `chain_id`; `info` untouched when no chain is open. Shared by the in-process backend and the `ipc_status` answer. |
| `void nodus_witness_ipc_handlers_for(struct nodus_witness *w, nodus_witness_ipc_handlers_t *out)` | **NEW.** Handlers serving `w` → `nodus_witness_dispatch_dnac`, `nodus_witness_handle_cc_collect`, and a status fill (`chain_open = w->db != NULL`, `p2p_opened = w->p2p != NULL`, `listen_port = nodus_witness_p2p_listen_port(w->p2p)`). `w` need not be initialised yet; it must be by the first poll. |
| `nodus_witness_ipc_t *nodus_witness_ipc_new(const nodus_witness_ipc_handlers_t *h)` | **NEW.** A listener not yet listening; NULL on a NULL handler or allocation failure. |
| `int nodus_witness_ipc_listen(nodus_witness_ipc_t *ipc, const char *path)` | **NEW.** `nodus_tcp_unix_listen(..., NODUS_TCP_UNIX_UID_SELF)` (0600, parent dir not group/other-writable, peers of this euid only). 0 / -1. |
| `void nodus_witness_ipc_poll(nodus_witness_ipc_t *ipc, int timeout_ms)` | **NEW.** One `nodus_tcp_poll` pass. First frame decides the kind (`ipc_hello` → session, records `peer_pk` / `peer_id` / `peer_id_set` from the preface key; `ipc_ctl` → control; anything else closes). Session frames: `nodus_t2_decode`, token must equal the preface token, `dnac_cc_collect` → `cc_collect`, other `dnac_*` → `dispatch_dnac`, anything else closes. Control frames: only `ipc_status`, answered with the 7-key map. Any frame on a connection at `NODUS_WITNESS_IPC_REPLY_QUEUE_MAX` closes it. |
| `struct nodus_tcp_conn *nodus_witness_ipc_find_session_conn(void *ctx, const uint8_t pk[NODUS_PK_BYTES], const uint8_t token[NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN])` | **NEW.** `nodus_witness_host_t.find_session_conn` in `nodus-witness` (`ctx` = the listener): the session connection whose preface named `pk` and `token` and that is not `close_pending`, else NULL (core closed it → the delayed `dnac_cc_collect` reply is dropped). |
| `void nodus_witness_ipc_free(nodus_witness_ipc_t *ipc)` | **NEW.** Closes every connection and the socket (file removed by the S2 dev/ino rule), frees. NULL-safe. |
| `static int ipc_parse_ctl(...)`, `static void ipc_refuse(...)`, `static void ipc_on_first_frame(...)`, `static void ipc_on_session_frame(...)`, `static void ipc_on_ctl_frame(...)`, `static void ipc_on_accept(...)`, `static void ipc_on_disconnect(...)`, `static size_t ipc_queued_bytes(const nodus_tcp_conn_t *)`, `static void ipc_on_frame(...)`, `static void witness_h_dispatch_dnac(...)`, `static void witness_h_cc_collect(...)`, `static void witness_h_status(...)` (`nodus_witness_ipc.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Strict control-map parser (1-3 entries of `q` / `pk` / `tk`, exact sizes, no repeats, no trailing bytes); close with WARN `closing IPC connection slot=<n>: <why>`; the three per-kind frame handlers; slot reset on accept/close; queued-byte count; the bound check + kind dispatch; the production handlers. |

**Process / config** (`nodus/tools/`):

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_node_config_load(int argc, char **argv, const char *title, nodus_server_config_t *out)` (`nodus_node_config.h`) | **NEW** (the option/config/network-file/derive code moved out of `tools/nodus-server.c`). Defaults, first option pass, JSON file (`-c`, `load_config_json`), second option pass (command line over file), network file. `--derive-v2-genesis` runs its offline one-shot here. `title` heads the `-h` text. Returns -1: start with `*out`; ≥ 0: exit with this code (`-h` → 0, a refused option or file → 1, or the derivation one-shot's result), `*out` untouched. Shared by `nodus-server` and `nodus-witness` (decision item 18). |
| `int main(...)` (`tools/nodus-witness.c`) | **NEW binary `nodus-witness`.** Requires `witness_external` (else exit 1); requires `identity_path`; exclusive `flock` on `<data_path>/nodus-witness.lock` (`NODUS_WITNESS_LOCK_NAME`) for its lifetime; network-file pin check; `nodus_identity_load_readonly`; host view = `nodus_server_witness_host_config` with `seq_dir` emptied, `find_session_conn = nodus_witness_ipc_find_session_conn`; `nodus_witness_init` (failure → exit 1); partial-wipe marker only when the chain is open AND `nodus.db` + `channels.db` exist; `nodus_witness_ipc_listen`; loop `nodus_witness_tick` + `nodus_witness_ipc_poll(ipc, (w->running && w->p2p) ? 0 : 50)` until SIGINT/SIGTERM, exit 0. |
| `static bool core_dbs_present(const char *data_path)`, `static void write_genesis_marker(const char *data_path)`, `static int take_data_lock(const char *data_path)`, `static void warn_addr_seq_not_migrated(const char *identity_path, const char *data_path)` (`tools/nodus-witness.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `nodus.db` and `channels.db` both present; the marker write (not fatal, `NODUS_WITNESS: WARNING …`); the lock (fd kept open, -1 logged when held / unopenable); one WARN when `<identity_path>/nodus.addr_seq` exists and `<data_path>/nodus.addr_seq` does not (installer step, item 21) — stat only, touches nothing. |
| `int main(...)` (`tools/nodus-server.c`) | **CHANGED:** now only `nodus_node_config_load(argc, argv, "Nodus Server", &config)` + `nodus_server_init` / `nodus_server_run` / `nodus_server_close`. |

## 20. Component split S4 — core/DHT state split + seam (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-01-nodus-component-split.md` items 2-4, 16, 17; `nodus/src/dht/nodus_dht.h`, `nodus/src/server/nodus_dht_backend.h`, `nodus/src/dht/nodus_dht_media.h`)

Inside the combined `nodus-server`, the DHT / storage half (routing, lookups, replication / republish / hinted handoff, subscriptions, batch forward, `nodus.db`, `channels.db`) has its own state `nodus_dht_t` and code in `nodus/src/dht/`; core reaches it only through `srv->dht` (`nodus_dht_backend_t`), the DHT reaches core only through `nodus_dht_host_t`. Stated as no behaviour change (commits `6185ff2b`, `b6bd6d3f`, `8eced563`). A session is an ORIGIN `{kind, slot}`, never a pointer. Narrative, crossing map, open S5 items: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §10 "Component split", S4.

**Types** (`nodus_dht.h`):

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `typedef enum { NODUS_DHT_ORIGIN_CLIENT = 0, NODUS_DHT_ORIGIN_INTER = 1 } nodus_dht_origin_kind_t;` + `typedef struct { nodus_dht_origin_kind_t kind; int slot; } nodus_dht_origin_t;` (`:399-419`) | **NEW.** The transport a request came in on (4001 / WebSocket vs 4002) and core's session slot. No generation, on purpose (`:410-415`): a later reply goes to whoever holds the slot then — pre-existing behaviour, OPEN in `nodus/BUGS.md`. |
| `typedef struct { const nodus_identity_t *identity; void *ctx; int (*send_to_origin)(void *ctx, nodus_dht_origin_t origin, const uint8_t *frame, size_t len); int (*udp_send)(void *ctx, const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, const char *ip, uint16_t port); int (*inter_send)(void *ctx, const char *ip, uint16_t port, const nodus_key_t *expected_peer_id, const uint8_t *frame, size_t flen); bool (*hint_wanted)(void *ctx, const nodus_key_t *node_id); } nodus_dht_host_t;` (`:427-463`) | **NEW.** DHT → core. `identity` read only, must outlive the DHT. `send_to_origin` HOT: one encoded frame to the slot's connection, returns the send's result (-1 no connection). `udp_send`: one raw-CBOR T1 datagram from UDP 4000, 0 / -1. `inter_send` HOT: one PRE-FRAMED frame over the node's persistent 4002 pool, dialing and pinning `expected_peer_id` when there is no connection; SYNCHRONOUS 0 / -1, the caller decides on a hint from it. `hint_wanted` COLD: true when `node_id` is a cluster member last seen less than `NODUS_HINT_OFFLINE_SKIP_SEC` ago. |
| `typedef struct { bool open; nodus_key_t listen_keys[NODUS_MAX_LISTEN_KEYS]; int listen_count; uint64_t rate_window_start; int puts_in_window; } nodus_dht_session_t;` (`:479-489`) | **NEW.** The DHT's shadow of one client session, by slot. `listen_keys` / `listen_count` / `rate_window_start` / `puts_in_window` were fields of `nodus_session_t` (**REMOVED** there, `nodus_server.h:198-199`). |
| `typedef struct { uint64_t sv_window_start; int sv_count; uint64_t fv_window_start; int fv_count; } nodus_dht_inter_session_t;` (`:494-499`) | **NEW.** The DHT's shadow of one 4002 session; the four fields were in `nodus_inter_session_t` (**REMOVED** there, `nodus_server.h:153-154`). `sv` and `m_sv` share the sv window. |
| `typedef enum { NODUS_DHT_PEER_PING = 0, NODUS_DHT_PEER_PONG_TOUCH = 1, NODUS_DHT_PEER_PONG = 2, NODUS_DHT_PEER_ALIVE = 3 } nodus_dht_peer_seen_t;` (`:504-520`) | **NEW.** PING: routing insert (ping-before-evict when full), then cancel that peer's pending eviction. PONG_TOUCH: routing touch, before core's cluster update. PONG: after it — cancel pending eviction, then insert. ALIVE: core's cluster saw a member turn ALIVE — plain `nodus_routing_insert`. |
| `typedef struct { char ip[64]; uint16_t tcp_port; } nodus_dht_peer_addr_t;` (`:524-527`) | **NEW.** One routing-table peer address — what presence sync dials. |
| `typedef struct nodus_dht { … } nodus_dht_t;` (`:531-578`) | **NEW.** `host`; `storage`, `media_storage`, `ch_store`, `routing`, `ring`; `storage_open` / `media_open` / `ch_open`; `sessions[NODUS_MAX_SESSIONS]`, `inter_sessions[NODUS_MAX_INTER_SESSIONS]`; `lookup_state`; `subscriptions`, `last_sub_cleanup`, `sub_renewal`; `bf_state`, `bf_fd_table[NODUS_BF_FD_TABLE_SIZE]`; `republish`, `media_republish`; `pending_evictions[NODUS_MAX_PENDING_EVICTIONS]`; `last_wal_checkpoint`, `last_vacuum`. Every one of these except `host`, the shadows and the open flags was a field of `nodus_server_t` (**REMOVED** there). |
| `nodus_dht_backend_t *dht` (field of `nodus_server_t`, `nodus_server.h:229-232`) | **NEW.** Replaces `storage`, `media_storage`, `ch_store`, `routing`, `ring`, `lookup_state`, `subscriptions`, `last_sub_cleanup`, `sub_renewal`, `bf_state`, `bf_fd_table`, `republish`, `media_republish`, `pending_evictions`, `last_wal_checkpoint`, `last_vacuum`. Set by `nodus_server_init`; NULL after close / a failed init. |
| `iterative_lookup_t.on_complete` — `void (*)(struct nodus_dht *dht, nodus_peer_t *closest, int count, void *user_data)` (`nodus_dht.h:135-137`) | **CHANGED:** first parameter was `struct nodus_server *srv`. |
| Subscription, lookup, batch-forward, Package A, eviction and republish types — `nodus_subscription_t`, `nodus_subscription_table_t`, `lookup_query_state_t`, `lookup_query_t`, `iterative_lookup_t`, `iterative_lookup_state_t`, `nodus_dht_cand_t`, `nodus_dht_src_page_t`, `nodus_dht_keyset_t`, `nodus_dht_merge_stats_t`, `nodus_dht_read_outcome_t`, `dht_bf_conn_t`, `dht_bf_batch_t`, `dht_bf_state_t`, `dht_bf_fd_entry_t`, `nodus_pending_eviction_t`, `dht_republish_state_t`, `dht_media_republish_state_t` (`nodus_dht.h:55-395`) | **MOVED** from `nodus_server.h`, unchanged except `on_complete` above. |
| Constants `NODUS_MAX_SESSIONS`, `NODUS_MAX_INTER_SESSIONS` (both `NODUS_TCP_MAX_CONNS`), `NODUS_MAX_LISTEN_KEYS` (128), `NODUS_MAX_SUBSCRIPTIONS` (4096), `NODUS_SUBSCRIPTION_TTL` (900), `NODUS_BF_MAX_FORWARDS` (8), `NODUS_BF_MAX_BATCHES` (4), `NODUS_BF_TIMEOUT_MS` (5000), `NODUS_DHT_VERIFY_CAP` (1024), `NODUS_DHT_MAX_SOURCES`, `NODUS_DHT_SRC_MAX_ROWS`, `NODUS_DHT_V_*`, `BF_*` states, `NODUS_BF_FD_TABLE_SIZE` (256), `NODUS_MAX_PENDING_EVICTIONS` (32), `NODUS_EVICT_PING_TIMEOUT` (10) | **MOVED** from `nodus_server.h` to `nodus_dht.h`, values unchanged. |

**Core → DHT entry points** (`nodus_dht.h`, `nodus_dht_server.c`; all run on the server's event-loop thread; `slot` out of range → nothing):

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `int nodus_dht_init(nodus_dht_t *dht, const nodus_dht_host_t *host)` | **NEW.** Phase one, BEFORE the identity is loaded: zero `dht`, copy `*host`, every BF fd to -1, jitter the first republish cycles, lookup txn counter = 1, create the BF epoll, clear its fd table. 0, or -1 (NULL argument, or the epoll could not be created — logged, nothing left open). |
| `int nodus_dht_open(nodus_dht_t *dht, const char *data_path, const char *self_ip, uint16_t self_peer_port)` | **NEW.** Phase two, after the identity: `<data_path>/nodus.db` (`"/tmp"` when empty), media storage on the same handle, `channels.db` + default channels, routing table keyed by `host.identity->node_id`, hash ring with this node at `self_ip:self_peer_port`. 0, or -1 when a database does not open (what opened stays open for `nodus_dht_close`). |
| `void nodus_dht_stop(nodus_dht_t *dht)` | **NEW.** Abandon every in-flight lookup (its `cb_data` freed) and every batch forward; close the BF epoll. |
| `void nodus_dht_close(nodus_dht_t *dht)` | **NEW.** Close the databases `nodus_dht_open` opened (media, values, channels) and the BF epoll if still open. |
| `void nodus_dht_session_opened(nodus_dht_t *dht, nodus_dht_origin_t origin)` / `void nodus_dht_session_closed(nodus_dht_t *dht, nodus_dht_origin_t origin)` | **NEW.** Zero the slot's shadow; `opened` also sets a client shadow's `open`. Lookups and batch forwards started for that slot notice on their next tick. |
| `void nodus_dht_client_request(nodus_dht_t *dht, int slot, const nodus_key_t *client_fp, const nodus_pubkey_t *client_pk, nodus_tier2_msg_t *msg)` | **NEW.** An authenticated client request with a DHT method → `handle_t2_put` / `_get` / `_get_all` / `_get_batch` / `_count_batch` / `_listen` / `_unlisten` / `_ch_list` / `_ch_search` / `_ch_get` (static) or the media handlers; any other method → `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` "unknown method". |
| `void nodus_dht_inter_request(nodus_dht_t *dht, int slot, const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, nodus_tier2_msg_t *msg)` | **NEW.** A 4002 T2 frame with `fv` (rate window, re-read from `payload` as T1), `get_batch` (local storage only) or `m_sv` (`msg->has_media`). Replies to (INTER, slot). |
| `void nodus_dht_inter_t1(nodus_dht_t *dht, int slot, const nodus_key_t *peer_fp, const char *peer_ip, nodus_tier1_msg_t *t1)` | **NEW.** A 4002 T1 frame after core's F2 gate: `sv`, `sub`, `unsub`, `ntf`. `peer_fp` = core's session identity (zero when none), `peer_ip` = "" when none. |
| `void nodus_dht_udp_request(nodus_dht_t *dht, const char *from_ip, uint16_t from_port, nodus_tier1_msg_t *msg)` | **NEW.** A UDP 4000 datagram that is not ping / pong: `fn`, `fn_r`, `sv`, `fv`; replies through `host.udp_send`. |
| `void nodus_dht_peer_seen(nodus_dht_t *dht, nodus_dht_peer_seen_t kind, const nodus_key_t *node_id, const char *ip, uint16_t udp_port, uint16_t tcp_port)` | **NEW.** See `nodus_dht_peer_seen_t`. Replaces the server's direct routing / eviction calls and the `nodus_routing_insert` in `nodus_cluster_on_pong`. |
| `void nodus_dht_peer_dead(nodus_dht_t *dht, const nodus_key_t *node_id)` | **NEW.** `nodus_routing_remove` — replaces the call `nodus_cluster_tick` made directly. |
| `int nodus_dht_hint_store(nodus_dht_t *dht, const nodus_key_t *node_id, const char *ip, uint16_t port, const uint8_t *frame, size_t len)` | **NEW.** `nodus_storage_hinted_insert` for core's pending-full hook: 0 parked, `NODUS_STORAGE_RC_QUOTA` a cap reached, other -1 (also -1 for a NULL `dht`). |
| `int nodus_dht_routing_snapshot(const nodus_dht_t *dht, nodus_dht_peer_addr_t *out, int max)` | **NEW.** Active routing entries in bucket order, at most `max`, read at the call; returns the count (0 on a NULL argument or `max <= 0`). Presence sync's peer list. |
| `void nodus_dht_evict_tick(nodus_dht_t *dht)` | **NEW.** Ping-before-evict sweep (`eviction_sweep`). |
| `void nodus_dht_tick(nodus_dht_t *dht)` | **NEW.** In order: hinted-handoff retry, subscription cleanup (60 s) and renewal, lookup engine, batch forward, bucket refresh, storage cleanup, republish, media republish, WAL checkpoint, incremental vacuum. |
| `static inline int nodus_dht_send_client(nodus_dht_t *dht, int slot, const uint8_t *frame, size_t len)` (`nodus_dht.h:704-708`) | **NEW.** `send_to_origin` to (CLIENT, slot); shared with the media TU. |

## 21. Server identity start-up — load or create, never replace (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-01-nodus-component-split.md` item 10; `nodus/src/server/nodus_server.h`)

| Function | Description |
|----------|-------------|
| `int nodus_server_identity_load_or_create(const char *path, nodus_identity_t *out)` (`nodus_server.h`, `nodus_server.c`) | **NEW.** Used by `nodus_server_init_ex` when `identity_path` is non-empty. `nodus_identity_load(path)` succeeds → **0**. It fails and neither `<path>/nodus.pk` nor `<path>/nodus.sk` exists (`lstat` → `ENOENT` for both) → generate + `nodus_identity_save` → **1** (WARN `created and saved a new one`); a failed generate or save → **-1**. It fails and either file exists, or `lstat` fails otherwise (unreadable, truncated, half-restored) → **-1** with an ERROR naming the path; nothing is generated and no file is written. `out` is securely cleared on every -1. `nodus_server_init` fails (`goto fail`) on -1. Before: any load failure generated a new identity and overwrote both key files; the save result was ignored. |
| `static bool identity_file_may_exist(const char *dir, const char *name)` (`nodus_server.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `lstat("<dir>/<name>")`: false only on `ENOENT`; true when it exists (a dangling symlink included), on any other error, or when the path does not fit 1024 bytes. |

## 22. Block retention + SeenCommit cleanup (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-03-block-pruning-7-paydays.md`; `nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_cmt_{app,node,store}.h`)

Node-local: none of these changes a consensus byte, block, app_hash or state root. Narrative: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Block retention (pruning) and the SeenCommit cleanup"; operation: `nodus/docs/DEPLOY_RUNBOOK.md` §2.5.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `nodus_witness_config_t.retain_blocks` (`int64_t`, `nodus_witness.h`) | **NEW field.** nodus.json `"retain_blocks"` (parsed in `nodus/tools/nodus_node_config.c`, `load_config_json`; a non-integer or negative value refuses the start). 0 (default) = archive. Reaches the witness as `nodus_server_config_t.witness` → `nodus_witness_init` (both binaries); not carried over the witness IPC socket. |
| `int64_t nodus_cmt_app_retain_height(int64_t height, int64_t retain_blocks)` (`nodus_witness_cmt_app.h`) | **NEW.** Pure. 0 when `retain_blocks <= 0` or `height <= retain_blocks`; else `height - retain_blocks + 1`, so `PruneBlocks` leaves exactly `retain_blocks` blocks. |
| `int nodus_cmt_app_commit(void *ctx, nodus_abci_response_commit_t *resp)` | **CHANGED (behaviour).** `resp->retain_height = nodus_cmt_app_retain_height(ctx->fb_height, ctx->w->config.retain_blocks)` (was always 0). Signature unchanged. |
| `nodus_cmt_app_ledger_t.fb_height` (`int64_t`) | **NEW field.** The height of the last successful `nodus_cmt_app_finalize_block` (set just before it returns CMT_OK); Commit's input. 0 before the first FinalizeBlock. |
| `int nodus_cmt_node_check_retain_blocks(int64_t retain_blocks, const cmt_evidence_params_t *ev)` (`nodus_witness_cmt_node.h`) | **NEW.** Pure. CMT_OK for 0 or `> ev->max_age_num_blocks`; CMT_REJECT for a negative value or 1..`max_age_num_blocks`; CMT_FAULT on NULL `ev`. |
| `int nodus_cmt_node_init(nodus_cmt_node_t *n, nodus_witness_t *w, const nodus_cmt_node_opts_t *opts)` | **CHANGED (behaviour).** Calls `nodus_cmt_bs_set_seen_commit_window(&n->store, n->config.double_sign_check_height)` after the consensus config is validated; and, right after the state loads (before the handshake), fails (CMT_FAULT, ERROR `retain_blocks <N> refused …`) when `nodus_cmt_node_check_retain_blocks(w->config.retain_blocks, &state->consensus_params.evidence)` is not CMT_OK; logs INFO `block retention: …` when N > 0. Signature unchanged. |
| `nodus_cmt_store_t.seen_commit_window` (`int64_t`, `nodus_witness_cmt_store.h`) | **NEW field.** W; 0 after `nodus_cmt_store_init`. |
| `int nodus_cmt_bs_set_seen_commit_window(nodus_cmt_store_t *s, int64_t w)` | **NEW.** Sets W. CMT_OK; CMT_FAULT on NULL or `w < 0` (W unchanged). |
| `int nodus_cmt_bs_prune_blocks(nodus_cmt_store_t *s, int64_t height, const cmt_state_t *state, uint64_t *out_pruned, int64_t *out_evidence_point)` (`nodus_witness_cmt_store.h`) | **CHANGED (behaviour) — documented deviation from cometbft v0.38.26 store/store.go:392-396 (decision item 5).** Never deletes block 1's BlockMeta row `H:1`, which the start-time V2 preflight (`nodus_witness_v2_preflight.c` check 5) reads on every open; height 1's `BH:`, `C:`, `SC:`, `EC:` and parts are deleted as before, height 1 still counts in `*out_pruned`, base still moves to `height`. Every other height unchanged (evidence-point rule as the reference). Signature and return codes unchanged. |
| `int nodus_cmt_bs_save_block(...)`, `int nodus_cmt_bs_save_block_with_extended_commit(...)` (via `static int save_block_to_batch(...)`, `nodus_witness_cmt_store.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour) — documented deviation from cometbft v0.38.26 store.go:574.** In the same batch that writes `C:h−1` and `SC:h`, deletes `SC:(h−1−W)` when that height is ≥ 1; a failed delete is CMT_FAULT and aborts the batch like every other write. Signatures unchanged. |


---

## 23. Scan payout display (explorer 0.2.2)

Display-only, outside consensus. Source tables are read through a separate
`SQLITE_OPEN_READONLY` handle; no schema migration, history backfill, key
access or change to owner-gated `dnac_addr_history`. CLI `--rewards-db PATH`
is optional and a failed source affects only the four rewards endpoints
(`/api/paydays`, `/api/payday/<h>`, `/api/releases/<h>`, `/api/rewards/<fp>`).
Stake releases (`kind='release'`) are read by the same path and never summed
into a payout figure.

| Function / type | Contract |
|---|---|
| `exp_http_ctx_t.rewards_db_path` (`explorer/src/exp_http.h`) | Optional plain filename of the co-located Nodus database. NULL means rewards unavailable. `main.c` wires the `--rewards-db` argument; existing routes remain available. |
| `exp_rewards_t`, `exp_payout_t`, `exp_rewards_page_t` (`explorer/src/exp_rewards.h`) | Request-scoped source snapshot; payout `(height,time_ms,amount,sequence,owner[64])`; exact total, matching row count (`recipients`: payout rows, or release rows for the release queries — the field name is kept), pending, at most 100 rows, and next-sequence metadata. Source row amounts must fit nonnegative signed 64 bits; sums are checked unsigned 64 bits. |
| `int exp_rewards_open(const char *path, const exp_block_row_t *anchor, exp_rewards_t **out)` | Begins a read transaction before reading any source state. Requires the copied indexed height/block id to match `v2_blocks`; reads source tip and current coverage marker in that snapshot. Requires positive `from_height <= last_height == source tip` and source tip >= indexed anchor. Plain filename only, no URI/immutable/write/migration. 0 success; -1 unavailable with `*out=NULL`. Opens with READONLY/NOMUTEX, no busy retries, a request-wide 2,000,000-VM-step progress budget sampled every 1,000 steps. |
| `void exp_rewards_close(exp_rewards_t *r)` | NULL-safe; disables progress callback, rolls back read transaction and closes source handle on every exit. |
| `uint64_t exp_rewards_from_height(const exp_rewards_t *r)`, `uint64_t exp_rewards_at_height(const exp_rewards_t *r)` | Current contiguous history run start and source snapshot tip; 0 on NULL. |
| `int exp_rewards_payday(exp_rewards_t *r, uint64_t height, uint32_t from, int limit, exp_rewards_page_t *out)` | Height-index range over real `kind='payout'` rows, checked native boundary rows only, sequence ascending. Whole-height total and payout-recipient row count plus inclusive `from` page; limit 0 = total/count only, 1..100 = page. `next_from` = first unshown sequence, not previous sequence + 1 (gaps allowed). Height outside coverage or any fault = -1; 0 success. **CHANGED (implementation only):** now `rewards_boundary_scan(r, "payout", …)`; signature and behaviour unchanged. |
| `int exp_rewards_releases(exp_rewards_t *r, uint64_t height, uint32_t from, int limit, exp_rewards_page_t *out)` | **NEW.** The `exp_rewards_payday` contract over `kind='release'` rows (graduation bond and delegation releases — the node writes both through `nodus_witness_addr_index_boundary` with `NODUS_ADDR_KIND_RELEASE`, one kind). `out->recipients` = release row count, `pending` 0. Payout and release rows share one `(h, i=UINT32_MAX)` sequence space, so each kind's sequences have gaps. Height outside coverage or any fault = -1; 0 success. The caller (`route_rewards` kind 3) rejects non-multiples of 720 before calling. |
| `int exp_rewards_address(exp_rewards_t *r, const uint8_t owner[64], uint64_t before_h, uint32_t before_seq, int limit, exp_rewards_page_t *out)` | Owner-index range restricted to current coverage through source tip. One scan computes exact paid total and selects a descending `(height,sequence)` page, exclusive before cursor; `(0,0)` = newest. Reads current owner accrual in the same transaction; absent row is zero only after source validation. A source fault, malformed row or exhausted budget is -1, never a partial total. **CHANGED (implementation only):** the scan is `rewards_owner_scan(r, "payout", …)`, then the accrual read; signature and behaviour unchanged — `total` remains payouts only. |
| `int exp_rewards_address_releases(exp_rewards_t *r, const uint8_t owner[64], uint64_t before_h, uint32_t before_seq, int limit, exp_rewards_page_t *out)` | **NEW.** `rewards_owner_scan(r, "release", …)`: exact released total of the owner within current coverage and a descending `(height,sequence)` page before the exclusive cursor (`(0,0)` = newest). No accrual read; `pending` 0. A fault, malformed row or exhausted budget is -1. |
| `static int rewards_boundary_scan(exp_rewards_t *r, const char *kind, uint64_t height, uint32_t from, int limit, exp_rewards_page_t *out)`, `static int rewards_owner_scan(exp_rewards_t *r, const char *kind, const uint8_t owner[64], uint64_t before_h, uint32_t before_seq, int limit, exp_rewards_page_t *out)` (`exp_rewards.c`) | **NEW.** The two former bodies of `exp_rewards_payday` / `exp_rewards_address` (minus the accrual read), with the kind bound as a parameter (`kind=?`) instead of the `'payout'` literal. Same primary-key `h` range / `INDEXED BY idx_addr_history_owner` range, same checks. `kind` is one of the file's `REWARDS_KIND_PAYOUT` ("payout") / `REWARDS_KIND_RELEASE` ("release") constants. |
| `static int rewards_progress(void *ctx)`, `static int rewards_u64(sqlite3_stmt *s, int col, uint64_t max, uint64_t *out)`, `static int rewards_blob64(sqlite3_stmt *s, int col, uint8_t out[64])` (`exp_rewards.c`) | Deterministic operation budget and strict integer/64-byte BLOB readers; no implicit SQLite conversion. |
| `static int rewards_row(sqlite3_stmt *s, exp_payout_t *p)`, `static int rewards_add(exp_rewards_page_t *out, const exp_payout_t *p)` | Validates positive height, boundary item position UINT32_MAX, u32 sequence, 64-byte owner/native token, nonnegative amount, and seconds-to-ms conversion; checked total and row count. Applies unchanged to payout and release rows (same writer, same shape). |
| `static int rewards_query(const char *query, int kind, uint64_t *before_h, uint32_t *sequence, uint64_t *release_h, uint32_t *release_seq, int *limit)` (`exp_http.c`) | **CHANGED signature** (+ `release_h`, `release_seq`). Strict bounded query parsing for payday list (kind 0), payday detail (1), address rewards (2) or boundary releases (3); kinds 1 and 3 take `from` + `limit` (default 100), kinds 0 and 2 `before`; kind 2 also takes `release_before=<height:sequence>` (its own duplicate bit, height ≤ INT64_MAX) into `release_h`/`release_seq`, (0,0) when absent. Rejects duplicate/unknown/empty/truncated parameters and out-of-range cursors, clamps positive limit to 100. Colon is literal, matching existing API parsing. |
| `static void emit_rewards_coverage(exp_json_t *j, uint64_t from, uint64_t at)` | Emits `from_height` and `at_height`; amounts elsewhere use exact decimal-string helpers. |
| `static void route_rewards(exp_http_ctx_t *ctx, int kind, const char *key, const char *query, exp_json_t *j, int *status)` | `/api/paydays`, `/api/payday/<h>`, `/api/rewards/<fp>` implementation. Copies indexed anchor and scheduled dates under a short db lock, releases it for all source reads, then rechecks the anchor under lock before success (F4 reset safety). Scheduled list includes pre-coverage paydays with null amounts/counts and `available:false`. List/detail `at_height` is indexed anchor; address `at_height` is source tip so current pending has an honest stamp. Malformed requests are 400; source/coverage/binding faults are 503. Full response reset on failure prevents partially emitted totals. **CHANGED:** kind 3 = `/api/releases/<h>` (height ≥ 1 and a multiple of `EXP_EPOCH_BLOCKS` 720, else 400; index block time and anchor bound as the payday detail; `exp_rewards_releases`; emits `{height, time, total, count, releases:[{address, amount, sequence}], next_from, from_height, at_height}`). Kind 2 appends, after the unchanged payout keys, `released_total`, `releases:[{height, time, amount, sequence}]` and `next_release_before` from `exp_rewards_address_releases` in the same snapshot, reusing the one page buffer; `paid_total` stays payouts only. |

Deployment and exact JSON/pagination contracts: `explorer/README.md`.
`test_rewards` covers real-row totals, exact large amounts, bounded pages,
coverage versus empty, malformed/unavailable sources, snapshot consistency,
source non-mutation and deterministic work exhaustion, and stake releases
(exact amounts, paging, empty boundary versus unavailable source, non-boundary
heights, releases kept out of `paid_total`); it uses disposable
SQLite fixtures only. No live-node or key prerequisite.

---

## 24. Storage reward v1, package B1 — storage registry, GEN_STORAGE, `NDS.SYS.v5` (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-04-storage-reward-approved.md`; design `docs/plans/2026-10-04-storage-reward-v1-design.md` rev 2.2 §1, §6)

**Superseded in part by HF-6 slice 8c (§5b.19):** generation 4 is voted by param 18 `RULESET_GEN_ROLES` = D4; param 16 is RETIRED and `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_STORAGE_D` DELETED — the param-16 rows below are the storage-only package's history.

Branch only — not versioned, not voted. Generation, op and param numbers assigned in main merge order (design §6; Nodus EVM merged first): generation 4 built on the EVM generation, param 16, SYSTEM v9 / CORE v7. EVM-only like the EVM generation (decision `2026-10-05-storage-reward-is-for-archive.md` K10): compiled only under `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED`. The GEN_STORAGE pins and `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_STORAGE_D` (S4 = 0x0c6fd6f2484e6024; re-derived to 0x329b0ea92c8b8c58 on branch `hf6` by slice 6a, §5b.6) are filled from the independent oracle `shared/dnac/tests/storage_oracle.py` (`4fedbbdc`; controls moved to EVM ruleset v2 on 2026-10-06, S4 unchanged); `nodus_witness_runtime_selfcheck` re-derives them at startup. Narrative: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Storage reward v1, package B1".

### 24.1 Constants and parameters (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`, `nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_runtime.h`, `nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_roots_v2.h`)
