A failed ROLLBACK is logged. |

### 25.7 `cmt_light_provider.h` (`light/provider/provider.go:9-29`, `light/provider/errors.go`) — interface only

No implementation in `shared/`. Implementations: the mocks in `nodus/tests/test_cmt_light_block.c`, `test_cmt_light_client.c` and `test_cmt_light_detector.c` (ports of `light/provider/mock/mock.go`, `deadmock.go`), and the one networked provider, `nodus/src/client/nodus_light_provider.c` over `dnac_light_block` — pinned 4001 only, never unpinned (Kurultay #25 item 8; §26). `context.Context` has no counterpart: an implementation MUST bound its own wait and answer NO_RESPONSE when it runs out — the client has no clock and sets no deadline itself (`cmt_light_provider.h:52-62`). An implementation need NOT validate what it returns: the light client checks every answer as the reference's provider would — every member present, the header at the requested height, `LightBlock.ValidateBasic` against the client's chain id (`cmt_light_client.h` D15; `light/provider/http/http.go:80-84`, `:96-99`) — and then verifies it.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `CMT_LIGHT_PROVIDER_ERR_NONE` 0, `_HEIGHT_TOO_HIGH` 1, `_LIGHT_BLOCK_NOT_FOUND` 2, `_NO_RESPONSE` 3, `_BAD_LIGHT_BLOCK` 4 | **NEW.** errors.go: `ErrHeightTooHigh`, `ErrLightBlockNotFound`, `ErrNoResponse`, `ErrBadLightBlock{Reason}`; NONE = unnamed. |
| `typedef struct { int code; int reason; } cmt_light_provider_error_t;` | **NEW.** `code` NONE on CMT_OK / CMT_FAULT; on CMT_REJECT one of the named codes or NONE. `reason` only for BAD_LIGHT_BLOCK: the CMT_* return of the check that refused the block. |
| `typedef struct { void *ctx; int (*chain_id)(void *ctx, const uint8_t **out, size_t *out_len); int (*light_block)(void *ctx, int64_t height, cmt_light_block_buf_t *out, cmt_light_provider_error_t *err); int (*report_evidence)(void *ctx, const cmt_pb_evidence_t *ev); } cmt_light_provider_t;` | **NEW.** The `Provider` interface as a callback table; every row required (a NULL row is CMT_FAULT to the caller). `chain_id`: points `*out` at the provider-owned id. `light_block`: height 0 = latest; negative = CMT_FAULT; not found = CMT_REJECT with LIGHT_BLOCK_NOT_FOUND; a failure to fetch = CMT_REJECT with NO_RESPONSE or NONE; `err` may be NULL. `report_evidence`: implementation to be LOCAL RECORD ONLY (Kurultay #25 item 2); its argument type cannot carry LightClientAttackEvidence (the port does not have that type). The detector (§25.10) therefore never calls it: an attack is kept as the client's local record (`cmt_light_detector.h` DD3, DD4). |

### 25.8 Tests

`nodus/tests/test_cmt_light_verifier.c`, `test_cmt_light_block.c`, `test_cmt_light_store.c` (ctest entries of the same names; default build, no environment, no clock, fixed-seed ML-DSA-87 keys). What each proves, requires, leaves behind and how it can lie is in its file header; summary in `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Light client package 1". Packages 2 and 3 add `nodus/tests/test_cmt_light_client.c` and `test_cmt_light_detector.c` (ctest entries of the same names, `nodus/CMakeLists.txt`; default build, no flags, no environment, no network, no clock — every time is a fixed value after the reference's bTime 2006-01-02T15:04:05Z; keys from `qgp_dsa87_keypair_derand` with fixed seeds; each makes and removes `test_cmt_light_client.XXXXXX` / `test_cmt_light_detector.XXXXXX` SQLite files in the working directory, a crash can leave one). Summary: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Light client packages 2 and 3".

### 25.9 `cmt_light_client.h` — light client package 2 (`light/client.go` 1190 lines, `light/trust_options.go` 53 lines, `light/errors.go`)

The client that moves its trust forwards (sequential or skipping), between and backwards over a primary provider, a list of witnesses and a light store (`cmt_light_client.h:1-6`). **No production caller in a node** (`:8-11`); the program `nodus-light` (nodus 0.26.11, `nodus/tools/nodus-light.c`) is its one caller outside the tests, and supplies the trust root (operator input; the release default is empty) and the 48 h period. Trust root and trusting period are the CALLER's: the library embeds no checkpoint (K25-1) and hard-codes no trusting period (K25-2, 48 h in production is the caller's `period` / `trusting_period`) (`:34-40`). The reference's `detectDivergence` call after every forward and between verification goes to ONE internal hook, `static int detect_divergence(...)` (`cmt_light_client.c:555`), which runs the detector (§25.10); a verified block is saved ONLY when the detector returns CMT_OK (`:13-31`). An attack also LOCKS the client until the operator clears it, and its record is written to the light store, which reloads the lock after a restart (K25-10; D20 below; `:26-29`). Every provider answer passes `static int provider_light_block(...)` (`cmt_light_client.c:324`), the D15 contract check. No clock (`now` is a parameter), no randomness, no map, no floating point; witnesses in index order (`:334-338`); single owner, NOT thread-safe (D14, `:194-196`).

**Init agreement rule (Kurultay #25 item 9, replaces the reference's lenient `compareFirstLightBlockWithWitnesses`, client.go:1131-1190)** (`:42-92`): R1 each configured witness asked ONCE for its block at `options.height`, in index order; R2 agreement counted over DISTINCT identities (`cmt_light_client_peer_t.id`), the primary's included; R3 agree = same header hash AND same proposer priorities as the full vector (K25-3); R4 NO_RESPONSE / LIGHT_BLOCK_NOT_FOUND / HEIGHT_TOO_HIGH add nothing, BAD_LIGHT_BLOCK or an unnamed CMT_REJECT add nothing and remove the witness; R5 the first different hash (CONFLICTING_HEADERS) or same hash with different priorities (PROPOSER_PRIORITIES_DIVERGE) ends init CMT_REJECT with `witness_index`, nothing saved; R6 distinct agreeing identities ≥ `min_init_agreement` (never below 2), else INIT_AGREEMENT_SHORT. ⚠ That the primary counts as one of the "≥ 2" is the port's READING of item 9, not a recorded fact (`:82-84`); a caller wanting two witnesses besides the primary sets `min_init_agreement` = 3. A consistency check, never the root: the root is `options.hash` (`:85-86`). The deadline of item 9 is the provider implementation's (`:87-90`).

**Deviations D1-D21 and reference quirks Q1-Q8** are listed with their reasons in the header (deviations `:94-294`, quirks `:295-325`). In short: D1 the init rule above; D2 witnesses queried in INDEX order, not arrival order; D3 four caps with NO default (a zero cap is refused at construction) — `max_trace_len`, `max_verify_steps`, `max_skipping_cache`, `max_backwards_steps` — each reached is a named CMT_REJECT; memory bounded by `max_verify_steps`, about 2 × max_verify_steps light-block buffers per operation, `sizeof(cmt_light_block_buf_t)` = 945 856 bytes on x86_64 gcc ("read from a compile-time diagnostic, not a run", `:124-126`); D4 the two recursions are loops; D5 the hook IS the detector (its deviations DD1-DD10; the former `CMT_LIGHT_CLIENT_ERR_DETECTOR_NOT_IMPLEMENTED` is gone; code 21 is now LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK); D6 a CMT_FAULT never triggers a primary replacement or witness removal — ONE EXCEPTION inside the detector: once an attack is established (the witness's conflicting header verified), a provider or examine CMT_FAULT while its evidence is built does NOT end the call with CMT_FAULT; the call returns LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK, the record names what it could not build in `missing` (DD10) and the client locks (D20) (`:144-153`); D7 Go nil dereferences are CMT_FAULT; D8 a failed delete ends `cleanupAfter`; **D9 ⚠ SECURITY — after a backwards walk the reached header's hash must equal the target's** (else VERIFICATION_FAILED, reason TARGET_MISMATCH; K25-8; the reference spec has the check, `spec/light-client/verification/README.md:442-444`, client.go lacks it on its success path); D10 `out` holds the block only on CMT_OK; D11 only a CMT_REJECT from `TrustedLightBlock` proceeds; D12 `pruning_size` 64-bit, `max_retry_attempts` uint16 and never read, `max_block_lag` read by the detector only; D13 `ConfirmationFunction` = callback with an action code and two heights; D14 Logger = QGP_LOG, no mutex, no `quit`; **D15 the provider contract moved into the client** (header at the requested height when not 0, `LightBlock.ValidateBasic(chainID)`, either failure BAD_LIGHT_BLOCK reason CMT_REJECT); D16 a CMT_OK answer missing a member is BAD_LIGHT_BLOCK; D17 the cleanup walk is counted against Size() + 1; D18 constructor check order; D19 the detector reads the caller's `now` where the reference does plus the DD2 wait; **D20 THE ATTACK LOCK** (K25-10, operator 2026-10-19, "1"; `:233-265`) — the reference has no latch (detector.go:72, client.go:820-821). A call that returns LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK LOCKS the client and writes the record to the store (`cmt_light_store.h` D9; byte form `cmt_light_attack_record_encode`, DD9). While locked, `cmt_light_client_update` and `cmt_light_client_verify_light_block_at_height` return CMT_REJECT with `ATTACK_LOCKED`, `cmt_light_client_cleanup` returns CMT_REJECT (it has no `err` argument), and none of them touches the providers or the store; the store write of `updateTrustedLightBlock` refuses too. Reads (TrustedLightBlock, the heights, the identities, the record) still answer. `cmt_light_client_new_from_trusted_store` loads the lock from the store (an uncleared record → locked, `cmt_light_client.c:1787-1796`); `cmt_light_client_new` refuses a locked store with ATTACK_LOCKED (`:1847-1850`), because its next steps change the trusted store. Only `cmt_light_client_attack_clear` unlocks; it marks every record cleared and deletes none. Rules that make the lock hold: ONE client object per store prefix — as a second guard `updateTrustedLightBlock` re-reads the store's uncleared records right before every save and refuses (and locks) when there is one (F2, `cmt_light_client.c:634-653`); a record NOT yet durable (`durable` false: encode or store write failed) is written again at the start of every call while locked and before a clear, and the clear REFUSES (CMT_REJECT, still locked) until the write succeeded (F3, `persist_attack`); encode and store failures are logged apart; a record whose encode fails every time can only be released at the store (`cmt_light_store_db_clear_attack_records`) with a new client. **D21 UPDATE KEEPS THE TARGET OF A RECORDED WAIT** (BUGS Z-41; `:266-294`) — the reference's Update fetches the latest block once, sleeps inside the call and trusts THAT block (client.go:447-459 → detector.go:164-197); here the sleep is the caller's next call (DD2), so the wait record holds a deep copy of the target LIGHT BLOCK and the primary is never asked for it a second time. A record PINS Update when its height is above the trusted one, and VerifyLightBlockAtHeight for exactly its height. Pinned and before the wait has run (`now` < since + 2*drift + lag): WITNESS_BEHIND without fetching anything; once it has run: THE STORED BLOCK is verified (the detector's post-sleep pass) and, on success, saved — the client may then be behind the tip and the NEXT Update moves to the latest. Whatever the verdict, the judged record is then cleared. A record is STALE only when its height is already in the store (Update drops it); a record below the tip that is not stored is left for VerifyLightBlockAtHeight. The record is still ONE target: a later behind detection for another target replaces it. Not ported (`:326-332`): `sendEvidence`, `VerifyHeader`, `context.Context`, `Primary()` / `Witnesses()` returning providers.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `typedef struct { int64_t period; int64_t height; const uint8_t *hash; size_t hash_len; } cmt_light_trust_options_t;` | **NEW.** `TrustOptions` (trust_options.go:11-36). `period` in nanoseconds; `hash` not owned, must outlive the call. Supplied by the caller (K25-1, K25-2). |
| `int cmt_light_trust_options_validate_basic(const cmt_light_trust_options_t *o)` | **NEW.** trust_options.go:38-53: `period <= 0`, `height <= 0`, `hash_len != tmhash.Size` (64 here) → CMT_REJECT. CMT_OK; CMT_FAULT on NULL. |

| `CMT_LIGHT_CLIENT_ERR_*`: `NONE` 0, `INVALID_TRUST_OPTIONS` 1, `NO_WITNESSES` 2, `WITNESS_WRONG_CHAIN` 3, `INVALID_TRUST_LEVEL` 4, `INVALID_OPTIONS` 5 (PROJECT), `VERIFICATION_FAILED` 6, `VERIFIER` 7, `PROVIDER` 8, `INVALID_LIGHT_BLOCK` 9, `HASH_MISMATCH` 10, `INVALID_COMMIT` 11, `CONFLICTING_HEADERS` 12, `PROPOSER_PRIORITIES_DIVERGE` 13, `INIT_AGREEMENT_SHORT` 14 (PROJECT), `NO_HEADERS` 15, `UNVERIFIED_HEIGHT` 16, `BAD_HEIGHT` 17, `NOT_FOUND` 18, `STORE` 19, `REFUSED_CLEANUP` 20, `LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK` 21, `SHORT_TRACE` 22, `CAP_TRACE` 23, `CAP_VERIFY_STEPS` 24, `CAP_SKIPPING_CACHE` 25, `CAP_BACKWARDS_STEPS` 26 (caps PROJECT), `FAILED_HEADER_CROSS_REFERENCING` 27, `WITNESS_BEHIND` 28 (PROJECT), `ATTACK_LOCKED` 29 (PROJECT, D20 / K25-10) | **NEW** (29 added by the K25-10 fix). light/errors.go:43-121 and the client's unnamed errors; each code's client.go / detector.go citation is in the header (`cmt_light_client.h:478-567`). LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK: a witness's conflicting header verified, nothing sent to any provider (DD4); the client is then locked (D20). WITNESS_BEHIND: call again, for the same target, with a `now` at least `2*max_clock_drift + max_block_lag` later (DD2) — Update keeps that target by itself (D21). ATTACK_LOCKED: an attack was detected and not yet cleared by the operator; the client verifies and saves nothing. |
| `CMT_LIGHT_CLIENT_REASON_NONE` 0, `_VERIFIER` 1, `_PROVIDER` 2, `_NO_WITNESSES` 3, `_TARGET_MISMATCH` 4 (PROJECT, D9) | **NEW.** Which nested field carries `ErrVerificationFailed`'s Reason. |
| `typedef struct { int code; int64_t from; int64_t to; int reason; cmt_light_error_t verify; cmt_light_provider_error_t provider; size_t witness_index; size_t agreeing; } cmt_light_client_error_t;` | **NEW.** The client's error values as one out-parameter; `code` NONE on CMT_OK and CMT_FAULT; no error text. `witness_index` for WRONG_CHAIN, CONFLICTING, DIVERGE, LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK; `agreeing` for INIT_AGREEMENT_SHORT. |
| `typedef struct cmt_light_client cmt_light_client_t;`, `typedef struct cmt_light_attack_record cmt_light_attack_record_t;` | **NEW.** Opaque client (fields visible only under `CMT_LIGHT_CLIENT_INTERNAL`, §25.10); the attack record (fields in `cmt_light_detector.h`). |
| `int cmt_light_client_new(const uint8_t *chain_id, size_t chain_id_len, const cmt_light_trust_options_t *trust, const cmt_light_client_peer_t *primary, const cmt_light_client_peer_t *witnesses, size_t n_witnesses, const cmt_light_store_t *store, const cmt_light_client_options_t *opts, cmt_light_client_t **out, cmt_light_client_error_t *err)` | **NEW.** `NewClient()` (client.go:164-207): TrustOptions.ValidateBasic; NewClientFromTrustedStore with `trust->period`; if the store trusts a block, checkTrustedHeaderUsingOptions; if it then trusts none, or one below `trust->height`, initializeWithTrustOptions with the item-9 agreement rule. No `now` (the reference checks no time at init). At least one witness; visited in index order. `store` copied, its ctx must outlive the client. Free with `cmt_light_client_free`. `err` may be NULL. CMT_OK; CMT_REJECT with `err->code` — `ATTACK_LOCKED` when the store holds an uncleared attack record (D20; checked right after NewClientFromTrustedStore, before anything changes the store, `cmt_light_client.c:1847-1850`); CMT_FAULT on NULL, allocation failure, or a provider / store CMT_FAULT (D6). |
| `int cmt_light_client_new_from_trusted_store(const uint8_t *chain_id, size_t chain_id_len, int64_t trusting_period, const cmt_light_client_peer_t *primary, const cmt_light_client_peer_t *witnesses, size_t n_witnesses, const cmt_light_store_t *store, const cmt_light_client_options_t *opts, cmt_light_client_t **out, cmt_light_client_error_t *err)` | **NEW.** `NewClientFromTrustedStore()` (client.go:209-264): the options (plus D3 / item-9 domain checks), ≥ 1 witness, every witness on `chain_id` (not the primary, Q4), ValidateTrustLevel, restoreTrustedLightBlock. `trusting_period` is the caller's, not range-checked. Every store row is required, the three attack-record rows included (`cmt_light_store.h` D9). Reads the store's attack records: an uncleared one → the client starts LOCKED (D20). Writes nothing, so it is the constructor an operator uses to inspect and clear. Returns as for `cmt_light_client_new`, never ATTACK_LOCKED. |
| `void cmt_light_client_free(cmt_light_client_t *c)` | **NEW.** Frees the client and everything it owns (the attack record and the recorded wait's stored target included, `cmt_light_client.c:1875-1885`); the store and providers are the caller's and are not closed. NULL is a no-op. |
| `int cmt_light_client_trusted_light_block(cmt_light_client_t *c, int64_t height, cmt_light_block_buf_t *out, cmt_light_client_error_t *err)` | **NEW.** `TrustedLightBlock(height)` (client.go:395-410, 0 = the latest) through compareWithLatestHeight (Q8). CMT_OK with the block in `out`; CMT_REJECT with NO_HEADERS, UNVERIFIED_HEIGHT, BAD_HEIGHT, NOT_FOUND or STORE; CMT_FAULT. |
| `int cmt_light_client_last_trusted_height(cmt_light_client_t *c, int64_t *out)`, `int cmt_light_client_first_trusted_height(cmt_light_client_t *c, int64_t *out)` | **NEW.** `LastTrustedHeight()` / `FirstTrustedHeight()` (client.go:830-844); -1 when none. |
| `int cmt_light_client_update(cmt_light_client_t *c, cmt_time_t now, cmt_light_block_buf_t *out, bool *advanced, cmt_light_client_error_t *err)` | **NEW.** `Update(now)` (client.go:433-462): no trusted block, or a latest block not above the trusted one → CMT_OK with `*advanced = false`; otherwise the latest block is verified (the detector included) and saved, and returned in `out` when `*advanced` is true. While locked (D20): CMT_REJECT with ATTACK_LOCKED before any read (a not-yet-durable record is written first, F3). While a wait is recorded above the trusted height (D21): WITNESS_BEHIND without a fetch until it has run, then the STORED target block — not the latest — is verified and saved, and the record is cleared whatever the verdict; a record whose height is already stored is dropped. |
| `int cmt_light_client_verify_light_block_at_height(cmt_light_client_t *c, int64_t height, cmt_time_t now, cmt_light_block_buf_t *out, cmt_light_client_error_t *err)` | **NEW.** `VerifyLightBlockAtHeight` (client.go:464-494): `height <= 0` BAD_HEIGHT; a block already trusted is returned as is (D11); otherwise fetched from the primary and verified (`verifyLightBlock`, :558-610) forwards from the latest trusted block, backwards from the first (with the D9 target check), or between from the closest trusted block below; on success saved and the store pruned (`updateTrustedLightBlock`, :910-928; that save refuses while locked and re-reads the store's uncleared attack records first, D20 / F2). `out` holds the block on CMT_OK only (D10). While locked (D20): CMT_REJECT with ATTACK_LOCKED before any read. A recorded wait for exactly this height pins its stored block (D21). |
| `int cmt_light_client_cleanup(cmt_light_client_t *c)` | **NEW.** `Cleanup()` (client.go:871-877): forgets the trusted block and prunes the store to zero. While locked (D20): CMT_REJECT, changing nothing (a not-yet-durable record is written first) (`cmt_light_client.c:689-703`). |
| `const cmt_light_attack_record_t *cmt_light_client_attack_record(const cmt_light_client_t *c)` | **NEW (PROJECT).** The record of the LAST attack THIS client object found (a call that returned LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK), owned by the client; a later attack replaces it, `cmt_light_client_free` frees it. NULL when none was found, or on NULL (a lock loaded from the store at construction has no in-memory record — read the store's with `cmt_light_store_db_attack_record`). It was sent nowhere (DD4). Every record is also in the store when its `durable` is true (D20). |
| `bool cmt_light_client_attack_locked(const cmt_light_client_t *c)` (`cmt_light_client.h:731`, `.c:1895`) | **NEW (PROJECT, D20 / K25-10).** True while an attack is not cleared (found by this client, or loaded from the store at construction, or seen by the F2 re-read before a save). False on NULL. |
| `int cmt_light_client_attack_clear(cmt_light_client_t *c)` (`cmt_light_client.h:742`, `.c:1901`) | **NEW (PROJECT, D20 / K25-10).** The operator's clear: a record not yet written to the store is written first; then every attack record in the store becomes cleared (none deleted, `clear_attack_records`) and the client unlocks. The in-memory record stays readable. Unlocked → CMT_OK. CMT_REJECT while that record still cannot be written (still locked); CMT_FAULT on NULL or a store failure of the clear (still locked). |
| `size_t cmt_light_client_witness_count(const cmt_light_client_t *c)` | **NEW.** `len(c.Witnesses())`; 0 on NULL. |
| `int cmt_light_client_primary_id(const cmt_light_client_t *c, const uint8_t **id, size_t *id_len)` | **NEW.** The primary's identity bytes (client-owned). CMT_FAULT on NULL. |
| `int cmt_light_client_witness_id(const cmt_light_client_t *c, size_t i, const uint8_t **id, size_t *id_len)` | **NEW.** Witness `i`'s identity bytes. CMT_REJECT for `i` out of range. |
| `static int provider_light_block(const cmt_light_client_t *c, const peer_t *pr, ...)`, `static int detect_divergence(cmt_light_client_t *c, const lbvec_t *trace, cmt_time_t now, cmt_light_client_error_t *err)` (`cmt_light_client.c:324`, `:555`) | **NEW (internal).** The single provider entry point that applies D15/D16, and the one detector hook — on LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK it sets `c->locked`, marks the record not durable and calls `persist_attack` (D20); a failed write keeps the lock in memory and the attack result. |
| `static void persist_attack(cmt_light_client_t *c)` (`cmt_light_client.c:525`) | **NEW (internal, D20 / F3).** Writes the client's attack record to the store if it is not there yet: `cmt_light_attack_record_encode` then `store.save_attack_record`; on success `durable = true`. Called when the attack is found and again at the start of every locked call and before `attack_clear`. An encode failure and a store failure are logged apart (ERROR); either leaves `durable` false and the lock in memory. |
| `static int pinned_target(cmt_light_client_t *c, bool for_update, int64_t height, int64_t last, cmt_time_t now, cmt_light_block_buf_t **out, bool *pinned, cmt_light_client_error_t *err)` (`cmt_light_client.c:2015`) | **NEW (internal, D21).** The target a recorded wait pins. `for_update`: a record above `last` applies; a record at or below `last` whose height is in the store is dropped (stale), one not in the store is left (VerifyLightBlockAtHeight's). Otherwise a record at exactly `height`. None applies → CMT_OK, `*pinned` false. Wait not run → CMT_REJECT WITNESS_BEHIND, nothing fetched. Run → CMT_OK, `*pinned` true, `*out` = the stored target taken out of the record (the caller frees it and clears the record after judging it). |

### 25.10 `cmt_light_detector.h` — light client package 3 (`light/detector.go` 439 lines; `types/evidence.go:204-318`)

The second wall of defence: after the primary's trace is verified, every witness is asked for the same block; a witness with another block is examined, and a conflict that verifies on BOTH sides is an attack, which stops the client with a LOCAL RECORD of what the reference would have sent as evidence (`cmt_light_detector.h:3-8`). **No production caller**; called only from the client's `detect_divergence` hook (`:10-17`). Kurultay #25 item 2: the detector is required in v1, examine step included; evidence sending replaced by "stop + local record" (K24 D3); K25-10 adds the lock and the durable record (governing records `:307`). Reference → port map: `:20-40`.

Deviations (`:42-264`): **DD1** K25-3 full-vector proposer-priority comparison instead of `ProposerPriorityHash()` (that hash writes every varint at offset 0, `types/validator_set.go:392-398`; node-local, no wire / block / root / consensus change; the same function serves init rule R3); **DD2** the reference's `time.Sleep(2*maxClockDrift + maxBlockLag)` (detector.go:168) is split into two calls separated by the CALLER's `now`: a first pass that reaches the sleep returns WITNESS_BEHIND after every other witness was handled and records (target height, target hash, `now`) AND a deep copy of the target light block itself; a later call for the SAME target with `now >= recorded + 2*max_clock_drift + max_block_lag` is the post-sleep pass. The record is ONE target: KEPT untouched by the returns that come before any witness is asked (NULL argument, SHORT_TRACE, a fault reading the target's height or hash, NO_WITNESSES with an empty list); KEPT or written (a record for another target replaced, `since` = this `now`) by WITNESS_BEHIND; CLEARED by every other return. Calls that do not reach the detector leave it — except Update, which drops a record whose height is already stored, and a pinned call (client D21), which clears it after judging the stored block. Every clear or replacement frees the stored block (`cmt_light_detector_wait_clear`). Witnesses marked bad in a pass that ends WITNESS_BEHIND are REMOVED before it returns (detector.go:98-100). The moving target (BUGS Z-41) is fixed in Update (client D21), not by keying the wait per witness — that alternative was REJECTED because a per-witness `since` taken at an earlier target gives a new target no `2*drift + lag` margin (`:119-142`); **DD3** no LightClientAttackEvidence type exists in the port, so `cmt_light_attack_evidence_t` holds the same fields (types/evidence.go:208-216) plus PROJECT fields (`now`, the identities, witness index, `missing`, `durable`); it is returned to the caller, written to the light store in the byte form of `cmt_light_attack_record_encode` (DD9), and never put on any wire (no proto, no hash); **DD4** `ReportEvidence` IS NOT CALLED — nothing is sent, and the attack error claims detection and local recording only; **DD5** the client's D3 caps bound every loop and a cap reached inside the detector FAILS CLOSED (nothing saved, witness NOT removed); **DD6** Go nil / index panics are CMT_FAULT — none reachable with two verified traces — EXCEPT the one reachable case (BUGS Z-42): a nil validator from GetByzantineValidators in the equivocation branch (evidence.go:288-289). The commit check stops counting once more than 2/3 has signed (types/validation.go:289-291, :401-403; port `cmt_validation.c:264-266`), so a COMMIT signature after that point may name no validator and the block still verifies; such a signature is SKIPPED and counted (`n_unknown_signers`). ⚠ CLAIMED, NOT VERIFIED (F4, `:205-214`): for the same reason a COMMIT entry past the 2/3 stop point that DOES name a validator was never signature-checked either, so `byzantine_validators` lists the validators the two commits CLAIM signed, in both branches — the reference's evidence is the same list; any later use that punishes or blames a validator (the future K25-4 evidence work) must verify each named signature first; **DD7** witnesses compared in INDEX order; **DD8** no `context.Canceled` / `DeadlineExceeded`; **DD9 THE ATTACK LOCK** (K25-10, operator 2026-10-19, "1"; `:223-251`) — the reference has no latch (detector.go:72 → client.go:820-821); here the client's hook LOCKS the client on LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK and writes the record's bytes to the store (`cmt_light_store.h` D9); a failed write still locks in memory and still returns the attack (`durable` false, logged, retried, the clear refuses until it succeeded — client D20); node-local, no wire / block / root / consensus change; cost accepted by the operator: a false alarm halts that light client until the operator looks; **DD10 AN ESTABLISHED ATTACK IS NEVER DOWNGRADED** (BUGS Z-42; `:252-263`) — once the witness's conflicting header has verified (detector.go:240-250 passed), the call returns LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK and keeps the record whatever happens while the evidence is built (malformed trace, evidence build failure, a second examine that faults); what could not be built is named in `missing`; Q-D4 (the second examine REJECTING) stays as in the reference — `has_against_witness` false, nothing in `missing`; the one exception is an allocation failure of the record itself: CMT_FAULT, and the client is still LOCKED in memory only. Reference quirks reproduced as-is: Q-D1..Q-D6 (`:265-288`). Determinism: no clock, no randomness, no map, no floating point; byzantine validators sorted with `cmt_validators_sort_by_voting_power` (power descending, address ascending); every record is node-local (`:289-295`). Threat model: K25-4 — more than 2/3 of the trusted set honest, at least one provider honest, the root right; no economic punishment (BUGS Z-12) (`:297-301`).

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `bool cmt_light_priorities_equal(const cmt_validator_set_t *a, const cmt_validator_set_t *b)` | **NEW.** K25-3: the full proposer-priority vector, in validator order, length included; replaces `bytes.Equal(ProposerPriorityHash(), …)` (detector.go:211-216; DD1). Two NULL sets are equal; one NULL is not. Used by the detector and by the init agreement rule R3. |
| `CMT_LIGHT_ATTACK_LUNATIC` 1, `CMT_LIGHT_ATTACK_EQUIVOCATION` 2, `CMT_LIGHT_ATTACK_AMNESIA` 3 | **NEW (PROJECT label).** Which branch of `GetByzantineValidators` (types/evidence.go:252-299) produced `byzantine_validators`: :257-271 header invalid / :272-292 same commit round / :294-297 rounds differ. |
| `typedef struct { cmt_light_block_buf_t *conflicting_block; int64_t common_height; cmt_validator_t *byzantine_validators; size_t n_byzantine; int64_t total_voting_power; cmt_time_t timestamp; int attack; size_t n_unknown_signers; } cmt_light_attack_evidence_t;` (`cmt_light_detector.h:357-371`) | **NEW; `n_unknown_signers` added by the Z-42 fix.** The fields of `types.LightClientAttackEvidence` (evidence.go:208-216), held locally; every pointer owned (`conflicting_block` a deep copy; `byzantine_validators` NULL if 0). `timestamp` is a BLOCK's time (detector.go:430, :434), never a clock. `n_unknown_signers` (PROJECT, DD6): equivocation branch only — COMMIT signatures of the conflicting commit whose address is NOT in the conflicting set, skipped (the reference appends a nil there, evidence.go:288-289, and its sort at :291 panics). `byzantine_validators` is the CLAIMED signer list, not a verified one (DD6 F4). Release with `cmt_light_attack_evidence_release`. |
| `#define CMT_LIGHT_ATTACK_MISSING_AGAINST_PRIMARY 1u`, `#define CMT_LIGHT_ATTACK_MISSING_AGAINST_WITNESS 2u` (`cmt_light_detector.h:375-376`) | **NEW (PROJECT, DD10).** Bits of `cmt_light_attack_record.missing`: which evidence the record does NOT hold although the attack is established — against the primary (detector.go:252-255 not built), against the witness (:281-283 not built although the second examine succeeded or faulted; not Q-D4). |
| `struct cmt_light_attack_record { cmt_time_t now; size_t witness_index; uint8_t primary_id[CMT_LIGHT_CLIENT_PEER_ID_MAX]; size_t primary_id_len; uint8_t witness_id[CMT_LIGHT_CLIENT_PEER_ID_MAX]; size_t witness_id_len; cmt_light_attack_evidence_t against_primary; bool has_against_witness; cmt_light_attack_evidence_t against_witness; bool commit_rounds_differ; uint32_t missing; bool durable; };` (`cmt_light_detector.h:388-403`) | **NEW; `missing` and `durable` added by the Z-42 / K25-10 fix.** What one detected attack leaves behind (DD3). `against_primary` = the evidence the reference sends to the witness (detector.go:254-258); `against_witness`, present when the second examine succeeded (:269-286, Q-D4), the one it sends to the primary. NEITHER IS SENT (DD4). `now`, the identities and `witness_index` are PROJECT additions; `commit_rounds_differ` keeps the amnesia notice (Q-D5); `missing` = `CMT_LIGHT_ATTACK_MISSING_*` (DD10); `durable` (DD9) = the store's save of this record's bytes returned CMT_OK — false means the lock holds in memory only and a restart loses it. Read through `cmt_light_client_attack_record`. |
| `int cmt_light_attack_record_encode(const cmt_light_attack_record_t *r, uint8_t **out, size_t *out_len)` (`cmt_light_detector.h:431`, `cmt_light_detector.c:482`) | **NEW (PROJECT, DD9 / K25-10; no reference counterpart).** The durable form of a record — what the client hands the store (`cmt_light_store.h` D9). Node-local; never on any wire. All integers big-endian; the same record encodes to the same bytes every time (no clock, no padding, no pointer). Layout (`cmt_light_detector.h:405-430`): `u8 version = 1`; `i64 now.seconds`, `i32 now.nanos`; `u64 witness_index`; `u8 primary_id_len`, primary_id; `u8 witness_id_len`, witness_id; `u8 flags` (bit 0 has_against_witness, bit 1 commit_rounds_differ); `u8 missing`; EVIDENCE against_primary — present unless MISSING_AGAINST_PRIMARY; EVIDENCE against_witness — present iff has_against_witness. EVIDENCE = `u32 n` then n bytes of `cmt_light_block_encode` of the conflicting block; `i64 common_height`; `i64 total_voting_power`; `i64 timestamp.seconds`, `i32 timestamp.nanos`; `i32 attack`; `u32 n_unknown_signers`; `u32 n_byzantine`, then per validator `u8 address_len`, address, `u8 pub_key.present`, the 2592 key bytes when present, `i64 voting_power`, `i64 proposer_priority`. `durable` is not encoded. `*out` is malloc'd. CMT_OK; CMT_REJECT when the conflicting block does not encode; CMT_FAULT on NULL, a present evidence without its conflicting block, a field too long for its length byte, or an allocation failure. |
| `bool cmt_light_conflicting_header_is_invalid(const cmt_header_t *trusted, const cmt_header_t *conflicting)` | **NEW.** `ConflictingHeaderIsInvalid` (types/evidence.go:304-310): true when any of ValidatorsHash, NextValidatorsHash, ConsensusHash, AppHash or LastResultsHash differs (a lunatic attack). NULL is false. |
| `int cmt_light_attack_evidence_build(const cmt_light_block_t *conflicted, const cmt_light_block_t *trusted, const cmt_light_block_t *common, cmt_light_attack_evidence_t *out)` | **NEW.** `newLightClientAttackEvidence` (detector.go:423-439) with `GetByzantineValidators(common.ValidatorSet, trusted.SignedHeader)`, as a local record: invalid header → CommonHeight / Timestamp / total power of `common`, byzantine = the conflicting commit's COMMIT signers found in `common`'s set; otherwise of `trusted`, equal commit rounds → the conflicting set's validators that signed COMMIT in both commits at the same index (Q-D6), different rounds → none (amnesia); sorted by ValidatorsByVotingPower. In the equivocation branch a COMMIT signer whose address is not in the conflicting set is SKIPPED and counted in `n_unknown_signers` (DD6, Z-42 — formerly CMT_FAULT). `out` zeroed first; holds nothing on failure. CMT_OK; CMT_FAULT on NULL, a missing member, allocation failure, a total-power overflow (validator_set.go:344-345), an address lookup that faults, or the DD6 index case (evidence.go:283). |
| `void cmt_light_attack_evidence_release(cmt_light_attack_evidence_t *ev)` | **NEW.** Frees what `ev` owns and zeroes it. NULL is a no-op. |
| `void cmt_light_attack_record_free(cmt_light_attack_record_t *r)` | **NEW.** Frees both evidences, then the record. NULL is a no-op. |
| `int cmt_light_detect_divergence(cmt_light_client_t *c, const cmt_light_trace_t *trace, cmt_time_t now, cmt_light_client_error_t *err)` | **NEW (INTERNAL — only under `CMT_LIGHT_CLIENT_INTERNAL`, which `cmt_light_client.c:21` and `cmt_light_detector.c:17` define; no other file may).** `detectDivergence()` (detector.go:28-110). `trace` = the primary's verified trace; its blocks must outlive the call. CMT_OK (a witness matched and none diverged: the caller may save); CMT_REJECT with SHORT_TRACE (:29-31), NO_WITNESSES (:44-46, :98-100), PROPOSER_PRIORITIES_DIVERGE (:83-86, `witness_index`), LIGHT_CLIENT_ATTACK (:72, :278, :288; `witness_index`; record in `c->attack` — an established attack is returned even when its evidence could not all be built, DD10), WITNESS_BEHIND (DD2), FAILED_HEADER_CROSS_REFERENCING (:109) or a cap (DD5); CMT_FAULT. The detector only RETURNS the attack: locking the client and writing the record are the client hook's (`detect_divergence`, §25.9, D20). |
| `typedef struct { bool pending; int64_t height; uint8_t hash[CMT_TMHASH_SIZE]; size_t hash_len; cmt_time_t since; cmt_light_block_buf_t *target; } cmt_light_detector_wait_t;` (`cmt_light_detector.h:502-509`) | **NEW (INTERNAL, same guard); `target` added by the Z-41 fix.** The one recorded DD2 wait: a witness reached the sleep (:164-168) while the client verified `height` / `hash`, at the caller's `since`. `target` is a deep copy of THAT light block (owned; NULL only while the client has taken it out to judge it, D21) — after the wait the client judges this block, never a re-fetched one. One buffer (about 0.9 MiB) at most. Release with `cmt_light_detector_wait_clear`. |

NULL is a no-op. Every path that clears or replaces the record calls it, and so does `cmt_light_client_free`. |
| `bool cmt_light_detector_wait_elapsed(const cmt_light_client_t *c, cmt_time_t now)` (`cmt_light_detector.h:578`, `cmt_light_detector.c:1036`) | **NEW (INTERNAL, same guard).** True when a wait is recorded and `now` is at or past its `since` + 2*max_clock_drift + max_block_lag (`!cmt_time_before(now, since + wait_ns)`); false on NULL. Used by the client's D21 pin. |
