## 26. `dnac_light_block` — the light client's block source over pinned 4001 (decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-16-dnac-light-block-rpc.md`, APPROVED B1-B4; Kurultay #25 item 8)

A new tier-2 `dnac_*` method on TCP 4001 that returns one cometbft `LightBlock` (signed header + commit + validator set), the server builder, the client SDK call and its exported codec, a new error code, and the PINNED light provider that implements `cmt_light_provider_t` (§25.7) over it. Node-local: no consensus, block or root change; both sides ship together (decision, Consequences). The SERVER handler is reachable by any authenticated 4001 session in a node built from this tree; the pinned-only rule is enforced on the CLIENT side, by `nodus_light_provider_init` and on every `light_block` call (`nodus.h:1656-1660`, `nodus_light_provider.h:16-21`). **No production caller in a node** of the client call or the provider (`nodus_light_provider.h:8-14`, which still reads "nothing in a node or a client calls it yet"); since nodus 0.26.11 the program `nodus-light` opens one pinned provider per node (`nodus/tools/nodus-light.c:636`). The RPC is on no live node yet. This is not a "proven RPC": the answer is a peer's word, checked only for wire shape here and verified by the light client (ValidateBasic + verification) (`nodus.h:1679-1680`; Kurultay #25 item 6).

**Wire** (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus.h:1656-1680`, the one place it is specified): request `"a"`: `{"h": u64}` — 0 = the node's latest stored block (cometbft `rpc/core/env.go:173-191`, the nil height). Response `"r"`: `{"h": u64 the resolved height (never 0), "lb": bstr}` — `"lb"` is the `tendermint.types.LightBlock` proto (`types.proto:161-164`), `cmt_light_block_encode` of {SignedHeader{BlockMeta.Header, commit}, LoadValidators(h)}; the commit is the SEEN commit at the node's latest height and the canonical one (from block h+1) below it (`rpc/core/blocks.go:166-174`); at most `NODUS_DNAC_LIGHT_BLOCK_MAX_BYTES`. Errors: `NODUS_ERR_HEIGHT_TOO_HIGH` (h above the latest stored block), `NODUS_ERR_NOT_FOUND` (pruned / not held — below the base, no meta, no commit, no validator set — or no version-3 chain served), `NODUS_ERR_INTERNAL_ERROR` (a read or encode fault), `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` (a malformed request). Front-door cost: `nodus_server.c:1944-1954` charges every routed chain method one unit except `dnac_spend` (charged by size); "routed" = the `dnac_` / `evm_` prefix test `nodus_chain_method_routed` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_ipc.h:81-87`), which `dnac_light_block` matches, so this request costs what one `dnac_v3_block` page costs — that front-door charge is UNCHANGED (`nodus_server.c` is not touched by the limiter fix). Decision B4 says "set only after a measurement". The measurement is recorded in the header (`nodus_witness_handlers.h:137-145`: -O2, one worst-case build — the validator-set replay from the farthest height — 15 ms at 7 validators, 57 ms at 32, 220 ms at 128), and what followed it is NOT a front-door weight but a NODE-WIDE limiter in front of the build: `NODUS_LIGHT_BLOCK_RATE_PER_SEC` = 4 builds per second, whoever asks, refused above it with `NODUS_ERR_RATE_LIMITED` (4) before any decode or store read (BUGS Z-44; the header names it "Operator decision 2026-10-19"; the decision file `2026-10-16-dnac-light-block-rpc.md` still reads B4 as a pending front-door weight and no decision file records the 4/s limiter). The reason (`nodus_witness_handlers.c:4753-4769`): one build can replay `cmt_validator_set_increment_proposer_priority` up to 99,999 times inside `nodus_cmt_ss_load_validators`, on the witness loop consensus runs on, and a per-session charge does not bound that loop because free identities open more sessions. At 4/s the worst case holds the witness loop ~60 ms of every second at 7 validators and ~230 ms at 32 (the header's figures). No queue, no cache. The BUILD reads no clock; the HANDLER reads the node's MONOTONIC wait clock (`n->mono`, decision `2026-09-30-monotonic-waits.md`) for admission only — node-local scheduling: nothing it reads or keeps reaches a block, the state, a vote or replication.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_ERR_HEIGHT_TOO_HIGH = 22` (`nodus_error_t`, `nodus/include/nodus/nodus_types.h:499`) | **NEW.** "Height above this node's latest stored block (dnac_light_block; light/provider/errors.go:9-11 ErrHeightTooHigh)" — the next free id after `NODUS_ERR_UNAVAILABLE = 21` (decision B3). |
| `#define NODUS_DNAC_LIGHT_BLOCK_MAX_BYTES (1024u * 1024u)` (`nodus.h`) | **NEW.** Ceiling of `"lb"`: 1 MiB. A full set (CMT_VALSET_MAX = 128 validators and as many signatures) is at most `cmt_pb_light_block_upper_bound(128, 128)` = 949 929 bytes (`nodus.h:1682-1691`); the node checks that bound against this constant before it answers and `test_light_block_rpc` pins it. A constant, not that call, so `nodus_client.c` does not link the cmt codec (the web wallet's WASM build compiles it without it). |
| `int nodus_dnac_light_block_request_encode(uint32_t txn, const uint8_t token[NODUS_SESSION_TOKEN_LEN], uint64_t height, uint8_t *buf, size_t cap, size_t *len_out)` (`nodus.h:1700`, `nodus_client.c:5339`) | **NEW.** Encodes the T2 query frame with the session token and `"a": {"h": height}` — the encoder `nodus_client_dnac_light_block` uses, exported for tests. 0 and `*len_out` set; -1 on NULL `token` / `buf` / `len_out`, `cap == 0`, or a buffer too small. |
| `int nodus_dnac_light_block_decode(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, uint64_t *height_out, const uint8_t **lb_out, size_t *lb_len_out)` (`nodus.h:1715`, `nodus_client.c:5360`) | **NEW.** Decodes a raw `dnac_light_block` response — exported for tests. STRICT: `"h"` (u64, non-zero) and `"lb"` (bstr, 1 .. `NODUS_DNAC_LIGHT_BLOCK_MAX_BYTES` bytes) both required; a duplicate key or a truncated message refused; unknown keys skipped. `*lb_out` points INTO `raw` (valid while `raw` is). 0; -1 malformed (outputs zeroed). |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_light_block(nodus_client_t *client, uint64_t height, uint64_t *height_out, uint8_t **lb_out, size_t *lb_len_out, int *server_err_out)` (`nodus.h:1740`, `nodus_client.c:5407`) | **NEW.** Fetches one LightBlock, bounded by the client's `request_timeout_ms`. `height` 0 = the node's latest; otherwise the reply must be exactly that height, anything else refused `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` (the reference provider's check, `light/provider/http/http.go:70-74`). `*lb_out` = a malloc'd copy of the `"lb"` bytes, freed with `free()`, NULL on every non-zero return. `*server_err_out` (may be NULL) = the node's `NODUS_ERR_*` from an ERROR reply, 0 when the node did not answer with one. Returns 0; the node's `NODUS_ERR_*` code (also in `*server_err_out`); `NODUS_ERR_TIMEOUT` when no reply came in time; `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` for a reply refused here; -1 on invalid arguments, a client not ready, or an encode / send / allocation failure. Verifies nothing beyond the wire shape. |
| `int nodus_witness_light_block_build(nodus_cmt_store_t *store, const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, uint32_t txn_id, uint8_t **out, size_t *out_len, int *err_code, char *err_msg, size_t err_cap)` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_handlers.h:129`, `nodus_witness_handlers.c:4801`) | **NEW (server, non-static; prototype in `nodus_witness_handlers.h` since the limiter fix — `test_light_block_rpc.c:130` includes the header; the former local extern is gone).** The answer without its admission and its send (`nodus_witness_light_block_serve` puts the limiter in front of it). Request: `"h"` exactly once, an unsigned integer (else PROTOCOL_ERROR). Refused INTERNAL_ERROR while a ledger transaction is open on the borrowed connection (`sqlite3_get_autocommit == 0`; only COMMITTED state is an answer). Height per `env.go:173-191`: h = 0 → store height (NOT_FOUND "no block stored" if none); h > store height → HEIGHT_TOO_HIGH; h < base → NOT_FOUND. Header from `nodus_cmt_bs_load_block_meta`; commit `nodus_cmt_bs_load_seen_commit` at the store height, `nodus_cmt_bs_load_block_commit` below it; set `nodus_cmt_ss_load_validators(h)` sent whole, not paged (CMT_REJECT = ErrNoValSetForHeight → NOT_FOUND). Absent meta / commit / set → NOT_FOUND; a read fault (CMT_FAULT), an encode failure, `cmt_pb_light_block_upper_bound(CMT_VALSET_MAX, CMT_VALSET_MAX) > NODUS_DNAC_LIGHT_BLOCK_MAX_BYTES`, or an `"lb"` above the ceiling → INTERNAL_ERROR, never a partial answer. PURE READ: no write, no clock, no cache; every large struct is heap. On 0 `*out` is a malloc'd frame (`{"t","y":"r","q":"dnac_light_block","r":{"h","lb"}}`) the caller frees; -1 with `*err_code` / `err_msg` set. |
| `#define NODUS_LIGHT_BLOCK_RATE_PER_SEC 4`, `#define NODUS_LIGHT_BLOCK_INTERVAL_NS (1000000000LL / (int64_t)NODUS_LIGHT_BLOCK_RATE_PER_SEC)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.h:146-148`) | **NEW (BUGS Z-44).** The node-wide `dnac_light_block` build rate per second; the bucket holds the same number (a burst of 4, then one build every 250 ms). A `_Static_assert` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c:4772-4774`) requires the rate > 0 and the interval a whole number of nanoseconds. Measurement and reason: the §26 intro. |
| `typedef struct { int64_t tat_ns; } nodus_light_block_limiter_t;` (`nodus_witness_handlers.h:157-159`) | **NEW.** The limiter, kept as its theoretical arrival time: `tat_ns` = the monotonic ns at which the bucket is full again. A zeroed struct is a full bucket. NODE-LOCAL admission: nothing in it reaches a block, the state, a vote or replication. |
| `bool nodus_witness_light_block_admit(nodus_light_block_limiter_t *lim, int64_t now_ns)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.h:168`, `.c:5016`) | **NEW.** Takes one token at `now_ns`, a reading of the node's MONOTONIC wait clock (never the wall clock). GCRA: `tat = max(tat_ns, now)`; admitted while `tat − now ≤ (RATE − 1) × INTERVAL` (at least one token left), then `tat_ns = tat + INTERVAL`. Integer nanoseconds only, no rounding; progress shorter than one interval is kept. true = admitted (one token taken); false = refused and the limiter UNCHANGED. NULL, a negative `now_ns`, or one above `INT64_MAX − (RATE + 1) × INTERVAL` is refused. |
| `int nodus_witness_light_block_serve(nodus_light_block_limiter_t *lim, int64_t now_ns, nodus_cmt_store_t *store, const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, uint32_t txn_id, uint8_t **out, size_t *out_len, int *err_code, char *err_msg, size_t err_cap)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.h:179`, `.c:5035`) | **NEW.** Admission, then the build — what the handler runs with the node's one limiter and its monotonic `now`; tests pass their own. `*out` / `*out_len` zeroed first; -1 on NULL `lim` / `out` / `out_len` / `err_code` / `err_msg` or `err_cap` 0. A refused admission returns -1 with `*err_code` = `NODUS_ERR_RATE_LIMITED` ("light block rate limit reached, ask another node") and reads NOTHING — no decode, no store access. Otherwise returns as `nodus_witness_light_block_build`. |
| `static nodus_light_block_limiter_t g_light_block_limiter;` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c:5063`) | **NEW (internal).** The node's ONE limiter, node-wide, whoever asks. Read and written only by `handle_dnac_light_block` on the witness loop (one thread, one witness per process in split and combined mode). Zero = a full bucket. |
| `static void handle_dnac_light_block(nodus_witness_t *w, struct nodus_tcp_conn *conn, const uint8_t *payload, size_t len, uint32_t txn_id)` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c:5069`) | **NEW (internal).** Dispatched for method `"dnac_light_block"`. A node with no version-3 chain (`!w->v2_successor`, no cmt node, or store not ready) answers NOT_FOUND "this node serves no version-3 chain". Then reads the monotonic wait clock (`n->mono`); no clock, or a clock fault, answers INTERNAL_ERROR "internal error" (WARN logged) without touching the limiter or the store. Otherwise calls `nodus_witness_light_block_serve(&g_light_block_limiter, now_ns, &n->store, …)` and sends its frame, or `send_error` with its code — RATE_LIMITED when the limiter refused, INTERNAL_ERROR "internal error" when no code was set. |
| `static int lbk_fail(int *err_code, char *err_msg, size_t err_cap, uint64_t h, int code, const char *msg)`, `#define NODUS_LIGHT_BLOCK_ENVELOPE_MAX 256u` (`nodus_witness_handlers.c`) | **NEW (internal).** Records a refusal and returns -1, logging a node-side fault (INTERNAL_ERROR) at WARN and a client-caused refusal at DEBUG. The envelope headroom; a `_Static_assert` requires `NODUS_DNAC_LIGHT_BLOCK_MAX_BYTES + 256 < NODUS_MAX_FRAME_TCP`. |
| `typedef struct { nodus_client_t *client; uint8_t chain_id[CMT_MAX_CHAIN_ID_LEN]; size_t chain_id_len; uint64_t block_protocol; uint64_t evidence_recorded; } nodus_light_provider_t;` (`nodus/src/client/nodus_light_provider.h`) | **NEW.** One pinned provider. `client` BORROWED and pinned; must outlive the `cmt_light_provider_t` table that points at it. `evidence_recorded` = `report_evidence`'s local record. |
| `bool nodus_light_provider_config_pinned(const nodus_client_config_t *cfg)` | **NEW.** True when `cfg->pinned_server_fps != NULL` and `cfg->pinned_server_fp_count > 0` (the shape `nodus_client_init` accepts as pinned). |
| `int nodus_light_provider_init(nodus_light_provider_t *p, nodus_client_t *client, const uint8_t *chain_id, size_t chain_id_len, uint64_t block_protocol, cmt_light_provider_t *table)` | **NEW.** Binds `p` to `client` and fills `table` (ctx = p, the three rows). CMT_OK; CMT_REJECT when the client is NOT pinned (Kurultay #25 item 8: "never an unpinned provider") or `chain_id_len` is 0 or above `CMT_MAX_CHAIN_ID_LEN` — `p` and `table` are then zeroed; CMT_FAULT on NULL `p` / `client` / `table`, or NULL `chain_id` with non-zero length. A pinned session authenticates the server with ML-DSA-87 and runs ML-KEM-1024 only (`nodus_light_provider.h:19-21`). |
| `int nodus_light_provider_map_error(int rc, int server_err, cmt_light_provider_error_t *err)` | **NEW.** The pure error mapping `light_block` applies to `nodus_client_dnac_light_block`'s result, exported for tests: rc 0 → CMT_OK (NONE). Otherwise CMT_REJECT with node NOT_FOUND → LIGHT_BLOCK_NOT_FOUND; node HEIGHT_TOO_HIGH → HEIGHT_TOO_HIGH; node `NODUS_ERR_RATE_LIMITED` → NO_RESPONSE (DEVIATION, BUGS Z-45, `nodus_light_provider.h:53-63`: the reference RPC has no "busy" refusal and its only transient one, a timed-out request retried with backoff, ends in ErrNoResponse — http.go:200-210, errors.go:16-18; an unnamed error is outside the light client's benign set, light/client.go:757-763, :1106-1112, and would remove an honest node that is merely at its node-wide limit; no retry here, the client asks another provider); any other node error → NONE (unnamed — INTERNAL_ERROR and a node-sent PROTOCOL_ERROR included, the reference's default branch http.go:205-207); no node error and rc `NODUS_ERR_TIMEOUT` → NO_RESPONSE; no node error and rc -1 (not connected, or a local allocation / send failure) → NO_RESPONSE (DEVIATION, BUGS Z-45, `nodus_light_provider.h:64-69`: the reference's default branch returns the raw error for a refused connection, outside the benign set; here a provider whose session is down is kept); rc `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` (reply refused, wrong height) → BAD_LIGHT_BLOCK, reason CMT_REJECT; anything else → NONE. `err` may be NULL. |
| `static int lp_chain_id(void *ctx, const uint8_t **out, size_t *out_len)`, `static int lp_light_block(void *ctx, int64_t height, cmt_light_block_buf_t *out, cmt_light_provider_error_t *err)`, `static int lp_report_evidence(void *ctx, const cmt_pb_evidence_t *ev)` (`nodus_light_provider.c`) | **NEW (internal).** The three rows. `chain_id` = the CONFIGURED id, never read from the server (http.go:59-62). `light_block`: negative height, NULL, or a client whose pin was removed after init → CMT_FAULT (the pin is re-checked on every call); then `nodus_client_dnac_light_block` + `cmt_light_block_decode` with the configured `block_protocol`; an `"lb"` that does not decode → BAD_LIGHT_BLOCK reason CMT_REJECT. DEVIATION from the reference HTTP provider: it does NOT run `LightBlock.ValidateBasic` itself (http.go:91-95) — the light client does (D15); the height match IS checked, by the client call. `report_evidence`: LOCAL RECORD ONLY — `evidence_recorded++` and a WARN log, nothing sent; NULL → CMT_FAULT. |
| `int nodus_client_dnac_v3_block(nodus_client_t *client, uint64_t height, uint32_t from_index, uint32_t budget, nodus_dnac_v3_block_result_t *result_out)`, `int nodus_dnac_v3_block_decode(const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len, nodus_dnac_v3_block_result_t *result_out)`, `void nodus_client_free_v3_block_result(nodus_dnac_v3_block_result_t *result)` (`nodus.h:1633`, `:1649`, `:1654`) | **EXISTING, previously unlisted here** (drift repair; the `dnac_v3_block` SDK call `dnac_light_block` sits beside). One page of a committed version-3 block (`budget` 0 = the node's maximum); 0 and `result_out` filled (free with `nodus_client_free_v3_block_result`), the node's `NODUS_ERR_*`, `NODUS_ERR_PROTOCOL_ERROR` for a reply the decoder refuses, -1 on invalid arguments / encode / transport failure. The decoder is STRICT and leaves `result_out` empty on any refusal; free is NULL-safe and zeroes the struct. |
| Tests | `nodus/tests/test_light_block_rpc.c` (ctest `test_light_block_rpc`; `NODUS_WITNESS_INTERNAL_API` set by its CMake target; default build otherwise, no environment, no network, no port; SQLite ≥ 3.35.0). Proves, over a REAL derived version-3 chain: empty store → NOT_FOUND; h = 0 and h = tip answer the same bytes and two builds identical frames; a mid height carries the canonical commit and the tip the seen commit; every `"lb"` decodes and passes ValidateBasic; above the tip → HEIGHT_TOO_HIGH (22); a deleted commit row and heights below the pruned base → NOT_FOUND; malformed requests → PROTOCOL_ERROR; an open transaction → INTERNAL_ERROR; the ceiling holds the codec bound; ADMISSION through `nodus_witness_light_block_serve` — with an open transaction (the build's first store touch answers INTERNAL_ERROR) four requests at one `now` reach the build and the fifth answers RATE_LIMITED with no frame, a NULL store behind an empty bucket answers RATE_LIMITED too, after ROLLBACK and one interval a request is served whole (t_server); `nodus_witness_light_block_admit` alone — a burst of exactly 4, the 5th refused with the bucket unchanged, one more after one interval, 4 (not more) after 10 s, 200 ms steps still refill, NULL / negative / unrepresentable `now` refused (t_limiter); the client decoder's refusals (t_decoder); the provider's pin refusal (t_provider_pin) and error mapping including RATE_LIMITED → NO_RESPONSE (t_provider_errors). Five cases. Leaves one `/tmp/test_lbrpc_*` directory per fixture, removed at close (a CHECK abort leaves it). Can lie (file header 1-6): the handler's own layer — the cmt-node lookup, the no-chain refusal, the `n->mono` read, the node's file-static limiter, the send — and the front-door charge are not driven (the serve seam is called with the test's own limiter and `now`); the client call's network half is not driven (no socket); the tip's seen commit is written by the test; an absent validator set at a held height is not constructed; "no store read" on a refusal is shown by OUTCOME, not by a counter of store calls; written, not run, by its builder. |

## 27. HF-7 P1b — schema S19: `v2_replay` + `v2_domain_heads.last_upd_hash` (INERT; design `docs/plans/2026-09-11-nodus-ledger-snapshot-design.md` §10.2 item 4, §10.9, §10.11 khan decision D; Kurultay #27 summary `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-20-kurultay-27-hf7-p1-summary.md` items 3, 6; branch `hf7-p1b`, not versioned)

Nothing calls the new objects yet (writers / readers: HF-7 P1c). (Superseded for `v2_replay` on branch `hf7-p1c2` by §28 below — written, pruned and read as the replay guard at rule-set generation 5+.) Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-7 P1b — schema S19". Tests: `nodus/tests/test_v2_schema.c` (`run_s19_rung`), `test_v2_evm.c` (at-open climb to S19), `test_v2_gen.c` (derived chain at S19).

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `NODUS_V2_SCHEMA_VERSION_S19` (= 19u), `nodus_v2s19_mig_fail_t`, `int nodus_witness_db_migrate_v2s19(nodus_witness_t *w)`, `int nodus_witness_db_migrate_v2s19_ex(nodus_witness_t *w, nodus_v2s19_mig_fail_t fail_at)` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_v2_schema.h`) | **NEW.** Atomic 18 → 19 rung (versions below 18 cascade through S18 first): `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS v2_replay (tx_id BLOB NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY CHECK(typeof(tx_id)='blob' AND length(tx_id)=64), intent_id BLOB NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK(typeof(intent_id)='blob' AND length(intent_id)=64), expiry_height INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(typeof(expiry_height)='integer' AND expiry_height >= 1))`, `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS v2_replay_by_expiry ON v2_replay(expiry_height)`, `ALTER TABLE v2_domain_heads ADD COLUMN last_upd_hash BLOB` when the column is absent; verified: the table's stored `sqlite_master.sql` equals this build's for the same statement, the index covers exactly `expiry_height`, the table is empty, `v2_domain_heads` = the S5 columns + `last_upd_hash` (BLOB, nullable, no default, not a key); `user_version = 19`. 0 migrated / already 19; -1 failure (full rollback) incl. version 20+. Five fault stages (AFTER_BEGIN, AFTER_REVALIDATE, AFTER_TABLES, AFTER_VERIFY, BEFORE_COMMIT). |
| `static int table_ddl_exact(nodus_witness_t *w, const char *table, const char *ddl)`, `static int s19_heads_shape_ok(nodus_witness_t *w)` (`nodus_witness_v2_schema.c`) | **NEW (internal).** `table_ddl_exact`: 1 = the table's stored `sqlite_master.sql` equals what this build's SQLite stores for `ddl` (executed into a private `:memory:` database — the `nodus_witness.c` `witness_ddl_shape_check` pattern), 0 = absent / another definition, -1 = fault. `s19_heads_shape_ok`: 1 = `v2_domain_heads` is exactly `(domain_id, head, domain_height, last_updated_global, last_upd_hash)` with `last_upd_hash` BLOB, nullable, no default, pk 0; 0 = another shape; -1 = fault. |
| `int nodus_witness_db_ensure_v2s17_indexes(nodus_witness_t *w)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** runs at S17, S18 **or S19** — the at-open rung now reaches S19 before this step. |
| `static int witness_open_migrate_s19(nodus_witness_t *witness, const char *db_path)` (`nodus_witness.c`) | **RENAMED + CHANGED** from `witness_open_migrate_s18`: on an established version-3 chain at S16, S17 **or S18**, calls `nodus_witness_db_migrate_v2s19`. |
| `static int phase_6b_gen4_edge(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **CHANGED (gate only):** schema S18 **or S19** (was exactly S18), else FAULT. |
| EVM edge (phase 6b″), `v2_apply_block_body` (the Comet lane), `nodus_witness_v2_genesis_cmt` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`), `nodus_witness_v2_preflight` (`nodus_witness_v2_preflight.c`), `nodus_witness_v2_pools_startup_check` / `nodus_rt_core_state_init` (`nodus_witness_v2_pools.c`) | **CHANGED (gate only):** each accepts 19 wherever it accepted 18. No other behaviour moves. |
| `nodus_witness_v2_gen_derive_v3` (`nodus_witness_v2_gen.c`), the join (`nodus_witness_v2_join.c`), `nodus_witness_v2_bundle_apply` (`nodus_witness_v2_bundle.c`) | **CHANGED:** migrate to S19 (was S18). `gen_seed_state`'s `must_be_empty` list gains `v2_replay`. |

## 28. HF-7 P1c-2 — the replay relation and the D2 expiry rule (design `docs/plans/2026-09-11-nodus-ledger-snapshot-design.md` §10.2 items 5-7, §10.3, §10.9, §10.11; Kurultay #27 summary `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-20-kurultay-27-hf7-p1-summary.md` items 3, 4, 5; decision `docs/plans/decisions/2026-09-25-mempool-policy.md` 1; branch `hf7-p1c2`, not versioned)

Every new behaviour is gated `generation >= NODUS_RT_GEN_ROOTS` (5), judged by the BLOCK-START generation; below it every verdict and table is unchanged. INERT on every chain without a committed param-22 row (HF-7 P1d, §29, compiled the generation-5 entry and its edge; `NODUS_RT_GEN_MAX` = 5 there). Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-7 P1c-2". Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf7_replay.c`.

| Item | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `nodus_witness_v2_block_ctx_t.generation` (`nodus/src/witness/nodus_witness_v2_env.h`) | **NEW field.** `uint32_t`: the rule-set generation of the resolved SYSTEM runtime, set once by `block_ctx_from_doms` from the same `sys->rt` that supplies the metering policy (the registry tuple `nodus_witness_v2_key_generation` resolves). Built before the block mutates anything, so block A−1 reads 4 and block A reads 5. Consumers: the item loop, phase 12d, phase 0d, the dry run (`s->bctx`), the seam (`bctx`). |
| `static int block_ctx_from_doms(...)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **CHANGED (internal, behaviour):** also sets `ctx->generation = sys->rt->generation`. Signature unchanged. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_replay_guard(nodus_witness_t *w, const dna_env_preflight_t *p, uint32_t generation, size_t item, int *hit, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **CHANGED signature** (+ `generation`). Below 5: `v2_intent_index.intent_id`, then `v2_tx_index.tx_id` (unchanged statements). At 5+: `v2_replay.intent_id`, then `v2_replay.tx_id` — an intent OR a wire hit is a replay; the index tables are never read as a guard. Same binding (`sqlite3_bind_blob`, 64 bytes), same fault texts; 0 with `*hit` / -2 node fault. Callers: the item loop (`bctx.generation`), the CheckTx dry run (`s.bctx->generation`), the seam (`bctx->generation`, HF-3 block), `verify_v2_successor_tx` (from 5 only). |
| `static int env_replay_guard(nodus_witness_t *w, const dna_env_preflight_t *p, uint32_t generation, size_t item, int *hit, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **CHANGED (internal signature)** (+ `generation`), still a one-line wrapper. |
| `NODUS_V2_EXPIRY_WINDOW_BLOCKS` (`((uint64_t)720)`, `nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **NEW.** The D2 window (§10.11 C). Equal in value to `DNA_VALBIND_MAX_EXPIRY_BLOCKS` (the validator-binding consent window, `shared/dnac/valbind_wire.h`) but a separate constant on purpose — a change there must not move this consensus rule. Bound by the HF-7 vote literal (P1d); not the node-local mempool window `NODUS_CMT_APP_MAX_EXPIRY_AHEAD` (100). |
| `static inline int nodus_witness_v2_expiry_in_window(uint64_t e, uint64_t h)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **NEW.** THE ONE D2 predicate: `e != 0 && e >= h && e - h <= NODUS_V2_EXPIRY_WINDOW_BLOCKS` → 1, else 0. Overflow-safe (the subtraction runs only once `e >= h`; no `h + 720` formed). Pure; the caller gates on the generation. Callers: `produce_batch_check_impl` (seam), `v2_apply_block_body` phase 0d. |
| `int nodus_witness_v2_replay_prune(nodus_witness_t *w, uint32_t generation, uint64_t height, uint64_t *n_deleted, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`) | **NEW.** Phase 12d: `DELETE FROM v2_replay WHERE expiry_height <= ?1` with `height` bound by `sqlite3_bind_int64`. Below 5: returns 0, touches nothing (`*n_deleted` 0). `height > INT64_MAX`, a NULL handle, a prepare or step failure → -2 (reason written). `n_deleted` optional (`sqlite3_changes`). Runs inside the caller's transaction. Production caller: `v2_apply_block_body`, after phase 12c and before phase 13 (so the replay root is composed over the pruned relation). |
| `static int cmt_item_index(nodus_witness_t *w, uint64_t global_height, const dna_env_preflight_t *p, dom_ctx_t *doms, size_t n_dom, uint32_t gidx, uint32_t generation, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **CHANGED (internal signature)** (+ `generation`). The two index rows as before; at 5+ a third INSERT `v2_replay (tx_id = p->wire_id, intent_id = p->intent_id, expiry_height)` — ids `sqlite3_bind_blob`, expiry `sqlite3_bind_int64` — inside the applied item's savepoint, so a refused item leaves none. An expiry of 0 or above INT64_MAX (unreachable: phase 0d) and any SQL failure (incl. a UNIQUE / CHECK refusal) → -1, which the item loop turns into a node FAULT. CLAIM items never reach it (they keep their nullifier). |
| `v2_apply_block_body` phase 0d (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **NEW stage.** At `bctx.generation >= 5`, after phase 0c and before the item loop: every envelope that decodes must satisfy `nodus_witness_v2_expiry_in_window(e, blk->global_height)`, else a node FAULT (`"phase 0d: envelope … outside the D2 window …"`, exit `fail_fault_pre`) — never a failed item. An envelope that does not decode is skipped (the item loop's DECODE). |
| `v2_apply_block_body` phase 12d | **NEW stage.** `nodus_witness_v2_replay_prune(w, bctx.generation, blk->global_height, …)`; -2 → `fail_fault`. |
| `produce_batch_check_impl` (`nodus_witness_v2_produce.c`; reached through `nodus_witness_v2_produce_batch_check_capped`) | **CHANGED (behaviour), signature unchanged:** the per-envelope loop after a reservation-clean run now runs when `bctx->hf3_active` OR `bctx->generation >= 5`. At 5+, FIRST per envelope, a D2 violation at `candidate` → -1, kind `NODUS_V2_BATCH_FAIL_ENTRY_INVALID`, `*fail_index_out` = its index (PrepareProposal's drop loop excludes it; ProcessProposal REJECTs — the same path that already refused `e < H` through the preflight's ERR_EXPIRED). The HF-3 rules (price read, replay guard, fee judge, rule 5) still run only when `hf3_active`; the replay guard is asked at `bctx->generation`. |
| `verify_v2_successor_tx` (`nodus_witness_verify.c`, static) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** reads `nodus_witness_v2_key_generation` (a read fault → reject "rule-set generation unreadable on this node"); at 5+ calls `nodus_witness_v2_replay_guard(w, pf, gen, …)` — hit → reject "intent or wire id already committed on this chain", fault → reject "replay-relation lookup failed"; below 5 the intent-index statement runs unchanged. Now includes `nodus_witness_v2_apply.h`, `nodus_witness_v2_epoch.h`, `nodus_witness_runtime.h`. |
| `app_check_envelope` (`nodus_witness_cmt_app.c`, static) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** reads `nodus_witness_v2_key_generation` (a read fault → `CMT_FAULT`); when the generation judging tip + 1 is 5+, also refuses `e != 0 && e < tip + 1` (log `"expiry_height … is below the next block …"`). Node-local mempool policy, not consensus; the upper bound `tip + NODUS_CMT_APP_MAX_EXPIRY_AHEAD` and `e == 0` refusal unchanged. NOTE: on the public CheckTx path the admission lane's preflight at tip + 1 refuses `e < tip + 1` first, so this branch is a belt. |
| Tests | `nodus/tests/test_hf7_replay.c` (ctest `test_hf7_replay`; `NODUS_WITNESS_INTERNAL_API` by its CMake target; default build, no environment). Seven cases: the predicate's endpoints (0, H−1, H, H+720, H+721, UINT64_MAX, around 2^63); rows at generation 5 (a row per applied item with its fields, a failed item none, e == H pruned in its own block, the index tables still written, A and A' refused by the item loop / dry run / seam / admission lane, the in-block duplicate, the exact guard swap by direct calls, the engine prune at H = 6); the direct prune (exactly `expiry <= H`, idempotent, no-op below 5); the D2 endpoints at the seam / ProcessProposal / PrepareProposal / FinalizeBlock (FAULT, database byte-identical); the seam with HF-3 off; CheckTx's window; generation 4 unchanged. Generation 5 is reached by a scripted table whose `generation` is patched before a seeded genesis (the database climbed to S19 first; the SYSTEM / CORE `state_root` hooks pinned to the generation-1 compositions, since at ≥ 4 the native ones need HF-6-edge rows) — no edge; the A−1 cell is not covered (file header "HOW IT CAN LIE"). Written, NOT run by the builder. |

## 29. HF-7 P1d — the activation: param 22 `RULESET_GEN_ROOTS`, the vote literal D5, generation 5 compiled, the edge at the end of block A−1 (design `docs/plans/2026-09-11-nodus-ledger-snapshot-design.md` §10.2 items 1-3, §10.3, §10.9, §10.11 khan decisions B / C; Kurultay #27 summary `docs/plans/decisions/2026-10-20-kurultay-27-hf7-p1-summary.md` items 1, 2, 7, 10; branch `hf7-p1d`, not versioned)

The HF-6 param-18 / D4 mechanism, mirrored site by site. Generation 5 becomes REACHABLE: a committed param-22 row = D5 switches the registry 4 → 5 at the end of block A−1, and every `>= NODUS_RT_GEN_ROOTS` gate of P1c then acts from that block's roots (A−1) and items (A) on. No param-22 row on any chain today, so every chain is byte-unchanged. Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "HF-7 P1d". Tests: `nodus/tests/test_hf7_activation.c`, `nodus/tests/test_hf7_edge.c` (both `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED`; written, NOT run by the builder).

| Symbol | Change |
|---|---|
| `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROOTS` (22) (`dnac/include/dnac/dnac.h`) | **NEW.** The generation-5 vote; value exactly `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROOTS_D`; on `dnac_cfg_param_read_by_consensus`. `DNAC_CFG_PARAM_MAX_ID` 21 → 22 (so the witness's `chain_config_cache` arrays, sized `DNAC_CFG_PARAM_MAX_ID + 1`, grow by one slot). |
| `DNAC_HF7_SWITCH_SPEC_VERSION` (`1u`) (`dnac.h`) | **NEW.** The 4 → 5 procedure version hashed into D5 (the oracle's spec_version field). |
| `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROOTS_D` (= `0x6f4c60640dd57094ULL` = 8019891019285098644, owner K8) (`dnac.h`) | **NEW.** D5 = `dna_rolegen_vote_digest(5, 4, SYSTEM v11 hash, CORE v10 hash, 1, DNAC_HF6_FOUNDATION_OWNER, {720}, 1)`, 232-byte preimage — the INDEPENDENT oracle's literal (`shared/dnac/tests/hf7_vote_oracle.py`, `hf7_vote_oracle_out.txt`). `#ifndef`-guarded: a TEST build overriding the owner must define it too (`stagef_hf6_owner.sh` prints it). `_Static_assert`s: ≤ INT64_MAX; ≠ D2, ≠ EVM_ACTIVE D, ≠ D4; id 22 == `DNAC_CFG_PARAM_MAX_ID`. |
| `dnac_tx_verify_chain_config_rules` / `verify_chain_config_rules` (`dnac/src/transaction/verify.c`) | **CHANGED (behaviour):** id 22 accepted iff `new_value == DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROOTS_D` (the param-18 shape); the stateful rules are not mirrored. |
| `NODUS_RT_GEN_MAX` (`nodus_witness_runtime.h`) | **CHANGED:** `NODUS_RT_GEN_ROOTS` (5) under `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED` (was `NODUS_RT_GEN_STORAGE`); `NODUS_RT_GEN_2` otherwise, unchanged. |
| `NODUS_RT_GEN5_VOTE_EXPIRY_WINDOW` (`((uint64_t)720)`, `nodus_witness_runtime.h`) | **NEW.** D5's one constant — a restatement for the runtime module (free of the engine's db headers); pinned `== NODUS_V2_EXPIRY_WINDOW_BLOCKS` by a `_Static_assert` in `nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`. |
| compiled table (`nodus_witness_runtime.c`, `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED`) | **CHANGED:** generation 5 = slots 9-10 (`GEN_SLICES` `{9, 2}`; `BUILTIN_COUNT` 9 → 11): SYSTEM v11 (`SYS_RULES_GST`, `SYS_TYPES`, its own sealed policy `g_sys_policy_grt`, ops 1..9, digest `SYS_METER_POLICY_DIGEST_GRT` = oracle C1), CORE v10 (`CORE_RULES_GEVM`, `CORE_TYPES`); hooks / adapters / allowlists = generation 4's; no EVM slot. Pins `SYS_RULESET_HASH_GRT` (bbd0a18d…671ae9) / `CORE_RULESET_HASH_GRT` (ff06f682…bbb870) = oracle R1 / R2. |
| `int nodus_runtime_gen5_vote_digest(uint64_t *d_out)` (`nodus_witness_runtime.h`, `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED`) | **NEW.** The `nodus_runtime_gen4_vote_digest` shape over generation 5, `DNAC_HF7_SWITCH_SPEC_VERSION`, the compiled owner and `GEN5_VOTE_CONSTS` = { `NODUS_RT_GEN5_VOTE_EXPIRY_WINDOW` }. 0 / -1. Only caller: the selfcheck. |
| `int nodus_witness_runtime_selfcheck(void)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** EVM-enabled builds also (a) re-derive D5 and refuse ("the compiled RULESET_GEN_ROOTS literal … does not re-derive") when it differs from the literal; (b) refuse unless every domain of the table either has a generation-5 entry whose version exceeds its generation-4 one, or is the EVM domain with no generation-5 entry while CORE's version moved (the EVM pair rule `nodus_rt_evm_pair_check` ties every EVM envelope to a CORE EVMFUND leg) — Kurultay #27 item 1. |
| `nodus_cc_state_facts_t.ruleset_gen_roots_voted` (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus_chain_config.h`) | **NEW member.** "any committed param-22 row". Filled by the 0x71 approval responder (`cc_appr_rules_chain_config`) and, since HF-7 P1d-FIX, by the SYSTEM CHAIN_CONFIG exec from `ctx.ruleset_gen_roots_voted` (the rows below). (P1d alone left the exec's fill unwired, so on the block path rule (a) read 0.) |
| `nodus_rt_exec_ctx_t.ruleset_gen_roots_voted` (`nodus_witness_runtime.h`) | **NEW field (HF-7 P1d-FIX).** `uint8_t`, "any committed param-22 row, any effective height" — the `ruleset_gen_roles_voted` contract; filled by the engine at both ctx sites (`exec_one_env`, `env_authorize_legs`); UNMETERED (no mediated read, the CHAIN_CONFIG read plan stays empty, no `gas_used` moves). One consumer: the SYSTEM CHAIN_CONFIG exec's param-22 rule (a). |
| `static int env_ruleset_gen_roots_voted(nodus_witness_t *w, uint8_t *voted, char *reason, size_t reason_size)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **NEW (internal, HF-7 P1d-FIX).** `nodus_chain_config_get_u64(w, 22, INT64_MAX, 0, &v)`: row → 1, absent → 0, unreadable → −2 (node FAULT, "HF-7: the RULESET_GEN_ROOTS history is unreadable …"). Read once per item in `exec_one_env` and `env_authorize_legs`, so a row an earlier item of the same block wrote counts. |
| `int nodus_rt_system_exec(...)` CHAIN_CONFIG path (`nodus_witness_rt_native.c`) | **CHANGED (internal behaviour, HF-7 P1d-FIX):** also copies `ctx->ruleset_gen_roots_voted` into the facts — the param-22 single-use rule (a) now binds on the block path and in CheckTx's dry run, not only at the 0x71 responder. |
| startup banner (`nodus_witness.c`) | **CHANGED (HF-7 P1d-FIX):** a new INFO line after the HF-6 D4 line, `HF-7 rule-set generation 5 vote D5 0x<D5> (param 22 RULESET_GEN_ROOTS, switch spec v1), built from git commit <c>` (`NODUS_RT_GEN_ROOTS`, `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROOTS_D`, `DNAC_CFG_RULESET_GEN_ROOTS`, `DNAC_HF7_SWITCH_SPEC_VERSION`) — the 7/7 pre-vote comparison line (`DEPLOY_RUNBOOK.md` §2.2). Printed in every build; only an EVM-enabled build's selfcheck re-derives D5. The HF-6 line's text is unchanged (the harness greps it). |
| `int nodus_chain_config_scalar_rules(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** id 22 legal iff exactly D5. |
| `uint64_t nodus_chain_config_grace_for_param(uint8_t)` | **CHANGED:** id 22 → `DNAC_CHAIN_CONFIG_GRACE_ERGONOMIC_BLOCKS` (khan decision B), its own case. |
| `int nodus_chain_config_stateful_rules_ex(...)` | **CHANGED (behaviour):** id 22 refused unless (a) no param-22 row, (b) HF-2 active, (c) `judging_generation == NODUS_RT_GEN_ROOTS_BASE` (4), (d) A ≠ 0 and A−1 not an epoch boundary, (e) A not an epoch boundary. `nodus_chain_config_stateful_rules` (test form) sets the param-22 fact from its 4th argument too. |
| `static int cc_appr_rules_chain_config(...)` (`nodus_witness_chain_config.c`) | **CHANGED:** + "any param-22 row" read (INT64_MAX current block, a read fault → -2) into `facts.ruleset_gen_roots_voted`. |
| `CC_PARAM_MAX_ID` / `CC_PARAM_RULESET_GEN_ROOTS` (`nodus_witness_chain_config.c`) | **CHANGED / NEW:** 21 → 22; 22 pinned to the dnac id. |
| `static int phase_6b_gen5_edge(nodus_witness_t *w, nodus_v2_block_t *blk, dom_ctx_t *doms, size_t n_dom)` (`nodus_witness_v2_apply.c`) | **NEW** engine phase 6b'''' (after 6b''' `phase_6b_gen4_edge`, before 6b'' and 6c). Trigger: param 22 present at h+1, absent at h. Then: value == D5 (else FAULT); `NODUS_EVM_ENABLED` (else FAULT); schema EXACTLY S19 (else FAULT); `nodus_witness_domreg_generation_switch(4, 5)` ("registry is not generation 4" FAULT); `nodus_witness_v2_domain_heads_seed_last_upd` (its first production caller; a failure FAULTs); SYSTEM + CORE touched; INFO `HF-7: rule-set generation 4 -> 5 and the head last_upd_hash seed at the end of height <h> (param 22 RULESET_GEN_ROOTS, D5 0x<D5>); height <h+1> is judged under generation 5`. 0 / -2. |
| `static int cc_param_name_to_id(...)` / `cmd_chain_config_propose` usage (`nodus/tools/nodus-cli.c`) | **CHANGED:** `RULESET_GEN_ROOTS` / `ruleset_gen_roots` → 22; usage line `RULESET_GEN_ROOTS      exactly <D5>`; the accepted-name lists name it. |
| `nodus/include/nodus/nodus_ruleset_pins.h` (generated) | **NOT regenerated** by P1d (running `regen_ruleset_pins` runs a built binary — forbidden to the builder): still `NODUS_PIN_GEN_COUNT 4`. `test_ruleset_pins` and `test_hf4_names` §7 (pins == `NODUS_RT_GEN_MAX`) stay red until the ORCHESTRATOR regenerates it and adds the `PG5_*` row to `nodus/src/client/nodus_v2_spend.c` `PINS_GEN` in the same step. |

## 30. "Update required" — the node operator's line, package U4 (design `docs/plans/2026-10-20-update-required-warning-design.md` §1 C; branch `upd-u4`, not versioned)

A chain_config param id beyond this build's id space is a newer release's vote. The scalar rules refuse it exactly as before; the node now says so in plain words, once per id per process, and checks its own `chain_config_history` at start. Node-local only: no verdict, root, vote or block content changes. Architecture: `nodus/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` "Update required — the node operator's line"; runbook: `nodus/docs/DEPLOY_RUNBOOK.md` §2.2, "A node that missed the vote". Test: `nodus/tests/test_cc_unknown_param.c` (written, NOT run by the builder).

| Symbol | Change |
|---|---|
| `bool nodus_chain_config_param_unknown(int64_t param_id)` (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus_chain_config.h`) | **NEW.** Pure. False for `1..CC_PARAM_MAX_ID` (every id there is read by the running consensus or one this build names and refuses: 1, 2, 3, 16), for the genesis economic band `NODUS_CC_ECON_PARAM_MIN..MAX` (200-202) and for 0; true otherwise (including a negative or > 255 id from a corrupt row). |
| `int nodus_chain_config_scan_unknown_params(nodus_witness_t *w)` (`nodus/include/nodus/nodus_chain_config.h`) | **NEW.** Read-only: `sqlite_master` check for `chain_config_history` (absent → 0), then `SELECT DISTINCT param_id FROM chain_config_history ORDER BY param_id ASC`; each unknown id gets the UPDATE REQUIRED line (once per id per process). Returns the count of distinct unknown ids, 0, or -1 (NULL `w`, no open DB, SQLite failure — logged WARN). One production caller: `nodus_witness_init`. |
| `static void cc_warn_unknown_param(int64_t param_id)` (`nodus_witness_chain_config.c`) | **NEW (internal).** QGP_LOG_ERROR, tag `CHAIN_CONFIG`: `UPDATE REQUIRED: a chain_config proposal names param <id>, which this build (nodus <NODUS_VERSION_STRING>) does not know — the network has a newer release; install it (install-node.sh update). Until then this node cannot follow the chain past this block.` Once per id 0..255 per process (`static atomic_uchar cc_unknown_param_warned[256]`, `atomic_exchange`); no clock. |
| `int nodus_chain_config_scalar_rules(...)` | **CHANGED (logging only), signature and every return value unchanged:** the `param_id < 1 \|\| param_id > CC_PARAM_MAX_ID` refusal calls `cc_warn_unknown_param` when `nodus_chain_config_param_unknown(param_id)`, then returns -1 as before. Reached by the 0x71 approval responder (`cc_appr_rules_chain_config`), the CheckTx dry run and the block apply (`nodus_rt_system_exec` CHAIN_CONFIG). |
| `int nodus_witness_init(...)` (`nodus_witness.c`) | **CHANGED (logging only):** after `nodus_witness_scan_chain_db` succeeds and `witness->db` is open, calls `nodus_chain_config_scan_unknown_params`; the result is ignored (never refuses or changes the start). |
